==> CMakeLists.txt <==
    1| cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.28)
    2| set(CMAKE_CXX_SCAN_FOR_MODULES OFF)
    3| project(yosys VERSION 0.66 LANGUAGES C CXX)
    4| set(YOSYS_VERSION "0.66")
    5| set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20 CACHE STRING "C++ language standard")
    6| set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
    7| set(CMAKE_CXX_SCAN_FOR_MODULES OFF)
    8| set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)
    9| option(BUILD_SHARED_LIBS "Build the shared Yosys library" ON)
   10| option(YOSYS_INSTALL_LIBRARY "Install libyosys" OFF)
   11| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_HELP_SOURCE "Include source locations in help" OFF)
   12| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_PYOSYS "Build Python bindings" OFF)
   13| option(YOSYS_DISABLE_THREADS "Disable threads" OFF)
   14| set(YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX "" CACHE STRING "Program name prefix")
   15| set(YOSYS_ABC_EXTERNAL "" CACHE STRING "Use an external ABC command")
   16| set(SANITIZE "" CACHE STRING "Clang sanitizers")
   17| foreach(feature TCL READLINE ZLIB PLUGINS ABC)
   18|   option(YOSYS_WITHOUT_${feature} "Disable ${feature}" OFF)
   19| endforeach()
   20| set(YOSYS_WASI OFF)
   21| if(CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME STREQUAL "WASI")
   22|   set(YOSYS_WASI ON)
   23|   set(YOSYS_DISABLE_THREADS ON)
   24| endif()
   25| find_package(Python3 REQUIRED COMPONENTS Interpreter)
   26| find_package(BISON REQUIRED)
   27| find_package(FLEX REQUIRED)
   28| if(NOT YOSYS_WITHOUT_TCL OR NOT YOSYS_WITHOUT_PLUGINS)
   29|   find_package(PkgConfig REQUIRED)
   30| endif()
   31| if(NOT YOSYS_DISABLE_THREADS)
   32|   find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
   33| endif()
   34| find_package(Git QUIET)
   35| set_property(DIRECTORY APPEND PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/.gitcommit" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/backends/smt2/smtbmc.py" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/backends/smt2/witness.py")
   36| set(YOSYS_VER "${YOSYS_VERSION}")
   37| if(GIT_FOUND)
   38|   execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --git-dir WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} RESULT_VARIABLE git_status OUTPUT_QUIET ERROR_QUIET)
   39|   if(git_status EQUAL 0)
   40|     foreach(git_file HEAD index packed-refs)
   41|       execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --git-path ${git_file} WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE git_path OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)
   42|       get_filename_component(git_path "${git_path}" ABSOLUTE BASE_DIR "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}")
   43|       if(EXISTS "${git_path}")
   44|         set_property(DIRECTORY APPEND PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${git_path}")
   45|       endif()
   46|     endforeach()
   47|     execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} symbolic-ref -q HEAD WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE git_ref OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE ERROR_QUIET)
   48|     if(git_ref)
   49|       execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --git-path ${git_ref} WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE git_path OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)
   50|       get_filename_component(git_path "${git_path}" ABSOLUTE BASE_DIR "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}")
   51|       set_property(DIRECTORY APPEND PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${git_path}")
   52|     endif()
   53|     execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-list --count v${YOSYS_VERSION}..HEAD WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE count OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE ERROR_QUIET)
   54|     if(count AND NOT count STREQUAL "0")
   55|       string(APPEND YOSYS_VER "+${count}")
   56|     endif()
   57|   else()
   58|     string(APPEND YOSYS_VER "+post")
   59|   endif()
   60| endif()
   61| file(STRINGS "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/.gitcommit" GIT_REV)
   62| set(GIT_DIRTY "")
   63| if(GIT_REV MATCHES "Format:")
   64|   set(GIT_REV UNKNOWN)
   65|   if(GIT_FOUND)
   66|     execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} rev-parse --short=9 HEAD WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE rev OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE ERROR_QUIET RESULT_VARIABLE status)
   67|     if(status EQUAL 0)
   68|       set(GIT_REV "${rev}")
   69|     endif()
   70|     execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} diff --exit-code --quiet WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} RESULT_VARIABLE dirty ERROR_QUIET)
   71|     if(NOT dirty EQUAL 0)
   72|       set(GIT_DIRTY "-dirty")
   73|     endif()
   74|   else()
   75|     set(GIT_DIRTY "-dirty")
   76|   endif()
   77| endif()
   78| string(REGEX REPLACE "^[^+]+\\+" "" YOSYS_COMMIT "${YOSYS_VER}")
   79| if(NOT YOSYS_COMMIT)
   80|   set(YOSYS_COMMIT "${YOSYS_VERSION}")
   81| endif()
   82| set(DATDIR "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/share/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys")
   83| set(BINDIR "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/bin")
   84| set(LIBDIR "lib/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys")
   85| set(YOSYS_DEFINITIONS _YOSYS_ "YOSYS_VER=\"${YOSYS_VER}\"" YOSYS_MAJOR=0 YOSYS_MINOR=66 "YOSYS_COMMIT=${YOSYS_COMMIT}" YOSYS_ENABLE_GLOB)
   86| set(YOSYS_OPTIONS -Wall -Wextra -Werror=unused -ggdb -fPIC)
   87| if(MSVC)
   88|   set(YOSYS_OPTIONS /W4 /Z7 /bigobj /Zc:__cplusplus)
   89|   list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS _CRT_SECURE_NO_WARNINGS)
   90|   set(CMAKE_WINDOWS_EXPORT_ALL_SYMBOLS ON)
   91| endif()
   92| set(OPT -O3)
   93| if(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Debug")
   94|   set(OPT -Og)
   95|   list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS DEBUG)
   96| endif()
   97| if(MSVC)
   98|   list(APPEND YOSYS_OPTIONS /O2)
   99| else()
  100|   list(APPEND YOSYS_OPTIONS ${OPT})
  101| endif()
  102| set(YOSYS_LIBRARIES stdc++ m)
  103| set(LIBS "-lstdc++ -lm")
  104| set(LINKFLAGS "-rdynamic")
  105| set(YOSYS_LINK_OPTIONS -rdynamic)
  106| if(MSVC)
  107|   set(YOSYS_LIBRARIES "")
  108|   set(YOSYS_LINK_OPTIONS "")
  109| endif()
  110| if(NOT APPLE AND NOT WIN32 AND NOT YOSYS_WASI)
  111|   list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES rt)
  112|   string(APPEND LIBS " -lrt")
  113| endif()
  114| set(YOSYS_INCLUDES "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}" "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/frontends/verilog" "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/include")
  115| if(NOT YOSYS_WASI)
  116|   list(APPEND YOSYS_INCLUDES ${FLEX_INCLUDE_DIRS})
  117| endif()
  118| set(CONFIG_INCLUDES "")
  119| foreach(feature READLINE PLUGINS ZLIB TCL)
  120|   if(NOT YOSYS_WITHOUT_${feature})
  121|     list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS YOSYS_ENABLE_${feature})
  122|     if(feature STREQUAL "READLINE")
  123|       find_path(READLINE_INCLUDE_DIR readline/readline.h REQUIRED)
  124|       find_library(READLINE_LIBRARY readline REQUIRED)
  125|       list(APPEND YOSYS_INCLUDES ${READLINE_INCLUDE_DIR})
  126|       list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES ${READLINE_LIBRARY})
  127|       string(APPEND LIBS " -lreadline")
  128|     elseif(feature STREQUAL "ZLIB")
  129|       find_package(ZLIB REQUIRED)
  130|       list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES ZLIB::ZLIB)
  131|       string(APPEND LIBS " -lz")
  132|     else()
  133|       if(feature STREQUAL "PLUGINS")
  134|         set(pkg libffi)
  135|       else()
  136|         set(pkg tcl)
  137|       endif()
  138|       pkg_check_modules(${feature} REQUIRED ${pkg})
  139|       list(APPEND YOSYS_INCLUDES ${${feature}_INCLUDE_DIRS})
  140|       list(APPEND YOSYS_OPTIONS ${${feature}_CFLAGS_OTHER})
  141|       list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES ${${feature}_LDFLAGS})
  142|       execute_process(COMMAND ${PKG_CONFIG_EXECUTABLE} --cflags ${pkg} OUTPUT_VARIABLE pkg_cflags OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)
  143|       string(APPEND CONFIG_INCLUDES " ${pkg_cflags}")
  144|       execute_process(COMMAND ${PKG_CONFIG_EXECUTABLE} --libs ${pkg} OUTPUT_VARIABLE pkg_libs OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)
  145|       string(APPEND LIBS " ${pkg_libs}")
  146|       if(feature STREQUAL "PLUGINS")
  147|         list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES ${CMAKE_DL_LIBS})
  148|         if(CMAKE_DL_LIBS)
  149|           string(APPEND LIBS " -ldl")
  150|         endif()
  151|       endif()
  152|     endif()
  153|   endif()
  154| endforeach()
  155| if(NOT YOSYS_DISABLE_THREADS)
  156|   list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS YOSYS_ENABLE_THREADS)
  157|   list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES Threads::Threads)
  158|   string(APPEND LIBS " -lpthread")
  159| endif()
  160| if(YOSYS_ENABLE_HELP_SOURCE)

  171|   set(YOSYS_LINK_OPTIONS -fwasm-exceptions)
  172|   list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS _WASI_EMULATED_PROCESS_CLOCKS YOSYS_DISABLE_SPAWN YOSYS_LINK_ABC)
  173|   list(APPEND YOSYS_OPTIONS -fwasm-exceptions -mllvm -wasm-use-legacy-eh=false)

  207| function(yosys_pattern output name)
  208|   get_filename_component(dir "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${output}" DIRECTORY)
  209|   file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${dir}")
  210|   set(inputs ${ARGN})
  211|   list(TRANSFORM inputs PREPEND "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/")
  212|   add_custom_command(OUTPUT "${output}" COMMAND ${Python3_EXECUTABLE} "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/passes/pmgen/pmgen.py" -o "${output}" -p "${name}" ${inputs} DEPENDS passes/pmgen/pmgen.py ${inputs} VERBATIM)
  213|   set(GENERATED ${GENERATED} "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${output}" PARENT_SCOPE)
  214| endfunction()

  237| if(GIT_FOUND AND git_status EQUAL 0)
  238|   execute_process(COMMAND ${GIT_EXECUTABLE} config --get remote.origin.url WORKING_DIRECTORY ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR} OUTPUT_VARIABLE remote OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE ERROR_QUIET)
  239|   string(TOLOWER "${remote}" remote)
  240|   string(REGEX REPLACE "\\.git$" "" remote "${remote}")
  241|   string(REGEX MATCH "[^/:]+/[^/]+$" repo "${remote}")
  242|   if(repo STREQUAL "yosyshq/yosys")
  243|     set(repo "")
  244|   endif()

  246|   if(NOT branch MATCHES "^(main|HEAD|release/v.*)$")

  248|   endif()
  249|   string(STRIP "${repo}" repo)
  250|   if(repo)

  310| function(yosys_data source destination)
  311|   if(YOSYS_WITHOUT_ZLIB AND source MATCHES "^(libs/fst/|kernel/fstdata)")
  312|     return()
  313|   endif()
  314|   if(source MATCHES "(lut_tree_cells.genlib|lut_tree_map.v|bram_types_sim.v)$")
  315|     set(input "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${source}")
  316|   else()
  317|     set(input "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/${source}")
  318|   endif()
  319|   get_filename_component(dir "${destination}" DIRECTORY)
  320|   add_custom_command(OUTPUT "${destination}" COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -DINPUT=${input} -DOUTPUT=${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${destination} -P "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/CopyData.cmake" DEPENDS "${input}" cmake/CopyData.cmake VERBATIM)
  321|   set(DATA_FILES ${DATA_FILES} "${destination}" PARENT_SCOPE)
  322| endfunction()
  323| include(cmake/Data.cmake)
  324| add_custom_target(share ALL DEPENDS ${DATA_FILES})
  325| add_dependencies(yosys share)
  326| foreach(program smtbmc witness)
  327|     file(READ "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/backends/smt2/${program}.py" script)
  328|     string(REPLACE "##yosys-sys-path##" "sys.path += [os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)) + p for p in [\"/share/python3\", \"/../share/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys/python3\"]]" script "${script}")
  329|     set(script_path "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-${program}")
  330|     set(previous_script "")
  331|     if(EXISTS "${script_path}")
  332|       file(READ "${script_path}" previous_script)
  333|     endif()
  334|     if(NOT previous_script STREQUAL script)
  335|       file(WRITE "${script_path}" "${script}")
  336|     endif()
  337|     file(CHMOD "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-${program}" PERMISSIONS OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE OWNER_EXECUTE GROUP_READ GROUP_EXECUTE WORLD_READ WORLD_EXECUTE)
  338|     install(PROGRAMS "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-${program}" DESTINATION bin)
  339| endforeach()
  340| # yosys-config is available immediately after configuration.
  341| set(CXX "${compiler_name}")
  342| set(CXXFLAGS "${CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS} -Wall -Wextra -Werror=unused -ggdb -I\"${DATDIR}/include\" -MD -MP -D_YOSYS_ -fPIC -I${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/include -DYOSYS_VER=\\\"${YOSYS_VER}\\\" -DYOSYS_MAJOR=0 -DYOSYS_MINOR=66 -DYOSYS_COMMIT=${YOSYS_COMMIT} -std=c++${CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD} ${OPT}")
  343| if(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Sanitize")
  344|   string(APPEND CXXFLAGS " -g -O1 -fno-omit-frame-pointer -fno-optimize-sibling-calls -fsanitize=${SANITIZE}")
  345| endif()
  346| foreach(feature READLINE PLUGINS GLOB ZLIB TCL THREADS ABC)
  347|   if("YOSYS_ENABLE_${feature}" IN_LIST YOSYS_DEFINITIONS)
  348|     if(feature STREQUAL "TCL")
  349|       string(APPEND CXXFLAGS "${CONFIG_INCLUDES}")
  350|     endif()
  351|     string(APPEND CXXFLAGS " -DYOSYS_ENABLE_${feature}")
  352|   endif()
  353| endforeach()
  354| if(YOSYS_WASI)
  355|   set(CXXFLAGS "-std=c++${CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD} ${OPT} -D_WASI_EMULATED_PROCESS_CLOCKS -fwasm-exceptions -mllvm -wasm-use-legacy-eh=false ${CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS} -Wall -Wextra -Werror=unused -ggdb -I\"${DATDIR}/include\" -MD -MP -D_YOSYS_ -I${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/include -DYOSYS_VER=\\\"${YOSYS_VER}\\\" -DYOSYS_MAJOR=0 -DYOSYS_MINOR=66 -DYOSYS_COMMIT=${YOSYS_COMMIT} -DYOSYS_DISABLE_SPAWN -DYOSYS_ENABLE_GLOB")
  356|   if(NOT YOSYS_WITHOUT_ABC)
  357|     string(APPEND CXXFLAGS " -DYOSYS_ENABLE_ABC -DYOSYS_LINK_ABC")
  358|   endif()
  359|   set(LINKFLAGS "${CMAKE_EXE_LINKER_FLAGS} -fwasm-exceptions -lunwind")
  360|   set(LIBS "-lwasi-emulated-process-clocks -lstdc++ -lm")
  361| endif()
  362| string(REGEX REPLACE "[ \t]+" " " CXXFLAGS "${CXXFLAGS}")
  363| string(STRIP "${CXXFLAGS}" CXXFLAGS)
  364| if(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL "Debug")
  365|   string(REPLACE " ${OPT}" "" CXXFLAGS "${CXXFLAGS}")
  366|   set(CXXFLAGS "-Og -DDEBUG ${CXXFLAGS}")
  367| endif()
  368| if(YOSYS_ENABLE_HELP_SOURCE)
  369|   string(REPLACE " -DYOSYS_VER=" " -DYOSYS_ENABLE_HELP_SOURCE -DYOSYS_VER=" CXXFLAGS "${CXXFLAGS}")
  370| endif()
  371| configure_file(misc/yosys-config.in "${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-config" @ONLY)
  372| file(CHMOD "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-config" PERMISSIONS OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE OWNER_EXECUTE GROUP_READ GROUP_EXECUTE WORLD_READ WORLD_EXECUTE)
  373| install(PROGRAMS "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-config" DESTINATION bin)
  374| install(DIRECTORY "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/share/" DESTINATION "share/${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys" USE_SOURCE_PERMISSIONS)
  375| install(TARGETS yosys RUNTIME DESTINATION bin)
  376| foreach(program yosys-abc yosys-filterlib)
  377|   if(TARGET ${program})
  378|     install(TARGETS ${program} RUNTIME DESTINATION bin)
  379|   endif()
  380| endforeach()

  389|     endif()

  392|     install(CODE "execute_process(COMMAND \"${CMAKE_STRIP}\" -S \"\$ENV{DESTDIR}\${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/${LIBDIR}/libyosys.so\" COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)")
  393|   endif()
  394| endif()
  395| include(CTest)
  396| if(BUILD_TESTING AND NOT YOSYS_WASI AND BUILD_SHARED_LIBS)

  409|     DEPENDS "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/CMakeFiles/programs/${program}" VERBATIM)
  410| endforeach()

  412| set(test_settings "")

  421|   set(YOSYS_PYTHON_DESTDIR "${python_install}" CACHE PATH "Python package installation directory")

==> abc/CMakeLists.txt <==
    1| include(${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/AbcSources.cmake)
    2| list(TRANSFORM ABC_SOURCES PREPEND "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/abc/")
    3| set(abc_link_order "")
    4| foreach(extension cc cpp c)
    5|   foreach(source IN LISTS ABC_SOURCES)
    6|     if(source MATCHES "\\.${extension}$")
    7|       list(APPEND abc_link_order "${source}")
    8|     endif()
    9|   endforeach()
   10| endforeach()
   11| set(ABC_SOURCES ${abc_link_order})
   12| set_source_files_properties(${ABC_SOURCES} PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CXX)
   13| if(YOSYS_WASI)
   14|   list(REMOVE_ITEM ABC_SOURCES "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/abc/src/base/main/main.c")
   15| endif()
   16| add_library(abc_objects OBJECT ${ABC_SOURCES})
   17| set_target_properties(abc_objects PROPERTIES CXX_STANDARD 17 POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ${YOSYS_WASI})
   18| target_include_directories(abc_objects PRIVATE ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/abc/src)
   19| target_compile_definitions(abc_objects PRIVATE ABC_USE_STDINT_H ABC_NAMESPACE=abc ABC_USE_CUDD=1)
   20| set(ABC_OPTFLAGS -g -O)
   21| if(YOSYS_WASI)
   22|   set(ABC_OPTFLAGS -Os)
   23| elseif(DEFINED ENV{OPTFLAGS})
   24|   separate_arguments(ABC_OPTFLAGS NATIVE_COMMAND "$ENV{OPTFLAGS}")
   25| endif()
   26| target_compile_options(abc_objects PRIVATE ${ABC_OPTFLAGS} -Wall -Wno-unused-function -Wno-write-strings -Wno-sign-compare -fpermissive)
   27| # The C sources are compiled as C++ but retain exceptions and the compiler's
   28| # default dialect, as required by ABC's namespace build.
   29| foreach(src IN LISTS ABC_SOURCES)
   30|   if(src MATCHES "\\.(cc|cpp)$")
   31|     set_property(SOURCE ${src} APPEND PROPERTY COMPILE_OPTIONS -std=c++17 -fno-exceptions)
   32|   else()
   33|     set_property(SOURCE ${src} PROPERTY COMPILE_OPTIONS -std=gnu++17)
   34|   endif()
   35| endforeach()
   36| set(ABC_LIBRARIES m ${CMAKE_DL_LIBS})
   37| if(NOT APPLE AND NOT YOSYS_WASI)
   38|   list(APPEND ABC_LIBRARIES rt)
   39| endif()
   40| if(NOT YOSYS_WITHOUT_READLINE)
   41|   target_compile_definitions(abc_objects PRIVATE ABC_USE_READLINE)
   42|   target_include_directories(abc_objects PRIVATE ${READLINE_INCLUDE_DIR})
   43|   list(PREPEND ABC_LIBRARIES ${READLINE_LIBRARY})
   44| endif()
   45| if(NOT YOSYS_DISABLE_THREADS)
   46|   target_compile_definitions(abc_objects PRIVATE ABC_USE_PTHREADS)
   47|   list(APPEND ABC_LIBRARIES Threads::Threads)
   48| endif()
   49| if(YOSYS_WASI)
   50|   target_compile_definitions(abc_objects PRIVATE _WASI_EMULATED_PROCESS_CLOCKS ABC_NO_DYNAMIC_LINKING ABC_NO_RLIMIT)
   51| else()
   52|   add_executable(yosys-abc $<TARGET_OBJECTS:abc_objects>)
   53|   set_target_properties(yosys-abc PROPERTIES OUTPUT_NAME "${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}yosys-abc" RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}")
   54|   target_link_libraries(yosys-abc PRIVATE ${ABC_LIBRARIES})
   55| endif()

==> backends/smt2/smtbmc.py <==
    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| #
    3| # yosys -- Yosys Open SYnthesis Suite
    4| #
    5| # Copyright (C) 2012  Claire Xenia Wolf <claire@yosyshq.com>
    6| #
    7| # Permission to use, copy, modify, and/or distribute this software for any
    8| # purpose with or without fee is hereby granted, provided that the above
    9| # copyright notice and this permission notice appear in all copies.
   10| #
   11| # THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS" AND THE AUTHOR DISCLAIMS ALL WARRANTIES
   12| # WITH REGARD TO THIS SOFTWARE INCLUDING ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF
   13| # MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE AUTHOR BE LIABLE FOR
   14| # ANY SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
   15| # WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN
   16| # ACTION OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF
   17| # OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.
   18| #
   19| 
   20| import os, sys, getopt, re, bisect, json
   21| ##yosys-sys-path##
   22| from smtio import SmtIo, SmtOpts, MkVcd
   23| from ywio import ReadWitness, WriteWitness, WitnessValues
   24| from collections import defaultdict
   25| 
   26| got_topt = False
   27| skip_steps = 0
   28| step_size = 1
   29| num_steps = 20
   30| append_steps = 0
   31| vcdfile = None
   32| inywfile = None
   33| outywfile = None
   34| cexfile = None
   35| aimfile = None
   36| aiwfile = None
   37| aigheader = True
   38| btorwitfile = None
   39| vlogtbfile = None
   40| vlogtbtop = None
   41| inconstr = list()
   42| outconstr = None
   43| gentrace = False
   44| covermode = False
   45| tempind = False
   46| dumpall = False
   47| assume_skipped = None
   48| final_only = False
   49| topmod = None
   50| noinfo = False
   51| presat = False
   52| smtcinit = False
   53| smtctop = None
   54| noinit = False
   55| binarymode = False
   56| keep_going = False
   57| check_witness = False
   58| detect_loops = False
   59| incremental = None
   60| track_assumes = False
   61| minimize_assumes = False
   62| so = SmtOpts()
   63| 
   64| 
   65| def help():
   66|     print(os.path.basename(sys.argv[0]) + """ [options] <yosys_smt2_output>
   67| 
   68|     -h, --help
   69|     	show this message
   70| 
   71|     -t <num_steps>
   72|     -t <skip_steps>:<num_steps>
   73|     -t <skip_steps>:<step_size>:<num_steps>
   74|         default: skip_steps=0, step_size=1, num_steps=20
   75| 
   76|     -g
   77|         generate an arbitrary trace that satisfies
   78|         all assertions and assumptions.
   79| 
   80|     -i
   81|         instead of BMC run temporal induction
   82| 
   83|     -c
   84|         instead of regular BMC run cover analysis
   85| 
   86|     -m <module_name>
   87|         name of the top module
   88| 
   89|     --smtc <constr_filename>
   90|         read constraints file
   91| 
   92|     --cex <cex_filename>
   93|         read cex file as written by ABC's "write_cex -n"
   94| 
   95|     --aig <prefix>
   96|         read AIGER map file (as written by Yosys' "write_aiger -map")
   97|         and AIGER witness file. The file names are <prefix>.aim for
   98|         the map file and <prefix>.aiw for the witness file.
   99| 
  100|     --aig <aim_filename>:<aiw_filename>
  101|         like above, but for map files and witness files that do not
  102|         share a filename prefix (or use different file extensions).
  103| 
  104|     --aig-noheader
  105|         the AIGER witness file does not include the status and
  106|         properties lines.
  107| 
  108|     --yw <yosys_witness_filename>
  109|         read a Yosys witness.
  110| 
  111|     --btorwit <btor_witness_filename>
  112|         read a BTOR witness.
  113| 
  114|     --noinfo
  115|         only run the core proof, do not collect and print any
  116|         additional information (e.g. which assert failed)
  117| 
  118|     --presat
  119|         check if the design with assumptions but without assertions
  120|         is SAT before checking if assertions are UNSAT. This will
  121|         detect if there are contradicting assumptions. In some cases
  122|         this will also help to "warm up" the solver, potentially
  123|         yielding a speedup.
  124| 
  125|     --final-only
  126|         only check final constraints, assume base case
  127| 
  128|     --assume-skipped <start_step>
  129|         assume asserts in skipped steps in BMC.
  130|         no assumptions are created for skipped steps
  131|         before <start_step>.
  132| 
  133|     --dump-vcd <vcd_filename>
  134|         write trace to this VCD file
  135|         (hint: use 'write_smt2 -wires' for maximum
  136|         coverage of signals in generated VCD file)
  137| 
  138|     --dump-yw <yw_filename>
  139|         write trace as a Yosys witness trace
  140| 
  141|     --dump-vlogtb <verilog_filename>
  142|         write trace as Verilog test bench
  143| 
  144|     --vlogtb-top <hierarchical_name>
  145|         use the given entity as top module for the generated
  146|         Verilog test bench. The <hierarchical_name> is relative
  147|         to the design top module without the top module name.
  148| 
  149|     --dump-smtc <constr_filename>
  150|         write trace as constraints file
  151| 
  152|     --smtc-init
  153|         write just the last state as initial constraint to smtc file
  154| 
  155|     --smtc-top <old>[:<new>]
  156|         replace <old> with <new> in constraints dumped to smtc
  157|         file and only dump object below <old> in design hierarchy.
  158| 
  159|     --noinit
  160|         do not assume initial conditions in state 0
  161| 
  162|     --dump-all
  163|         when using -g or -i, create a dump file for each
  164|         step. The character '%' is replaced in all dump
  165|         filenames with the step number.
  166| 
  167|     --append <num_steps>
  168|         add <num_steps> time steps at the end of the trace
  169|         when creating a counter example (this additional time
  170|         steps will still be constrained by assumptions)
  171| 
  172|     --binary
  173|         dump anyconst values as raw bit strings
  174| 
  175|     --keep-going
  176|         continue BMC after the first failed assertion and report
  177|         further failed assertions. To output multiple traces
  178|         covering all found failed assertions, the character '%' is
  179|         replaced in all dump filenames with an increasing number.
  180|         In cover mode, don't stop when a cover trace contains a failed
  181|         assertion.
  182| 
  183|     --check-witness
  184|         check that the used witness file contains sufficient
  185|         constraints to force an assertion failure.
  186| 
  187|     --detect-loops
  188|         check if states are unique in temporal induction counter examples
  189|         (this feature is experimental and incomplete)
  190| 
  191|     --incremental
  192|         run in incremental mode (experimental)
  193| 
  194|     --track-assumes
  195|         track individual assumptions and report a subset of used
  196|         assumptions that are sufficient for the reported outcome. This
  197|         can be used to debug PREUNSAT failures as well as to find a
  198|         smaller set of sufficient assumptions.
  199| 
  200|     --minimize-assumes
  201|         when using --track-assumes, solve for a minimal set of sufficient assumptions.
  202| """ + so.helpmsg())
  203| 
  204| def usage():
  205|     help()
  206|     sys.exit(1)
  207| 
  208| 
  209| try:
  210|     opts, args = getopt.getopt(sys.argv[1:], so.shortopts + "t:higcm:", so.longopts +
  211|             ["help", "final-only", "assume-skipped=", "smtc=", "cex=", "aig=", "aig-noheader", "yw=", "btorwit=", "presat",
  212|              "dump-vcd=", "dump-yw=", "dump-vlogtb=", "vlogtb-top=", "dump-smtc=", "dump-all", "noinfo", "append=",
  213|              "smtc-init", "smtc-top=", "noinit", "binary", "keep-going", "check-witness", "detect-loops", "incremental",
  214|              "track-assumes", "minimize-assumes"])
  215| except:
  216|     usage()
  217| 
  218| for o, a in opts:
  219|     if o in ("-h", "--help"):
  220|         help()
  221|         sys.exit(0)
  222|     elif o == "-t":
  223|         got_topt = True
  224|         a = a.split(":")
  225|         if len(a) == 1:
  226|             num_steps = int(a[0])
  227|         elif len(a) == 2:
  228|             skip_steps = int(a[0])
  229|             num_steps = int(a[1])
  230|         elif len(a) == 3:
  231|             skip_steps = int(a[0])
  232|             step_size = int(a[1])
  233|             num_steps = int(a[2])
  234|         else:
  235|             assert False
  236|     elif o == "--assume-skipped":
  237|         assume_skipped = int(a)
  238|     elif o == "--final-only":
  239|         final_only = True
  240|     elif o == "--smtc":
  241|         inconstr.append(a)
  242|     elif o == "--cex":
  243|         cexfile = a
  244|     elif o == "--aig":
  245|         if ":" in a:
  246|             aimfile, aiwfile = a.split(":")
  247|         else:
  248|             aimfile = a + ".aim"
  249|             aiwfile = a + ".aiw"
  250|     elif o == "--aig-noheader":
  251|         aigheader = False
  252|     elif o == "--yw":
  253|         inywfile = a
  254|     elif o == "--btorwit":
  255|         btorwitfile = a
  256|     elif o == "--dump-vcd":
  257|         vcdfile = a
  258|     elif o == "--dump-yw":
  259|         outywfile = a
  260|     elif o == "--dump-vlogtb":
  261|         vlogtbfile = a
  262|     elif o == "--vlogtb-top":
  263|         vlogtbtop = a
  264|     elif o == "--dump-smtc":
  265|         outconstr = a
  266|     elif o == "--smtc-init":
  267|         smtcinit = True
  268|     elif o == "--smtc-top":
  269|         smtctop = a.split(":")
  270|         if len(smtctop) == 1:
  271|             smtctop.append("")
  272|         assert len(smtctop) == 2
  273|         smtctop = tuple(smtctop)
  274|     elif o == "--dump-all":
  275|         dumpall = True
  276|     elif o == "--presat":
  277|         presat = True
  278|     elif o == "--noinfo":
  279|         noinfo = True
  280|     elif o == "--noinit":
  281|         noinit = True
  282|     elif o == "--append":
  283|         append_steps = int(a)
  284|     elif o == "-i":
  285|         tempind = True
  286|     elif o == "-g":
  287|         gentrace = True
  288|     elif o == "-c":
  289|         covermode = True
  290|     elif o == "-m":
  291|         topmod = a
  292|     elif o == "--binary":
  293|         binarymode = True
  294|     elif o == "--keep-going":
  295|         keep_going = True
  296|     elif o == "--check-witness":
  297|         check_witness = True
  298|     elif o == "--detect-loops":
  299|         detect_loops = True
  300|     elif o == "--incremental":
  301|         from smtbmc_incremental import Incremental
  302|         incremental = Incremental()
  303|     elif o == "--track-assumes":
  304|         track_assumes = True
  305|     elif o == "--minimize-assumes":
  306|         minimize_assumes = True
  307|     elif so.handle(o, a):
  308|         pass
  309|     else:
  310|         usage()
  311| 
  312| if len(args) != 1:
  313|     usage()
  314| 
  315| if sum([tempind, gentrace, covermode, incremental is not None]) > 1:
  316|     usage()
  317| 
  318| constr_final_start = None
  319| constr_asserts = defaultdict(list)
  320| constr_assumes = defaultdict(list)
  321| constr_write = list()
  322| 
  323| for fn in inconstr:
  324|     current_states = None
  325|     current_line = 0
  326| 
  327|     with open(fn, "r") as f:
  328|         for line in f:
  329|             current_line += 1
  330| 
  331|             if line.startswith("#"):
  332|                 continue
  333| 
  334|             tokens = line.split()
  335| 
  336|             if len(tokens) == 0:
  337|                 continue
  338| 
  339|             if tokens[0] == "initial":
  340|                 current_states = set()
  341|                 if not tempind:
  342|                     current_states.add(0)
  343|                 continue
  344| 
  345|             if tokens[0] == "final":
  346|                 constr_final = True
  347|                 if len(tokens) == 1:
  348|                     current_states = set(["final-%d" % i for i in range(0, num_steps+1)])
  349|                     constr_final_start = 0
  350|                 elif len(tokens) == 2:
  351|                     arg = abs(int(tokens[1]))
  352|                     current_states = set(["final-%d" % i for i in range(arg, num_steps+1)])
  353|                     constr_final_start = arg if constr_final_start is None else min(constr_final_start, arg)
  354|                 else:
  355|                     assert False
  356|                 continue
  357| 
  358|             if tokens[0] == "state":
  359|                 current_states = set()
  360|                 if not tempind:
  361|                     for token in tokens[1:]:
  362|                         tok = token.split(":")
  363|                         if len(tok) == 1:
  364|                             current_states.add(int(token))
  365|                         elif len(tok) == 2:
  366|                             lower = int(tok[0])
  367|                             if tok[1] == "*":
  368|                                 upper = num_steps
  369|                             else:
  370|                                 upper = int(tok[1])
  371|                             for i in range(lower, upper+1):
  372|                                 current_states.add(i)
  373|                         else:
  374|                             assert False
  375|                 continue
  376| 
  377|             if tokens[0] == "always":
  378|                 if len(tokens) == 1:
  379|                     current_states = set(range(0, num_steps+1))
  380|                 elif len(tokens) == 2:
  381|                     arg = abs(int(tokens[1]))
  382|                     current_states = set(range(arg, num_steps+1))
  383|                 else:
  384|                     assert False
  385|                 continue
  386| 
  387|             if tokens[0] == "assert":
  388|                 assert current_states is not None
  389| 
  390|                 for state in current_states:
  391|                     constr_asserts[state].append(("%s:%d" % (fn, current_line), " ".join(tokens[1:])))
  392| 
  393|                 continue
  394| 
  395|             if tokens[0] == "assume":
  396|                 assert current_states is not None
  397| 
  398|                 for state in current_states:
  399|                     constr_assumes[state].append(("%s:%d" % (fn, current_line), " ".join(tokens[1:])))
  400| 
  401|                 continue
  402| 
  403|             if tokens[0] == "write":
  404|                 constr_write.append(" ".join(tokens[1:]))
  405|                 continue
  406| 
  407|             if tokens[0] == "logic":
  408|                 so.logic = " ".join(tokens[1:])
  409|                 continue
  410| 
  411|             assert False
  412| 
  413| 
  414| def get_constr_expr(db, state, final=False, getvalues=False, individual=False):
  415|     if final:
  416|         if ("final-%d" % state) not in db:
  417|             return ([], [], []) if getvalues or individual else "true"
  418|     else:
  419|         if state not in db:
  420|             return ([], [], []) if getvalues or individual else "true"
  421| 
  422|     netref_regex = re.compile(r'(^|[( ])\[(-?[0-9]+:|)([^\]]*|\S*)\](?=[ )]|$)')
  423| 
  424|     def replace_netref(match):
  425|         state_sel = match.group(2)
  426| 
  427|         if state_sel == "":
  428|             st = state
  429|         elif state_sel[0] == "-":
  430|             st = state + int(state_sel[:-1])
  431|         else:
  432|             st = int(state_sel[:-1])
  433| 
  434|         expr = smt.net_expr(topmod, "s%d" % st, smt.get_path(topmod, match.group(3)))
  435| 
  436|         return match.group(1) + expr
  437| 
  438|     expr_list = list()
  439|     for loc, expr in db[("final-%d" % state) if final else state]:
  440|         actual_expr = netref_regex.sub(replace_netref, expr)
  441|         if getvalues or individual:
  442|             expr_list.append((loc, expr, actual_expr))
  443|         else:
  444|             expr_list.append(actual_expr)
  445| 
  446|     if getvalues or individual:
  447|         loc_list, expr_list, actual_expr_list = zip(*expr_list)
  448|         if individual:
  449|             return loc_list, expr_list, actual_expr_list
  450|         else:
  451|             value_list = smt.get_list(actual_expr_list)
  452|             return loc_list, expr_list, value_list
  453| 
  454|     if len(expr_list) == 0:
  455|         return "true"
  456| 
  457|     if len(expr_list) == 1:
  458|         return expr_list[0]
  459| 
  460|     return "(and %s)" % " ".join(expr_list)
  461| 
  462| 
  463| smt = SmtIo(opts=so)
  464| 
  465| if track_assumes:
  466|     smt.smt2_options[':produce-unsat-assumptions'] = 'true'
  467| 
  468| if noinfo and vcdfile is None and vlogtbfile is None and outconstr is None:
  469|     smt.produce_models = False
  470| 
  471| def print_msg(msg):
  472|     if incremental:
  473|         incremental.print_msg(msg)
  474|     else:
  475|         print("%s %s" % (smt.timestamp(), msg), flush=True)
  476| 
  477| print_msg("Solver: %s" % (so.solver))
  478| 
  479| with open(args[0], "r") as f:
  480|     for line in f:
  481|         smt.write(line)
  482| 
  483| for line in constr_write:
  484|     smt.write(line)
  485| 
  486| if topmod is None:
  487|     topmod = smt.topmod
  488| 
  489| assert topmod is not None
  490| assert topmod in smt.modinfo
  491| 
  492| if cexfile is not None:
  493|     if not got_topt:
  494|         skip_steps = 0
  495|         num_steps = 0
  496| 
  497|     with open(cexfile, "r") as f:
  498|         cex_regex = re.compile(r'([^\[@=]+)(\[\d+\])?([^@=]*)(@\d+)=([01])')
  499|         for entry in f.read().split():
  500|             match = cex_regex.match(entry)
  501|             assert match
  502| 
  503|             name, bit, extra_name, step, val = match.group(1), match.group(2), match.group(3), match.group(4), match.group(5)
  504| 
  505|             if extra_name != "":
  506|                 continue
  507| 
  508|             if name not in smt.modinfo[topmod].inputs:
  509|                 continue
  510| 
  511|             if bit is None:
  512|                 bit = 0
  513|             else:
  514|                 bit = int(bit[1:-1])
  515| 
  516|             step = int(step[1:])
  517|             val = int(val)
  518| 
  519|             if smt.modinfo[topmod].wsize[name] == 1:
  520|                 assert bit == 0
  521|                 smtexpr = "(= [%s] %s)" % (name, "true" if val else "false")
  522|             else:
  523|                 smtexpr = "(= ((_ extract %d %d) [%s]) #b%d)" % (bit, bit, name, val)
  524| 
  525|             # print("cex@%d: %s" % (step, smtexpr))
  526|             constr_assumes[step].append((cexfile, smtexpr))
  527| 
  528|             if not got_topt:
  529|                 if not check_witness:
  530|                     skip_steps = max(skip_steps, step)
  531|                 num_steps = max(num_steps, step+1)
  532| 
  533| if aimfile is not None:
  534|     input_map = dict()
  535|     init_map = dict()
  536|     latch_map = dict()
  537| 
  538|     if not got_topt:
  539|         skip_steps = 0
  540|         num_steps = 0
  541| 
  542|     with open(aimfile, "r") as f:
  543|         for entry in f.read().splitlines():
  544|             entry = entry.split()
  545| 
  546|             if entry[0] == "input":
  547|                 input_map[int(entry[1])] = (entry[3], int(entry[2]))
  548|                 continue
  549| 
  550|             if entry[0] == "init":
  551|                 init_map[int(entry[1])] = (entry[3], int(entry[2]))
  552|                 continue
  553| 
  554|             if entry[0] in ["latch", "invlatch"]:
  555|                 latch_map[int(entry[1])] = (entry[3], int(entry[2]), entry[0] == "invlatch")
  556|                 continue
  557| 
  558|             if entry[0] in ["output", "wire"]:
  559|                 continue
  560| 
  561|             assert False
  562| 
  563|     with open(aiwfile, "r") as f:
  564|         got_state = False
  565|         got_ffinit = False
  566|         step = 0
  567| 
  568|         if not aigheader:
  569|             got_state = True
  570| 
  571|         for entry in f.read().splitlines():
  572|             if len(entry) == 0 or entry[0] in "bcjfu.#":
  573|                 continue
  574| 
  575|             if not got_state:
  576|                 got_state = True
  577|                 assert entry == "1"
  578|                 continue
  579| 
  580|             if not got_ffinit:
  581|                 got_ffinit = True
  582|                 if len(init_map) == 0:
  583|                     for i in range(len(entry)):
  584|                         if entry[i] == "x":
  585|                             continue
  586| 
  587|                         if i in latch_map:
  588|                             value = int(entry[i])
  589|                             name = latch_map[i][0]
  590|                             bitidx = latch_map[i][1]
  591|                             invert = latch_map[i][2]
  592| 
  593|                             if invert:
  594|                                 value = 1 - value
  595| 
  596|                             path = smt.get_path(topmod, name)
  597|                             width = smt.net_width(topmod, path)
  598| 
  599|                             if width == 1:
  600|                                 assert bitidx == 0
  601|                                 smtexpr = "(= [%s] %s)" % (name, "true" if value else "false")
  602|                             else:
  603|                                 smtexpr = "(= ((_ extract %d %d) [%s]) #b%d)" % (bitidx, bitidx, name, value)
  604| 
  605|                             constr_assumes[0].append((cexfile, smtexpr))
  606|                 continue
  607| 
  608|             for i in range(len(entry)):
  609|                 if entry[i] == "x":
  610|                     continue
  611| 
  612|                 if (step == 0) and (i in init_map):
  613|                     value = int(entry[i])
  614|                     name = init_map[i][0]
  615|                     bitidx = init_map[i][1]
  616| 
  617|                     path = smt.get_path(topmod, name)
  618| 
  619|                     if not smt.net_exists(topmod, path):
  620|                         match = re.match(r"(.*)\[(\d+)\]$", path[-1])
  621|                         if match:
  622|                             path[-1] = match.group(1)
  623|                             addr = int(match.group(2))
  624| 
  625|                         if not match or not smt.mem_exists(topmod, path):
  626|                             print_msg("Ignoring init value for unknown net: %s" % (name))
  627|                             continue
  628| 
  629|                         meminfo = smt.mem_info(topmod, path)
  630|                         smtexpr = "(select [%s] #b%s)" % (".".join(path), bin(addr)[2:].zfill(meminfo[0]))
  631|                         width = meminfo[1]
  632| 
  633|                     else:
  634|                         smtexpr = "[%s]" % name
  635|                         width = smt.net_width(topmod, path)
  636| 
  637|                     if width == 1:
  638|                         assert bitidx == 0
  639|                         smtexpr = "(= %s %s)" % (smtexpr, "true" if value else "false")
  640|                     else:
  641|                         smtexpr = "(= ((_ extract %d %d) %s) #b%d)" % (bitidx, bitidx, smtexpr, value)
  642| 
  643|                     constr_assumes[0].append((cexfile, smtexpr))
  644| 
  645|                 if i in input_map:
  646|                     value = int(entry[i])
  647|                     name = input_map[i][0]
  648|                     bitidx = input_map[i][1]
  649| 
  650|                     path = smt.get_path(topmod, name)
  651|                     width = smt.net_width(topmod, path)
  652| 
  653|                     if width == 1:
  654|                         assert bitidx == 0
  655|                         smtexpr = "(= [%s] %s)" % (name, "true" if value else "false")
  656|                     else:
  657|                         smtexpr = "(= ((_ extract %d %d) [%s]) #b%d)" % (bitidx, bitidx, name, value)
  658| 
  659|                     constr_assumes[step].append((cexfile, smtexpr))
  660| 
  661|             if not got_topt:
  662|                 if not check_witness:
  663|                     skip_steps = max(skip_steps, step)
  664|                 # some solvers optimize the properties so that they fail one cycle early,
  665|                 # thus we check the properties in the cycle the aiger witness ends, and
  666|                 # if that doesn't work, we check the cycle after that as well.
  667|                 num_steps = max(num_steps, step+2)
  668|             step += 1
  669| 
  670| ywfile_hierwitness_cache = None
  671| 
  672| def ywfile_hierwitness():
  673|     global ywfile_hierwitness_cache
  674|     if ywfile_hierwitness_cache is None:
  675|         ywfile_hierwitness = smt.hierwitness(topmod, allregs=True, blackbox=True)
  676| 
  677|         inits, seqs, clocks, mems = ywfile_hierwitness
  678| 
  679|         smt_wires = defaultdict(list)
  680|         smt_mems = defaultdict(list)
  681| 
  682|         for wire in inits + seqs:
  683|             smt_wires[wire["path"]].append(wire)
  684| 
  685|         for mem in mems:
  686|             smt_mems[mem["path"]].append(mem)
  687| 
  688|         ywfile_hierwitness_cache = inits, seqs, clocks, mems, smt_wires, smt_mems
  689| 
  690|     return ywfile_hierwitness_cache
  691| 
  692| def_bits_re = re.compile(r'([01]+)')
  693| 
  694| def smt_extract_mask(smt_expr, mask):
  695|     chunks = []
  696|     def_bits = ''
  697| 
  698|     mask_index_order = mask[::-1]
  699| 
  700|     for matched in def_bits_re.finditer(mask_index_order):
  701|         chunks.append(matched.span())
  702|         def_bits += matched[0]
  703| 
  704|     if not chunks:
  705|         return
  706| 
  707|     if len(chunks) == 1:
  708|         start, end = chunks[0]
  709|         if start == 0 and end == len(mask_index_order):
  710|             combined_chunks = smt_expr
  711|         else:
  712|             combined_chunks = '((_ extract %d %d) %s)' % (end - 1, start, smt_expr)
  713|     else:
  714|         combined_chunks = '(let ((x %s)) (concat %s))' % (smt_expr, ' '.join(
  715|             '((_ extract %d %d) x)' % (end - 1, start)
  716|             for start, end in reversed(chunks)
  717|         ))
  718| 
  719|     return combined_chunks, ''.join(mask_index_order[start:end] for start, end in chunks)[::-1]
  720| 
  721| def smt_concat(exprs):
  722|     if not isinstance(exprs, (tuple, list)):
  723|         exprs = tuple(exprs)
  724|     if not exprs:
  725|         return ""
  726|     if len(exprs) == 1:
  727|         return exprs[1]
  728|     return "(concat %s)" % ' '.join(exprs)
  729| 
  730| def ywfile_signal(sig, step, mask=None):
  731|     assert sig.width > 0
  732| 
  733|     inits, seqs, clocks, mems, smt_wires, smt_mems = ywfile_hierwitness()
  734|     sig_end = sig.offset + sig.width
  735| 
  736|     output = []
  737| 
  738|     def ywfile_signal_error(reason, detail=None):
  739|         msg = f"Yosys witness signal mismatch for {sig.pretty()}: {reason}"
  740|         if detail:
  741|             msg += f" ({detail})"
  742|         raise ValueError(msg)
  743| 
  744|     if sig.path in smt_wires:
  745|         for wire in smt_wires[sig.path]:
  746|             width, offset = wire["width"], wire["offset"]
  747| 
  748|             smt_bool = smt.net_width(topmod, wire["smtpath"]) == 1
  749| 
  750|             offset = max(offset, 0)
  751| 
  752|             end = width + offset
  753|             common_offset = max(sig.offset, offset)
  754|             common_end = min(sig_end, end)
  755|             if common_end <= common_offset:
  756|                 continue
  757| 
  758|             smt_expr = smt.witness_net_expr(topmod, f"s{step}", wire)
  759| 
  760|             if not smt_bool:
  761|                 slice_high = common_end - offset - 1
  762|                 slice_low = common_offset - offset
  763|                 smt_expr = "((_ extract %d %d) %s)" % (slice_high, slice_low, smt_expr)
  764|             else:
  765|                 smt_expr = "(ite %s #b1 #b0)" % smt_expr
  766| 
  767|             output.append(((common_offset - sig.offset), (common_end - sig.offset), smt_expr))
  768| 
  769|     if sig.memory_path:
  770|         if sig.memory_path in smt_mems:
  771|             for mem in smt_mems[sig.memory_path]:
  772|                 width, size, bv = mem["width"], mem["size"], mem["statebv"]
  773| 
  774|                 if sig.memory_addr is not None and sig.memory_addr >= size:
  775|                     ywfile_signal_error(
  776|                         "memory address out of bounds",
  777|                         f"address={sig.memory_addr} size={size}",
  778|                     )
  779| 
  780|                 smt_expr = smt.net_expr(topmod, f"s{step}", mem["smtpath"])
  781| 
  782|                 if bv:
  783|                     word_low = sig.memory_addr * width
  784|                     word_high = word_low + width - 1
  785|                     smt_expr = "((_ extract %d %d) %s)" % (word_high, word_low, smt_expr)
  786|                 else:
  787|                     addr_width = (size - 1).bit_length()
  788|                     addr_bits = f"{sig.memory_addr:0{addr_width}b}"
  789|                     smt_expr = "(select %s #b%s )" % (smt_expr, addr_bits)
  790| 
  791|                 if sig.width < width:
  792|                     slice_high = sig.offset + sig.width - 1
  793|                     smt_expr = "((_ extract %d %d) %s)" % (slice_high, sig.offset, smt_expr)
  794| 
  795|                 output.append((0, sig.width, smt_expr))
  796|         else:
  797|             ywfile_signal_error("memory not found in design")
  798| 
  799|     output.sort()
  800| 
  801|     output = [chunk for chunk in output if chunk[0] != chunk[1]]
  802| 
  803|     if not output:
  804|         if sig.memory_path:
  805|             ywfile_signal_error("memory signal has no matching bits in design")
  806|         else:
  807|             ywfile_signal_error("signal not found in design")
  808| 
  809|     pos = 0
  810| 
  811|     for start, end, smt_expr in output:
  812|         if start != pos:
  813|             ywfile_signal_error(
  814|                 "signal width/offset mismatch",
  815|                 f"expected coverage at bit {pos}",
  816|             )
  817|         pos = end
  818| 
  819|     if pos != sig.width:
  820|         ywfile_signal_error(
  821|             "signal width/offset mismatch",
  822|             f"covered {pos} of {sig.width} bits",
  823|         )
  824| 
  825|     if len(output) == 1:
  826|         return output[0][-1]
  827|     return smt_concat(smt_expr for start, end, smt_expr in reversed(output))
  828| 
  829| def ywfile_constraints(inywfile, constr_assumes, map_steps=None, skip_x=False):
  830|     global ywfile_hierwitness_cache
  831|     if map_steps is None:
  832|         map_steps = {}
  833| 
  834|     with open(inywfile, "r") as f:
  835|         inyw = ReadWitness(f)
  836| 
  837|         inits, seqs, clocks, mems, smt_wires, smt_mems = ywfile_hierwitness()
  838| 
  839|         bits_re = re.compile(r'[01?]*$')
  840|         max_t = -1
  841| 
  842|         for t, step in inyw.steps():
  843|             present_signals, missing = step.present_signals(inyw.sigmap)
  844|             for sig in present_signals:
  845|                 bits = step[sig]
  846|                 if skip_x:
  847|                     bits = bits.replace('x', '?')
  848|                 if not bits_re.match(bits):
  849|                     raise ValueError("unsupported bit value in Yosys witness file")
  850| 
  851|                 if bits.count('?') == len(bits):
  852|                     continue
  853| 
  854|                 smt_expr = ywfile_signal(sig, map_steps.get(t, t))
  855| 
  856|                 smt_expr, bits = smt_extract_mask(smt_expr, bits)
  857| 
  858|                 smt_constr = "(= %s #b%s)" % (smt_expr, bits)
  859|                 constr_assumes[t].append((inywfile, smt_constr))
  860| 
  861|             max_t = t
  862|         return max_t
  863| 
  864| if inywfile is not None:
  865|     if not got_topt:
  866|         skip_steps = 0
  867|         num_steps = 0
  868| 
  869|     max_t = ywfile_constraints(inywfile, constr_assumes)
  870| 
  871|     if not got_topt:
  872|         if not check_witness:
  873|             skip_steps = max(skip_steps, max_t)
  874|         num_steps = max(num_steps, max_t+1)
  875| 
  876| if btorwitfile is not None:
  877|     with open(btorwitfile, "r") as f:
  878|         step = None
  879|         suffix = None
  880|         altsuffix = None
  881|         header_okay = False
  882| 
  883|         for line in f:
  884|             line = line.strip()
  885| 
  886|             if line == "sat":
  887|                 header_okay = True
  888|                 continue
  889| 
  890|             if not header_okay:
  891|                 continue
  892| 
  893|             if line == "" or line[0] == "b" or line[0] == "j":
  894|                 continue
  895| 
  896|             if line == ".":
  897|                 break
  898| 
  899|             if line[0] == '#' or line[0] == '@':
  900|                 step = int(line[1:])
  901|                 suffix = line
  902|                 altsuffix = suffix
  903|                 if suffix[0] == "@":
  904|                     altsuffix = "#" + suffix[1:]
  905|                 else:
  906|                     altsuffix = "@" + suffix[1:]
  907|                 continue
  908| 
  909|             line = line.split()
  910| 
  911|             if len(line) == 0:
  912|                 continue
  913| 
  914|             if line[-1].endswith(suffix):
  915|                 line[-1] = line[-1][0:len(line[-1]) - len(suffix)]
  916| 
  917|             if line[-1].endswith(altsuffix):
  918|                 line[-1] = line[-1][0:len(line[-1]) - len(altsuffix)]
  919| 
  920|             if line[-1][0] == "$":
  921|                 continue
  922| 
  923|             # BV assignments
  924|             if len(line) == 3 and line[1][0] != "[":
  925|                 value = line[1]
  926|                 name = line[2]
  927| 
  928|                 path = smt.get_path(topmod, name)
  929| 
  930|                 if not smt.net_exists(topmod, path):
  931|                     continue
  932| 
  933|                 width = smt.net_width(topmod, path)
  934| 
  935|                 if width == 1:
  936|                     assert value in ["0", "1"]
  937|                     value = "true" if value == "1" else "false"
  938|                 else:
  939|                     value = "#b" + value
  940| 
  941|                 smtexpr = "(= [%s] %s)" % (name, value)
  942|                 constr_assumes[step].append((btorwitfile, smtexpr))
  943| 
  944|             # Array assignments
  945|             if len(line) == 4 and line[1][0] == "[":
  946|                 index = line[1]
  947|                 value = line[2]
  948|                 name = line[3]
  949| 
  950|                 path = smt.get_path(topmod, name)
  951| 
  952|                 if not smt.mem_exists(topmod, path):
  953|                     continue
  954| 
  955|                 meminfo = smt.mem_info(topmod, path)
  956| 
  957|                 if meminfo[1] == 1:
  958|                     assert value in ["0", "1"]
  959|                     value = "true" if value == "1" else "false"
  960|                 else:
  961|                     value = "#b" + value
  962| 
  963|                 assert index[0] == "["
  964|                 assert index[-1] == "]"
  965|                 index = "#b" + index[1:-1]
  966| 
  967|                 smtexpr = "(= (select [%s] %s) %s)" % (name, index, value)
  968|                 constr_assumes[step].append((btorwitfile, smtexpr))
  969| 
  970|         skip_steps = step
  971|         num_steps = step+1
  972| 
  973| def collect_mem_trace_data(steps, vcd=None):
  974|     mem_trace_data = dict()
  975| 
  976|     for mempath in sorted(smt.hiermems(topmod)):
  977|         abits, width, rports, wports, asyncwr = smt.mem_info(topmod, mempath)
  978| 
  979|         expr_id = list()
  980|         expr_list = list()
  981|         for seq, i in enumerate(steps):
  982|             for j in range(rports):
  983|                 expr_id.append(('R', seq, j, 'A'))
  984|                 expr_id.append(('R', seq, j, 'D'))
  985|                 expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(topmod, "s%d" % i, mempath, "R%dA" % j))
  986|                 expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(topmod, "s%d" % i, mempath, "R%dD" % j))
  987|             for j in range(wports):
  988|                 expr_id.append(('W', seq, j, 'A'))
  989|                 expr_id.append(('W', seq, j, 'D'))
  990|                 expr_id.append(('W', seq, j, 'M'))
  991|                 expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(topmod, "s%d" % i, mempath, "W%dA" % j))
  992|                 expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(topmod, "s%d" % i, mempath, "W%dD" % j))
  993|                 expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(topmod, "s%d" % i, mempath, "W%dM" % j))
  994| 
  995|         rdata = list()
  996|         wdata = list()
  997|         addrs = set()
  998| 
  999|         for eid, edat in zip(expr_id, smt.get_list(expr_list)):
 1000|             t, i, j, f = eid
 1001| 
 1002|             if t == 'R':
 1003|                 c = rdata
 1004|             elif t == 'W':
 1005|                 c = wdata
 1006|             else:
 1007|                 assert False
 1008| 
 1009|             while len(c) <= i:
 1010|                 c.append(list())
 1011|             c = c[i]
 1012| 
 1013|             while len(c) <= j:
 1014|                 c.append(dict())
 1015|             c = c[j]
 1016| 
 1017|             c[f] = smt.bv2bin(edat)
 1018| 
 1019|             if f == 'A':
 1020|                 addrs.add(c[f])
 1021| 
 1022|         for addr in addrs:
 1023|             tdata = list()
 1024|             data = ["x"] * width
 1025|             gotread = False
 1026| 
 1027|             if len(wdata) == 0 and len(rdata) != 0:
 1028|                 wdata = [[]] * len(rdata)
 1029| 
 1030|             assert len(rdata) == len(wdata)
 1031| 
 1032|             for i in range(len(wdata)):
 1033|                 if not gotread:
 1034|                     for j_data in rdata[i]:
 1035|                         if j_data["A"] == addr:
 1036|                             data = list(j_data["D"])
 1037|                             gotread = True
 1038|                             break
 1039| 
 1040|                     if gotread:
 1041|                         buf = data[:]
 1042|                         for ii in reversed(range(len(tdata))):
 1043|                             for k in range(width):
 1044|                                 if tdata[ii][k] == "x":
 1045|                                     tdata[ii][k] = buf[k]
 1046|                                 else:
 1047|                                     buf[k] = tdata[ii][k]
 1048| 
 1049|                 if not asyncwr:
 1050|                     tdata.append(data[:])
 1051| 
 1052|                 for j_data in wdata[i]:
 1053|                     if j_data["A"] != addr:
 1054|                         continue
 1055| 
 1056|                     D = j_data["D"]
 1057|                     M = j_data["M"]
 1058| 
 1059|                     for k in range(width):
 1060|                         if M[k] == "1":
 1061|                             data[k] = D[k]
 1062| 
 1063|                 if asyncwr:
 1064|                     tdata.append(data[:])
 1065| 
 1066|             assert len(tdata) == len(rdata)
 1067| 
 1068|             int_addr = int(addr, 2)
 1069| 
 1070|             netpath = mempath[:]
 1071|             if vcd:
 1072|                 netpath[-1] += "<%0*x>" % ((len(addr)+3) // 4, int_addr)
 1073|                 vcd.add_net([topmod] + netpath, width)
 1074| 
 1075|             for seq, i in enumerate(steps):
 1076|                 if i not in mem_trace_data:
 1077|                     mem_trace_data[i] = list()
 1078|                 mem_trace_data[i].append((netpath, int_addr, "".join(tdata[seq])))
 1079| 
 1080|     return mem_trace_data
 1081| 
 1082| def write_vcd_trace(steps, index, seq_time=False):
 1083|     filename = vcdfile.replace("%", index)
 1084|     print_msg("Writing trace to VCD file: %s" % (filename))
 1085| 
 1086|     with open(filename, "w") as vcd_file:
 1087|         vcd = MkVcd(vcd_file)
 1088|         path_list = list()
 1089| 
 1090|         for netpath in sorted(smt.hiernets(topmod)):
 1091|             hidden_net = False
 1092|             for n in netpath:
 1093|                 if n.startswith("$"):
 1094|                     hidden_net = True
 1095|             if not hidden_net:
 1096|                 edge = smt.net_clock(topmod, netpath)
 1097|                 if edge is None:
 1098|                     vcd.add_net([topmod] + netpath, smt.net_width(topmod, netpath))
 1099|                 else:
 1100|                     vcd.add_clock([topmod] + netpath, edge)
 1101|                 path_list.append(netpath)
 1102| 
 1103|         mem_trace_data = collect_mem_trace_data(steps, vcd)
 1104| 
 1105|         for seq, i in enumerate(steps):
 1106|             vcd.set_time(seq if seq_time else i)
 1107|             value_list = smt.get_net_bin_list(topmod, path_list, "s%d" % i)
 1108|             for path, value in zip(path_list, value_list):
 1109|                 vcd.set_net([topmod] + path, value)
 1110|             if i in mem_trace_data:
 1111|                 for path, addr, value in mem_trace_data[i]:
 1112|                     vcd.set_net([topmod] + path, value)
 1113| 
 1114|         if seq_time:
 1115|             end_time = len(steps)
 1116|         elif steps:
 1117|             end_time = steps[-1] + 1
 1118|         else:
 1119|             end_time = 0
 1120| 
 1121|         vcd.set_time(end_time)
 1122| 
 1123| def detect_state_loop(steps_start, steps_stop):
 1124|     print_msg(f"Checking for loops in found induction counter example")
 1125|     print_msg(f"This feature is experimental and incomplete")
 1126| 
 1127|     path_list = sorted(smt.hiernets(topmod, regs_only=True))
 1128| 
 1129|     mem_trace_data = collect_mem_trace_data(steps_start, steps_stop)
 1130| 
 1131|     # Map state to index of step when it occurred
 1132|     states = dict()
 1133| 
 1134|     for i in range(steps_start, steps_stop):
 1135|         value_list = smt.get_net_bin_list(topmod, path_list, "s%d" % i)
 1136|         mem_state = sorted(
 1137|             [(tuple(path), addr, data)
 1138|              for path, addr, data in mem_trace_data.get(i, [])])
 1139|         state = tuple(value_list), tuple(mem_state)
 1140|         if state in states:
 1141|             return (i, states[state])
 1142|         else:
 1143|             states[state] = i
 1144| 
 1145|     return None
 1146| 
 1147| def char_ok_in_verilog(c,i):
 1148|     if ('A' <= c <= 'Z'): return True
 1149|     if ('a' <= c <= 'z'): return True
 1150|     if ('0' <= c <= '9' and i>0): return True
 1151|     if (c == '_'): return True
 1152|     if (c == '$'): return True
 1153|     return False
 1154| 
 1155| def escape_identifier(identifier):
 1156|     if type(identifier) is list:
 1157|         return map(escape_identifier, identifier)
 1158|     if "." in identifier:
 1159|         return ".".join(escape_identifier(identifier.split(".")))
 1160|     if (all(char_ok_in_verilog(identifier[i],i) for i in range(0, len(identifier)))):
 1161|         return identifier
 1162|     return "\\"+identifier+" "
 1163|

 1166| def write_vlogtb_trace(steps, index):
 1167|     filename = vlogtbfile.replace("%", index)
 1168|     print_msg("Writing trace to Verilog testbench: %s" % (filename))
 1169| 
 1170|     vlogtb_topmod = topmod
 1171|     vlogtb_state = "s@@step_idx@@"
 1172| 
 1173|     if vlogtbtop is not None:
 1174|         for item in vlogtbtop.split("."):
 1175|             if item in smt.modinfo[vlogtb_topmod].cells:
 1176|                 vlogtb_state = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (vlogtb_topmod, item, vlogtb_state)
 1177|                 vlogtb_topmod = smt.modinfo[vlogtb_topmod].cells[item]
 1178|             else:
 1179|                 print_msg("Vlog top module '%s' not found: no cell '%s' in module '%s'" % (vlogtbtop, item, vlogtb_topmod))
 1180|                 break
 1181| 
 1182|     with open(filename, "w") as f:
 1183|         print("`ifndef VERILATOR", file=f)
 1184|         print("module testbench;", file=f)
 1185|         print("  reg [4095:0] vcdfile;", file=f)
 1186|         print("  reg clock;", file=f)
 1187|         print("`else", file=f)
 1188|         print("module testbench(input clock, output reg genclock);", file=f)
 1189|         print("  initial genclock = 1;", file=f)
 1190|         print("`endif", file=f)
 1191| 
 1192|         print("  reg genclock = 1;", file=f)
 1193|         print("  reg [31:0] cycle = 0;", file=f)
 1194| 
 1195|         primary_inputs = list()
 1196|         clock_inputs = set()
 1197| 
 1198|         for name in smt.modinfo[vlogtb_topmod].inputs:
 1199|             if name in ["clk", "clock", "CLK", "CLOCK"]:
 1200|                 clock_inputs.add(name)
 1201|             width = smt.modinfo[vlogtb_topmod].wsize[name]
 1202|             primary_inputs.append((name, width))
 1203| 
 1204|         for name, width in primary_inputs:
 1205|             if name in clock_inputs:
 1206|                 print("  wire [%d:0] %s = clock;" % (width-1, escape_identifier("PI_"+name)), file=f)
 1207|             else:
 1208|                 print("  reg [%d:0] %s;" % (width-1, escape_identifier("PI_"+name)), file=f)
 1209| 
 1210|         print("  %s UUT (" % escape_identifier(vlogtb_topmod), file=f)
 1211|         print(",\n".join("    .%s(%s)" % (escape_identifier(name), escape_identifier("PI_"+name)) for name, _ in primary_inputs), file=f)
 1212|         print("  );", file=f)
 1213| 
 1214|         print("`ifndef VERILATOR", file=f)
 1215|         print("  initial begin", file=f)
 1216|         print("    if ($value$plusargs(\"vcd=%s\", vcdfile)) begin", file=f)
 1217|         print("      $dumpfile(vcdfile);", file=f)
 1218|         print("      $dumpvars(0, testbench);", file=f)
 1219|         print("    end", file=f)
 1220|         print("    #5 clock = 0;", file=f)
 1221|         print("    while (genclock) begin", file=f)
 1222|         print("      #5 clock = 0;", file=f)
 1223|         print("      #5 clock = 1;", file=f)
 1224|         print("    end", file=f)
 1225|         print("  end", file=f)
 1226|         print("`endif", file=f)
 1227| 
 1228|         print("  initial begin", file=f)
 1229| 
 1230|         regs = sorted(smt.hiernets(vlogtb_topmod, regs_only=True))
 1231|         regvals = smt.get_net_bin_list(vlogtb_topmod, regs, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(steps[0])))
 1232| 
 1233|         print("`ifndef VERILATOR", file=f)
 1234|         print("    #1;", file=f)
 1235|         print("`endif", file=f)
 1236|         for reg, val in zip(regs, regvals):
 1237|             hidden_net = False
 1238|             for n in reg:
 1239|                 if n.startswith("$"):
 1240|                     hidden_net = True
 1241|             print("    %sUUT.%s = %d'b%s;" % ("// " if hidden_net else "", ".".join(escape_identifier(reg)), len(val), val), file=f)
 1242| 
 1243|         anyconsts = sorted(smt.hieranyconsts(vlogtb_topmod))
 1244|         for info in anyconsts:
 1245|             if info[3] is not None:
 1246|                 modstate = smt.net_expr(vlogtb_topmod, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(steps[0])), info[0])
 1247|                 value = smt.bv2bin(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (info[1], modstate)))
 1248|                 print("    UUT.%s = %d'b%s;" % (".".join(escape_identifier(info[0] + [info[3]])), len(value), value), file=f);
 1249| 
 1250|         mems = sorted(smt.hiermems(vlogtb_topmod))
 1251|         for mempath in mems:
 1252|             abits, width, rports, wports, asyncwr = smt.mem_info(vlogtb_topmod, mempath)
 1253| 
 1254|             addr_expr_list = list()
 1255|             data_expr_list = list()
 1256|             for i in steps:
 1257|                 for j in range(rports):
 1258|                     addr_expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(vlogtb_topmod, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)), mempath, "R%dA" % j))
 1259|                     data_expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(vlogtb_topmod, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)), mempath, "R%dD" % j))
 1260| 
 1261|             addr_list = smt.get_list(addr_expr_list)
 1262|             data_list = smt.get_list(data_expr_list)
 1263| 
 1264|             addr_data = dict()
 1265|             for addr, data in zip(addr_list, data_list):
 1266|                 addr = smt.bv2bin(addr)
 1267|                 data = smt.bv2bin(data)
 1268|                 if addr not in addr_data:
 1269|                     addr_data[addr] = data
 1270| 
 1271|             for addr, data in addr_data.items():
 1272|                 print("    UUT.%s[%d'b%s] = %d'b%s;" % (".".join(escape_identifier(mempath)), len(addr), addr, len(data), data), file=f)
 1273| 
 1274|         print("", file=f)
 1275|         anyseqs = sorted(smt.hieranyseqs(vlogtb_topmod))
 1276| 
 1277|         for i in steps:
 1278|             pi_names = [[name] for name, _ in primary_inputs if name not in clock_inputs]
 1279|             pi_values = smt.get_net_bin_list(vlogtb_topmod, pi_names, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)))
 1280| 
 1281|             print("    // state %d" % i, file=f)
 1282| 
 1283|             if i > 0:
 1284|                 print("    if (cycle == %d) begin" % (i-1), file=f)
 1285| 
 1286|             for name, val in zip(pi_names, pi_values):
 1287|                 if i > 0:
 1288|                     print("      %s <= %d'b%s;" % (escape_identifier("PI_"+".".join(name)), len(val), val), file=f)
 1289|                 else:
 1290|                     print("    %s = %d'b%s;" % (escape_identifier("PI_"+".".join(name)), len(val), val), file=f)
 1291| 
 1292|             for info in anyseqs:
 1293|                 if info[3] is not None:
 1294|                     modstate = smt.net_expr(vlogtb_topmod, vlogtb_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)), info[0])
 1295|                     value = smt.bv2bin(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (info[1], modstate)))
 1296|                     if i > 0:
 1297|                         print("      UUT.%s <= %d'b%s;" % (".".join(escape_identifier(info[0] + [info[3]])), len(value), value), file=f);
 1298|                     else:
 1299|                         print("    UUT.%s = %d'b%s;" % (".".join(escape_identifier(info[0] + [info[3]])), len(value), value), file=f);
 1300| 
 1301|             if i > 0:
 1302|                 print("    end", file=f)
 1303|                 print("", file=f)
 1304| 
 1305|             if i == 0:
 1306|                 print("  end", file=f)
 1307|                 print("  always @(posedge clock) begin", file=f)
 1308| 
 1309|         print("    genclock <= cycle < %d;" % (steps[-1]), file=f)
 1310|         print("    cycle <= cycle + 1;", file=f)
 1311|         print("  end", file=f)
 1312| 
 1313|         print("endmodule", file=f)

 1316| def write_constr_trace(steps, index):
 1317|     filename = outconstr.replace("%", index)
 1318|     print_msg("Writing trace to constraints file: %s" % (filename))
 1319| 
 1320|     constr_topmod = topmod
 1321|     constr_state = "s@@step_idx@@"
 1322|     constr_prefix = ""
 1323| 
 1324|     if smtctop is not None:
 1325|         for item in smtctop[0].split("."):
 1326|             assert item in smt.modinfo[constr_topmod].cells
 1327|             constr_state = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (constr_topmod, item, constr_state)
 1328|             constr_topmod = smt.modinfo[constr_topmod].cells[item]
 1329|         if smtctop[1] != "":
 1330|             constr_prefix = smtctop[1] + "."
 1331| 
 1332|     if smtcinit:
 1333|         steps = [steps[-1]]
 1334| 
 1335|     with open(filename, "w") as f:
 1336|         primary_inputs = list()
 1337| 
 1338|         for name in smt.modinfo[constr_topmod].inputs:
 1339|             width = smt.modinfo[constr_topmod].wsize[name]
 1340|             primary_inputs.append((name, width))
 1341| 
 1342|         if steps[0] == 0 or smtcinit:
 1343|             print("initial", file=f)
 1344|         else:
 1345|             print("state %d" % steps[0], file=f)
 1346| 
 1347|         regnames = sorted(smt.hiernets(constr_topmod, regs_only=True))
 1348|         regvals = smt.get_net_list(constr_topmod, regnames, constr_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(steps[0])))
 1349| 
 1350|         for name, val in zip(regnames, regvals):
 1351|             print("assume (= [%s%s] %s)" % (constr_prefix, ".".join(name), val), file=f)
 1352| 
 1353|         mems = sorted(smt.hiermems(constr_topmod))
 1354|         for mempath in mems:
 1355|             abits, width, rports, wports, asyncwr = smt.mem_info(constr_topmod, mempath)
 1356| 
 1357|             addr_expr_list = list()
 1358|             data_expr_list = list()
 1359|             for i in steps:
 1360|                 for j in range(rports):
 1361|                     addr_expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(constr_topmod, constr_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)), mempath, "R%dA" % j))
 1362|                     data_expr_list.append(smt.mem_expr(constr_topmod, constr_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(i)), mempath, "R%dD" % j))
 1363| 
 1364|             addr_list = smt.get_list(addr_expr_list)
 1365|             data_list = smt.get_list(data_expr_list)
 1366| 
 1367|             addr_data = dict()
 1368|             for addr, data in zip(addr_list, data_list):
 1369|                 if addr not in addr_data:
 1370|                     addr_data[addr] = data
 1371| 
 1372|             for addr, data in addr_data.items():
 1373|                 print("assume (= (select [%s%s] %s) %s)" % (constr_prefix, ".".join(mempath), addr, data), file=f)
 1374| 
 1375|         for k in steps:
 1376|             if not smtcinit:
 1377|                 print("", file=f)
 1378|                 print("state %d" % k, file=f)
 1379| 
 1380|             pi_names = [[name] for name, _ in sorted(primary_inputs)]
 1381|             pi_values = smt.get_net_list(constr_topmod, pi_names, constr_state.replace("@@step_idx@@", str(k)))
 1382| 
 1383|             for name, val in zip(pi_names, pi_values):
 1384|                 print("assume (= [%s%s] %s)" % (constr_prefix, ".".join(name), val), file=f)
