 1386| def write_yw_trace(steps, index, allregs=False, filename=None):
 1387|     if filename is None:
 1388|         if outywfile is None:
 1389|             return
 1390|         filename = outywfile.replace("%", index)
 1391|         print_msg("Writing trace to Yosys witness file: %s" % (filename))
 1392| 
 1393|     mem_trace_data = collect_mem_trace_data(steps)
 1394| 
 1395|     with open(filename, "w") as f:
 1396|         inits, seqs, clocks, mems = smt.hierwitness(topmod, allregs)
 1397| 
 1398|         yw = WriteWitness(f, "smtbmc")
 1399| 
 1400|         for clock in clocks:
 1401|             yw.add_clock(clock["path"], clock["offset"], clock["type"])
 1402| 
 1403|         for seq in seqs:
 1404|             seq["sig"] = yw.add_sig(seq["path"], seq["offset"], seq["width"])
 1405| 
 1406|         for init in inits:
 1407|             init["sig"] = yw.add_sig(init["path"], init["offset"], init["width"], True)
 1408| 
 1409|         inits = seqs + inits
 1410| 
 1411|         mem_dict = {tuple(mem["smtpath"]): mem for mem in mems}
 1412|         mem_init_values = []
 1413| 
 1414|         for path, addr, value in mem_trace_data.get(0, ()):
 1415|             json_mem = mem_dict.get(tuple(path))
 1416|             if not json_mem:
 1417|                 continue
 1418| 
 1419|             bit_addr = addr * json_mem["width"]
 1420|             uninit_chunks = [(chunk["width"] + chunk["offset"], chunk["offset"]) for chunk in json_mem["uninitialized"]]
 1421|             first_chunk_nr = bisect.bisect_left(uninit_chunks, (bit_addr + 1,))
 1422| 
 1423|             for uninit_end, uninit_offset in uninit_chunks[first_chunk_nr:]:
 1424|                 assert uninit_end > bit_addr
 1425|                 if uninit_offset > bit_addr + json_mem["width"]:
 1426|                     break
 1427| 
 1428|                 word_path = (*json_mem["path"], f"\\[{addr}]")
 1429| 
 1430|                 overlap_start = max(uninit_offset - bit_addr, 0)
 1431|                 overlap_end = min(uninit_end - bit_addr, json_mem["width"])
 1432|                 overlap_bits = value[len(value)-overlap_end:len(value)-overlap_start]
 1433| 
 1434|                 sig = yw.add_sig(word_path, overlap_start, overlap_end - overlap_start, True)
 1435|                 mem_init_values.append((sig, overlap_bits.replace("x", "?")))
 1436| 
 1437|         exprs = []
 1438|         all_sigs = []
 1439| 
 1440|         for i, k in enumerate(steps):
 1441|             step_values = WitnessValues()
 1442| 
 1443|             if not i:
 1444|                 for sig, value in mem_init_values:
 1445|                     step_values[sig] = value
 1446|                 sigs = inits + seqs
 1447|             else:
 1448|                 sigs = seqs
 1449| 
 1450|             exprs.extend(smt.witness_net_expr(topmod, f"s{k}", sig) for sig in sigs)
 1451| 
 1452|             all_sigs.append((step_values, sigs))
 1453| 
 1454|         bvs = iter(smt.get_list(exprs))
 1455| 
 1456|         for (step_values, sigs) in all_sigs:
 1457|             for sig in sigs:
 1458|                 value = smt.bv2bin(next(bvs))
 1459|                 step_values[sig["sig"]] = value
 1460|             yw.step(step_values)
 1461| 
 1462|         yw.end_trace()
 1463| 
 1464| 
 1465| def write_trace(steps_start, steps_stop, index, allregs=False):
 1466|     if steps_stop is None:
 1467|         steps = steps_start
 1468|         seq_time = True
 1469|     else:
 1470|         steps = list(range(steps_start, steps_stop))
 1471|         seq_time = False
 1472| 
 1473|     if vcdfile is not None:
 1474|         write_vcd_trace(steps, index, seq_time=seq_time)
 1475| 
 1476|     if vlogtbfile is not None:
 1477|         write_vlogtb_trace(steps, index)
 1478| 
 1479|     if outconstr is not None:
 1480|         write_constr_trace(steps, index)
 1481| 
 1482|     if outywfile is not None:
 1483|         write_yw_trace(steps, index, allregs)
 1484| 
 1485| def escape_path_segment(segment):
 1486|     if "." in segment:
 1487|         return f"\\{segment} "
 1488|     return segment
 1489| 
 1490| def print_failed_asserts_worker(mod, state, path, extrainfo, infomap, infokey=()):
 1491|     assert mod in smt.modinfo
 1492|     found_failed_assert = False
 1493| 
 1494|     if smt.get("(|%s_a| %s)" % (mod, state)) in ["true", "#b1"]:
 1495|         return
 1496| 
 1497|     for cellname, celltype in smt.modinfo[mod].cells.items():
 1498|         cell_infokey = (mod, cellname, infokey)
 1499|         cell_path = path + "." + escape_path_segment(cellname)
 1500|         if print_failed_asserts_worker(celltype, "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, cellname, state), cell_path, extrainfo, infomap, cell_infokey):
 1501|             found_failed_assert = True
 1502| 
 1503|     for assertfun, assertinfo in smt.modinfo[mod].asserts.items():
 1504|         if smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (assertfun, state)) in ["false", "#b0"]:
 1505|             assert_key = (assertfun, infokey)
 1506|             print_msg("Assert failed in %s: %s%s%s" % (path, assertinfo, extrainfo, infomap.get(assert_key, '')))
 1507|             found_failed_assert = True
 1508| 
 1509|     return found_failed_assert
 1510| 
 1511| 
 1512| def print_failed_asserts(state, final=False, extrainfo="", infomap={}):
 1513|     if noinfo: return
 1514|     loc_list, expr_list, value_list = get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, state, final=final, getvalues=True)
 1515|     found_failed_assert = False
 1516| 
 1517|     for loc, expr, value in zip(loc_list, expr_list, value_list):
 1518|         if smt.bv2int(value) == 0:
 1519|             print_msg("Assert %s failed: %s%s%s" % (loc, expr, extrainfo, infomap.get(loc, '')))
 1520|             found_failed_assert = True
 1521| 
 1522|     if not final:
 1523|         if print_failed_asserts_worker(topmod, "s%d" % state, topmod, extrainfo, infomap):
 1524|             found_failed_assert = True
 1525| 
 1526|     return found_failed_assert
 1527| 
 1528| 
 1529| def print_anyconsts_worker(mod, state, path):
 1530|     assert mod in smt.modinfo
 1531| 
 1532|     for cellname, celltype in smt.modinfo[mod].cells.items():
 1533|         print_anyconsts_worker(celltype, "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, cellname, state), path + "." + escape_path_segment(cellname))
 1534| 
 1535|     for fun, info in smt.modinfo[mod].anyconsts.items():
 1536|         if info[1] is None:
 1537|             if not binarymode:
 1538|                 print_msg("Value for anyconst in %s (%s): %d" % (path, info[0], smt.bv2int(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (fun, state)))))
 1539|             else:
 1540|                 print_msg("Value for anyconst in %s (%s): %s" % (path, info[0], smt.bv2bin(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (fun, state)))))
 1541|         else:
 1542|             if not binarymode:
 1543|                 print_msg("Value for anyconst %s.%s (%s): %d" % (path, info[1], info[0], smt.bv2int(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (fun, state)))))
 1544|             else:
 1545|                 print_msg("Value for anyconst %s.%s (%s): %s" % (path, info[1], info[0], smt.bv2bin(smt.get("(|%s| %s)" % (fun, state)))))
 1546| 
 1547| 
 1548| def print_anyconsts(state):
 1549|     if noinfo: return
 1550|     print_anyconsts_worker(topmod, "s%d" % state, topmod)
 1551| 
 1552| 
 1553| def get_cover_list(mod, base, path=None):
 1554|     path = path or mod
 1555|     assert mod in smt.modinfo
 1556| 
 1557|     cover_expr = list()
 1558|     # A tuple of path and cell name
 1559|     cover_desc = list()
 1560| 
 1561|     for expr, desc in smt.modinfo[mod].covers.items():
 1562|         cover_expr.append("(ite (|%s| %s) #b1 #b0)" % (expr, base))
 1563|         cover_desc.append((path, desc))
 1564| 
 1565|     for cell, submod in smt.modinfo[mod].cells.items():
 1566|         cell_path = path + "." + escape_path_segment(cell)
 1567|         e, d = get_cover_list(submod, "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, cell, base), cell_path)
 1568|         cover_expr += e
 1569|         cover_desc += d
 1570| 
 1571|     return cover_expr, cover_desc
 1572| 
 1573| 
 1574| def get_assert_map(mod, base, path, key_base=()):
 1575|     assert mod in smt.modinfo
 1576| 
 1577|     assert_map = dict()
 1578| 
 1579|     for expr, desc in smt.modinfo[mod].asserts.items():
 1580|         assert_map[(expr, key_base)] = ("(|%s| %s)" % (expr, base), path, desc)
 1581| 
 1582|     for cell, submod in smt.modinfo[mod].cells.items():
 1583|         cell_path = path + "." + escape_path_segment(cell)
 1584|         assert_map.update(get_assert_map(submod, "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, cell, base), cell_path, (mod, cell, key_base)))
 1585| 
 1586|     return assert_map
 1587| 
 1588| 
 1589| def get_assert_keys():
 1590|     keys = set()
 1591|     keys.update(get_assert_map(topmod, 'state', topmod).keys())
 1592|     for step_constr_asserts in constr_asserts.values():
 1593|         keys.update(loc for loc, expr in step_constr_asserts)
 1594| 
 1595|     return keys
 1596| 
 1597| 
 1598| def get_active_assert_map(step, active):
 1599|     assert_map = dict()
 1600|     for key, assert_data in get_assert_map(topmod, "s%s" % step, topmod).items():
 1601|         if key in active:
 1602|             assert_map[key] = assert_data
 1603| 
 1604|     for loc, expr, actual_expr in zip(*get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, step, individual=True)):
 1605|         if loc in active:
 1606|             assert_map[loc] = (actual_expr, None, (expr, loc))
 1607| 
 1608|     return assert_map
 1609| 
 1610| assume_enables = {}
 1611| 
 1612| def declare_assume_enables():
 1613|     def recurse(mod, path, key_base=()):
 1614|         for expr, desc in smt.modinfo[mod].assumes.items():
 1615|             enable = f"|assume_enable {len(assume_enables)}|"
 1616|             smt.smt2_assumptions[(expr, key_base)] = enable
 1617|             smt.write(f"(declare-const {enable} Bool)")
 1618|             assume_enables[(expr, key_base)] = (enable, path, desc)
 1619| 
 1620|         for cell, submod in smt.modinfo[mod].cells.items():
 1621|             recurse(submod, f"{path}.{cell}", (mod, cell, key_base))
 1622| 
 1623|     recurse(topmod, topmod)
 1624| 
 1625| if track_assumes:
 1626|     declare_assume_enables()
 1627| 
 1628| def smt_assert_design_assumes(step):
 1629|     if not track_assumes:
 1630|         smt_assert_consequent("(|%s_u| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 1631|         return
 1632| 
 1633|     if not assume_enables:
 1634|         return
 1635| 
 1636|     def expr_for_assume(assume_key, base=None):
 1637|         expr, key_base = assume_key
 1638|         expr_prefix = f"(|{expr}| "
 1639|         expr_suffix = ")"
 1640|         while key_base:
 1641|             mod, cell, key_base = key_base
 1642|             expr_prefix += f"(|{mod}_h {cell}| "
 1643|             expr_suffix += ")"
 1644|         return f"{expr_prefix} s{step}{expr_suffix}"
 1645| 
 1646|     for assume_key, (enable, path, desc) in assume_enables.items():
 1647|         smt_assert_consequent(f"(=> {enable} {expr_for_assume(assume_key)})")
 1648| 
 1649| states = list()
 1650| asserts_antecedent_cache = [list()]
 1651| asserts_consequent_cache = [list()]
 1652| asserts_cache_dirty = False
 1653| 
 1654| def smt_state(step):
 1655|     smt.write("(declare-fun s%d () |%s_s|)" % (step, topmod))
 1656|     states.append("s%d" % step)
 1657| 
 1658| def smt_assert(expr):
 1659|     if expr == "true":
 1660|         return
 1661| 
 1662|     smt.write("(assert %s)" % expr)
 1663| 
 1664| def smt_assert_antecedent(expr):
 1665|     if expr == "true":
 1666|         return
 1667| 
 1668|     smt.write("(assert %s)" % expr)
 1669| 
 1670|     global asserts_cache_dirty
 1671|     asserts_cache_dirty = True
 1672|     asserts_antecedent_cache[-1].append(expr)
 1673| 
 1674| def smt_assert_consequent(expr):
 1675|     if expr == "true":
 1676|         return
 1677| 
 1678|     smt.write("(assert %s)" % expr)
 1679| 
 1680|     global asserts_cache_dirty
 1681|     asserts_cache_dirty = True
 1682|     asserts_consequent_cache[-1].append(expr)
 1683| 
 1684| def smt_forall_assert():
 1685|     if not smt.forall:
 1686|         return
 1687| 
 1688|     global asserts_cache_dirty
 1689|     asserts_cache_dirty = False
 1690| 
 1691|     assert (len(smt.modinfo[topmod].maximize) + len(smt.modinfo[topmod].minimize) <= 1)
 1692| 
 1693|     def make_assert_expr(asserts_cache):
 1694|         expr = list()
 1695|         for lst in asserts_cache:
 1696|             expr += lst
 1697| 
 1698|         assert len(expr) != 0
 1699| 
 1700|         if len(expr) == 1:
 1701|             expr = expr[0]
 1702|         else:
 1703|             expr = "(and %s)" % (" ".join(expr))
 1704|         return expr
 1705| 
 1706|     antecedent_expr = make_assert_expr(asserts_antecedent_cache)
 1707|     consequent_expr = make_assert_expr(asserts_consequent_cache)
 1708| 
 1709|     states_db = set(states)
 1710|     used_states_db = set()
 1711|     new_antecedent_expr = list()
 1712|     new_consequent_expr = list()
 1713|     assert_expr = list()
 1714| 
 1715|     def make_new_expr(new_expr, expr):
 1716|         cursor = 0
 1717|         while cursor < len(expr):
 1718|             l = 1
 1719|             if expr[cursor] in '|"':
 1720|                 while cursor+l+1 < len(expr) and expr[cursor] != expr[cursor+l]:
 1721|                     l += 1
 1722|                 l += 1
 1723|             elif expr[cursor] not in '() ':
 1724|                 while cursor+l < len(expr) and expr[cursor+l] not in '|"() ':
 1725|                     l += 1
 1726| 
 1727|             word = expr[cursor:cursor+l]
 1728|             if word in states_db:
 1729|                 used_states_db.add(word)
 1730|                 word += "_"
 1731| 
 1732|             new_expr.append(word)
 1733|             cursor += l
 1734| 
 1735|     make_new_expr(new_antecedent_expr, antecedent_expr)
 1736|     make_new_expr(new_consequent_expr, consequent_expr)
 1737| 
 1738|     new_antecedent_expr = ["".join(new_antecedent_expr)]
 1739|     new_consequent_expr = ["".join(new_consequent_expr)]
 1740| 
 1741|     if states[0] in used_states_db:
 1742|         new_antecedent_expr.append("(|%s_ex_state_eq| %s %s_)" % (topmod, states[0], states[0]))
 1743|     for s in states:
 1744|         if s in used_states_db:
 1745|             new_antecedent_expr.append("(|%s_ex_input_eq| %s %s_)" % (topmod, s, s))
 1746| 
 1747|     if len(new_antecedent_expr) == 0:
 1748|         new_antecedent_expr = "true"
 1749|     elif len(new_antecedent_expr) == 1:
 1750|         new_antecedent_expr = new_antecedent_expr[0]
 1751|     else:
 1752|         new_antecedent_expr = "(and %s)" % (" ".join(new_antecedent_expr))
 1753| 
 1754|     if len(new_consequent_expr) == 0:
 1755|         new_consequent_expr = "true"
 1756|     elif len(new_consequent_expr) == 1:
 1757|         new_consequent_expr = new_consequent_expr[0]
 1758|     else:
 1759|         new_consequent_expr = "(and %s)" % (" ".join(new_consequent_expr))
 1760| 
 1761|     assert_expr.append("(assert (forall (")
 1762|     first_state = True
 1763|     for s in states:
 1764|         if s in used_states_db:
 1765|             assert_expr.append("%s(%s_ |%s_s|)" % ("" if first_state else " ", s, topmod))
 1766|             first_state = False
 1767|     assert_expr.append(") (=> %s %s)))" % (new_antecedent_expr, new_consequent_expr))
 1768| 
 1769|     smt.write("".join(assert_expr))
 1770| 
 1771|     if len(smt.modinfo[topmod].maximize) > 0:
 1772|         for s in states:
 1773|             if s in used_states_db:
 1774|                 smt.write("(maximize (|%s| %s))\n" % (smt.modinfo[topmod].maximize.copy().pop(), s))
 1775|                 break
 1776| 
 1777|     if len(smt.modinfo[topmod].minimize) > 0:
 1778|         for s in states:
 1779|             if s in used_states_db:
 1780|                 smt.write("(minimize (|%s| %s))\n" % (smt.modinfo[topmod].minimize.copy().pop(), s))
 1781|                 break
 1782| 
 1783| def smt_push():
 1784|     global asserts_cache_dirty
 1785|     asserts_cache_dirty = True
 1786|     asserts_antecedent_cache.append(list())
 1787|     asserts_consequent_cache.append(list())
 1788|     smt.write("(push 1)")
 1789| 
 1790| def smt_pop():
 1791|     global asserts_cache_dirty
 1792|     asserts_cache_dirty = True
 1793|     asserts_antecedent_cache.pop()
 1794|     asserts_consequent_cache.pop()
 1795|     smt.write("(pop 1)")
 1796| 
 1797| def smt_check_sat(expected=["sat", "unsat"]):
 1798|     if asserts_cache_dirty:
 1799|         smt_forall_assert()
 1800|     return smt.check_sat(expected=expected)
 1801| 
 1802| def report_tracked_assumptions(msg):
 1803|     if track_assumes:
 1804|         print_msg(msg)
 1805|         for key in smt.get_unsat_assumptions(minimize=minimize_assumes):
 1806|             enable, path, descr = assume_enables[key]
 1807|             print_msg(f"  In {path}: {descr}")
 1808| 
 1809| 
 1810| if incremental:
 1811|     incremental.mainloop()
 1812| 
 1813| elif tempind:
 1814|     retstatus = "FAILED"
 1815|     skip_counter = step_size
 1816|     for step in range(num_steps, -1, -1):
 1817|         if smt.forall:
 1818|             print_msg("Temporal induction not supported for exists-forall problems.")
 1819|             break
 1820| 
 1821|         smt_state(step)
 1822|         smt_assert_design_assumes(step)
 1823|         smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 1824|         smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step))
 1825|         smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, step))
 1826| 
 1827|         if step == num_steps:
 1828|             smt_assert("(not (and (|%s_a| s%d) %s))" % (topmod, step, get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, step)))
 1829| 
 1830|         else:
 1831|             smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, step, step+1))
 1832|             smt_assert("(|%s_a| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 1833|             smt_assert(get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, step))
 1834| 
 1835|         if step > num_steps-skip_steps:
 1836|             print_msg("Skipping induction in step %d.." % (step))
 1837|             continue
 1838| 
 1839|         skip_counter += 1
 1840|         if skip_counter < step_size:
 1841|             print_msg("Skipping induction in step %d.." % (step))
 1842|             continue
 1843| 
 1844|         skip_counter = 0
 1845|         print_msg("Trying induction in step %d.." % (step))
 1846| 
 1847|         if smt_check_sat() == "sat":
 1848|             if step == 0:
 1849|                 print_msg("Temporal induction failed!")
 1850|                 print_anyconsts(num_steps)
 1851|                 print_failed_asserts(num_steps)
 1852|                 write_trace(step, num_steps+1, '%', allregs=True)
 1853|                 if detect_loops:
 1854|                     loop = detect_state_loop(step, num_steps+1)
 1855|                     if loop:
 1856|                         print_msg(f"Loop detected, increasing induction depth will not help. Step {loop[0]} = step {loop[1]}")
 1857| 
 1858|             elif dumpall:
 1859|                 print_anyconsts(num_steps)
 1860|                 print_failed_asserts(num_steps)
 1861|                 write_trace(step, num_steps+1, "%d" % step, allregs=True)
 1862| 
 1863|         else:
 1864|             print_msg("Temporal induction successful.")
 1865|             report_tracked_assumptions("Used assumptions:")
 1866|             retstatus = "PASSED"
 1867|             break
 1868| 
 1869| elif covermode:
 1870|     cover_expr, cover_desc = get_cover_list(topmod, "state")
 1871|     cover_mask = "1" * len(cover_desc)
 1872| 
 1873|     if len(cover_expr) > 1:
 1874|         cover_expr = "(concat %s)" % " ".join(cover_expr)
 1875|     elif len(cover_expr) == 1:
 1876|         cover_expr = cover_expr[0]
 1877|     else:
 1878|         cover_expr = "#b0"
 1879| 
 1880|     coveridx = 0
 1881|     smt.write("(define-fun covers_0 ((state |%s_s|)) (_ BitVec %d) %s)" % (topmod, len(cover_desc), cover_expr))
 1882| 
 1883|     step = 0
 1884|     retstatus = "FAILED"
 1885|     found_failed_assert = False
 1886| 
 1887|     assert step_size == 1
 1888| 
 1889|     while step < num_steps:
 1890|         smt_state(step)
 1891|         smt_assert_design_assumes(step)
 1892|         smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 1893|         smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, step))
 1894| 
 1895|         if step == 0:
 1896|             if noinit:
 1897|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step))
 1898|             else:
 1899|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_i| s0)" % (topmod))
 1900|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_is| s0)" % (topmod))
 1901| 
 1902|         else:
 1903|             smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, step-1, step))
 1904|             smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step))
 1905| 
 1906|         if step < skip_steps:
 1907|             print_msg("Skipping step %d.." % (step))
 1908|             step += 1
 1909|             continue
 1910| 
 1911|         while "1" in cover_mask:
 1912|             print_msg("Checking cover reachability in step %d.." % (step))
 1913|             smt_push()
 1914|             smt_assert("(distinct (covers_%d s%d) #b%s)" % (coveridx, step, "0" * len(cover_desc)))
 1915| 
 1916|             if smt_check_sat() == "unsat":
 1917|                 report_tracked_assumptions("Used assumptions:")
 1918|                 smt_pop()
 1919|                 break
 1920| 
 1921|             if append_steps > 0:
 1922|                 for i in range(step+1, step+1+append_steps):
 1923|                     print_msg("Appending additional step %d." % i)
 1924|                     smt_state(i)
 1925|                     smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, i))
 1926|                     smt_assert_design_assumes(i)
 1927|                     smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, i))
 1928|                     smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, i-1, i))
 1929|                     smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, i))
 1930|                 print_msg("Re-solving with appended steps..")
 1931|                 if smt_check_sat() == "unsat":
 1932|                     print("%s Cannot appended steps without violating assumptions!" % smt.timestamp())
 1933|                     report_tracked_assumptions("Conflicting assumptions:")
 1934|                     found_failed_assert = True
 1935|                     retstatus = "FAILED"
 1936|                     break
 1937| 
 1938|             reached_covers = smt.bv2bin(smt.get("(covers_%d s%d)" % (coveridx, step)))
 1939|             assert len(reached_covers) == len(cover_desc)
 1940| 
 1941|             new_cover_mask = []
 1942| 
 1943|             for i in range(len(reached_covers)):
 1944|                 if reached_covers[i] == "0":
 1945|                     new_cover_mask.append(cover_mask[i])
 1946|                     continue
 1947| 
 1948|                 path = cover_desc[i][0]
 1949|                 name = cover_desc[i][1]
 1950|                 print_msg("Reached cover statement in step %d at %s: %s" % (step, path, name))
 1951|                 new_cover_mask.append("0")
 1952| 
 1953|             cover_mask = "".join(new_cover_mask)
 1954| 
 1955|             for i in range(step+1+append_steps):
 1956|                 if print_failed_asserts(i, extrainfo=" (step %d)" % i):
 1957|                     found_failed_assert = True
 1958| 
 1959|             write_trace(0, step+1+append_steps, "%d" % coveridx)
 1960| 
 1961|             if found_failed_assert:
 1962|                 break
 1963| 
 1964|             coveridx += 1
 1965|             smt_pop()
 1966|             smt.write("(define-fun covers_%d ((state |%s_s|)) (_ BitVec %d) (bvand (covers_%d state) #b%s))" % (coveridx, topmod, len(cover_desc), coveridx-1, cover_mask))
 1967| 
 1968|         if found_failed_assert and not keep_going:
 1969|             break
 1970| 
 1971|         if "1" not in cover_mask:
 1972|             retstatus = "PASSED"
 1973|             break
 1974| 
 1975|         step += 1
 1976| 
 1977|     if "1" in cover_mask:
 1978|         for i in range(len(cover_mask)):
 1979|             if cover_mask[i] == "1":
 1980|                 print_msg("Unreached cover statement at %s: %s" % (cover_desc[i][0], cover_desc[i][1]))
 1981| 
 1982| else:  # not tempind, covermode
 1983|     active_assert_keys = get_assert_keys()
 1984|     failed_assert_infomap = dict()
 1985|     traceidx = 0
 1986| 
 1987|     step = 0
 1988|     retstatus = "PASSED"
 1989|     while step < num_steps:
 1990|         smt_state(step)
 1991|         smt_assert_design_assumes(step)
 1992|         smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 1993|         smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, step))
 1994| 
 1995|         if step == 0:
 1996|             if noinit:
 1997|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step))
 1998|             else:
 1999|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_i| s0)" % (topmod))
 2000|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_is| s0)" % (topmod))
 2001| 
 2002|         else:
 2003|             smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, step-1, step))
 2004|             smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step))
 2005| 
 2006|         if step < skip_steps:
 2007|             if assume_skipped is not None and step >= assume_skipped:
 2008|                 print_msg("Skipping step %d (and assuming pass).." % (step))
 2009|                 smt_assert("(|%s_a| s%d)" % (topmod, step))
 2010|                 smt_assert(get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, step))
 2011|             else:
 2012|                 print_msg("Skipping step %d.." % (step))
 2013|             step += 1
 2014|             continue
 2015| 
 2016|         last_check_step = step
 2017|         for i in range(1, step_size):
 2018|             if step+i < num_steps:
 2019|                 smt_state(step+i)
 2020|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, step+i))
 2021|                 smt_assert_design_assumes(step + i)
 2022|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, step+i))
 2023|                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, step+i-1, step+i))
 2024|                 smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, step+i))
 2025|                 last_check_step = step+i
 2026| 
 2027|         if not gentrace:
 2028|             if presat:
 2029|                 if last_check_step == step:
 2030|                     print_msg("Checking assumptions in step %d.." % (step))
 2031|                 else:
 2032|                     print_msg("Checking assumptions in steps %d to %d.." % (step, last_check_step))
 2033| 
 2034|                 if smt_check_sat() == "unsat":
 2035|                     print_msg("Assumptions are unsatisfiable!")
 2036|                     report_tracked_assumptions("Conficting assumptions:")
 2037|                     retstatus = "PREUNSAT"
 2038|                     break
 2039| 
 2040|             if not final_only:
 2041|                 recheck_current_step = True
 2042|                 while recheck_current_step:
 2043|                     recheck_current_step = False
 2044|                     if last_check_step == step:
 2045|                         print_msg("Checking assertions in step %d.." % (step))
 2046|                     else:
 2047|                         print_msg("Checking assertions in steps %d to %d.." % (step, last_check_step))
 2048|                     smt_push()
 2049| 
 2050|                     active_assert_maps = dict()
 2051|                     active_assert_exprs = list()
 2052|                     for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2053|                         assert_expr_map = get_active_assert_map(i, active_assert_keys)
 2054|                         active_assert_maps[i] = assert_expr_map
 2055|                         active_assert_exprs.extend(assert_data[0] for assert_data in assert_expr_map.values())
 2056| 
 2057|                     if active_assert_exprs:
 2058|                         if len(active_assert_exprs) == 1:
 2059|                             active_assert_expr = active_assert_exprs[0]
 2060|                         else:
 2061|                             active_assert_expr = "(and %s)" % " ".join(active_assert_exprs)
 2062| 
 2063|                         smt_assert("(not %s)" % active_assert_expr)
 2064|                     else:
 2065|                         active_assert_expr = "true"
 2066|                         smt_assert("false")
 2067| 
 2068| 
 2069|                     if smt_check_sat() == "sat":
 2070|                         if retstatus != "FAILED":
 2071|                             print("%s BMC failed!" % smt.timestamp())
 2072| 
 2073|                         if check_witness:
 2074|                             print_msg("Checking witness constraints...")
 2075|                             smt_pop()
 2076|                             smt_push()
 2077|                             smt_assert(active_assert_expr)
 2078|                             if smt_check_sat() != "sat":
 2079|                                 retstatus = "PASSED"
 2080|                                 check_witness = False
 2081|                                 num_steps = -1
 2082|                                 break
 2083| 
 2084|                         if append_steps > 0:
 2085|                             for i in range(last_check_step+1, last_check_step+1+append_steps):
 2086|                                 print_msg("Appending additional step %d." % i)
 2087|                                 smt_state(i)
 2088|                                 smt_assert_antecedent("(not (|%s_is| s%d))" % (topmod, i))
 2089|                                 smt_assert_design_assumes(i)
 2090|                                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_h| s%d)" % (topmod, i))
 2091|                                 smt_assert_antecedent("(|%s_t| s%d s%d)" % (topmod, i-1, i))
 2092|                                 smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, i))
 2093|                             print_msg("Re-solving with appended steps..")
 2094|                             if smt_check_sat() == "unsat":
 2095|                                 print_msg("Cannot append steps without violating assumptions!")
 2096|                                 report_tracked_assumptions("Conflicting assumptions:")
 2097|                                 retstatus = "FAILED"
 2098|                                 break
 2099|                         print_anyconsts(step)
 2100| 
 2101|                         for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2102|                             print_failed_asserts(i, infomap=failed_assert_infomap)
 2103| 
 2104|                         if keep_going:
 2105|                             for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2106|                                 for key, (expr, path, desc) in active_assert_maps[i].items():
 2107|                                     if key in active_assert_keys and not smt.bv2int(smt.get(expr)):
 2108|                                         failed_assert_infomap[key] = " [failed before]"
 2109| 
 2110|                                         active_assert_keys.remove(key)
 2111| 
 2112|                             if active_assert_keys:
 2113|                                 recheck_current_step = True
 2114| 
 2115|                         write_trace(0, last_check_step+1+append_steps, "%d" % traceidx if keep_going else '%')
 2116|                         traceidx += 1
 2117|                         retstatus = "FAILED"
 2118| 
 2119|                     smt_pop()
 2120|                     if recheck_current_step:
 2121|                         print_msg("Checking remaining assertions..")
 2122| 
 2123|                 if retstatus == "FAILED" and not (keep_going and active_assert_keys):
 2124|                     break
 2125| 
 2126|             if (constr_final_start is not None) or (last_check_step+1 != num_steps):
 2127|                 for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2128|                     assert_expr_map = get_active_assert_map(i, active_assert_keys)
 2129|                     for assert_data in assert_expr_map.values():
 2130|                         smt_assert(assert_data[0])
 2131| 
 2132|             if constr_final_start is not None:
 2133|                 for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2134|                     if i < constr_final_start:
 2135|                         continue
 2136| 
 2137|                     print_msg("Checking final constraints in step %d.." % (i))
 2138|                     smt_push()
 2139| 
 2140|                     smt_assert_consequent(get_constr_expr(constr_assumes, i, final=True))
 2141|                     smt_assert("(not %s)" % get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, i, final=True))
 2142| 
 2143|                     if smt_check_sat() == "sat":
 2144|                         print("%s BMC failed!" % smt.timestamp())
 2145|                         print_anyconsts(i)
 2146|                         print_failed_asserts(i, final=True)
 2147|                         write_trace(0, i+1, '%')
 2148|                         retstatus = "FAILED"
 2149|                         break
 2150| 
 2151|                     smt_pop()
 2152|                 if retstatus == "FAILED" or retstatus == "PREUNSAT":
 2153|                     break
 2154| 
 2155|         else:  # gentrace
 2156|             for i in range(step, last_check_step+1):
 2157|                 smt_assert("(|%s_a| s%d)" % (topmod, i))
 2158|                 smt_assert(get_constr_expr(constr_asserts, i))
 2159| 
 2160|             print_msg("Solving for step %d.." % (last_check_step))
 2161|             status = smt_check_sat()
 2162|             if status != "sat":
 2163|                 print("%s No solution found! (%s)" % (smt.timestamp(), status))
 2164|                 retstatus = "FAILED"
 2165|                 break
 2166| 
 2167|             elif dumpall:
 2168|                 print_anyconsts(0)
 2169|                 write_trace(0, last_check_step+1, "%d" % step)
 2170| 
 2171|         step += step_size
 2172| 
 2173|     if gentrace and retstatus == "PASSED":
 2174|         print_anyconsts(0)
 2175|         write_trace(0, num_steps, '%')
 2176| 
 2177|     if check_witness:
 2178|         retstatus = "FAILED"
 2179| 
 2180| smt.write("(exit)")
 2181| smt.wait()
 2182| 
 2183| if not incremental:
 2184|     print_msg("Status: %s" % retstatus)
 2185|     sys.exit(0 if retstatus == "PASSED" else 1)

   29| 
   30| # This is needed so that the recursive SMT2 S-expression parser
   31| # does not run out of stack frames when parsing large expressions
   32| if os.name == "posix":
   33|     smtio_reclimit = 64 * 1024
   34|     if sys.getrecursionlimit() < smtio_reclimit:
   35|         sys.setrecursionlimit(smtio_reclimit)
   36| 
   37|     current_rlimit_stack = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_STACK)
   38|     if current_rlimit_stack[0] != resource.RLIM_INFINITY:
   39|         smtio_stacksize = 128 * 1024 * 1024
   40|         if os.uname().sysname == "Darwin":
   41|             # MacOS has rather conservative stack limits
   42|             smtio_stacksize = 8 * 1024 * 1024
   43|         if current_rlimit_stack[1] != resource.RLIM_INFINITY:
   44|             smtio_stacksize = min(smtio_stacksize, current_rlimit_stack[1])
   45|         if current_rlimit_stack[0] < smtio_stacksize:
   46|             try:
   47|                 resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_STACK, (smtio_stacksize, current_rlimit_stack[1]))
   48|             except ValueError:
   49|                 # couldn't get more stack, just run with what we have
   50|                 pass
   51| 
   52| 
   53| # currently running solvers (so we can kill them)
   54| running_solvers = dict()
   55| forced_shutdown = False
   56| solvers_index = 0
   57| 
   58| def force_shutdown(signum, frame):
   59|     global forced_shutdown
   60|     if not forced_shutdown:
   61|         forced_shutdown = True
   62|         if signum is not None:
   63|             print("<%s>" % signal.Signals(signum).name)
   64|         for p in running_solvers.values():
   65|             # os.killpg(os.getpgid(p.pid), signal.SIGTERM)
   66|             os.kill(p.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
   67|     sys.exit(1)
   68| 
   69| if os.name == "posix":
   70|     signal.signal(signal.SIGHUP, force_shutdown)
   71| signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, force_shutdown)
   72| signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, force_shutdown)
   73| 
   74| def except_hook(exctype, value, traceback):
   75|     if not forced_shutdown:
   76|         sys.__excepthook__(exctype, value, traceback)
   77|     force_shutdown(None, None)
   78| 
   79| sys.excepthook = except_hook
   80| 
   81| 
   82| def recursion_helper(iteration, *request):
   83|     stack = [iteration(*request)]
   84| 
   85|     while stack:
   86|         top = stack.pop()
   87|         try:
   88|             request = next(top)
   89|         except StopIteration:
   90|             continue
   91| 
   92|         stack.append(top)
   93|         stack.append(iteration(*request))
   94| 
   95| 
   96| hex_dict = {
   97|     "0": "0000", "1": "0001", "2": "0010", "3": "0011",
   98|     "4": "0100", "5": "0101", "6": "0110", "7": "0111",
   99|     "8": "1000", "9": "1001", "A": "1010", "B": "1011",
  100|     "C": "1100", "D": "1101", "E": "1110", "F": "1111",
  101|     "a": "1010", "b": "1011", "c": "1100", "d": "1101",
  102|     "e": "1110", "f": "1111"
  103| }
