  129| class SmtIo:
  130|     def __init__(self, opts=None):
  131|         global solvers_index
  132| 
  133|         self.logic = None
  134|         self.logic_qf = True
  135|         self.logic_ax = True
  136|         self.logic_uf = True
  137|         self.logic_bv = True
  138|         self.logic_dt = False
  139|         self.forall = False
  140|         self.timeout = 0
  141|         self.produce_models = True
  142|         self.recheck = False
  143|         self.smt2cache = [list()]
  144|         self.smt2_options = dict()
  145|         self.smt2_assumptions = dict()
  146|         self.p = None
  147|         self.p_index = solvers_index
  148|         solvers_index += 1
  149| 
  150|         if opts is not None:
  151|             self.logic = opts.logic
  152|             self.solver = opts.solver
  153|             self.solver_opts = opts.solver_opts
  154|             self.debug_print = opts.debug_print
  155|             self.debug_file = opts.debug_file
  156|             self.dummy_file = opts.dummy_file
  157|             self.timeinfo = opts.timeinfo
  158|             self.timeout = opts.timeout
  159|             self.unroll = opts.unroll
  160|             self.noincr = opts.noincr
  161|             self.info_stmts = opts.info_stmts
  162|             self.nocomments = opts.nocomments
  163|             self.smt2_options.update(opts.smt2_options)
  164| 
  165|         else:
  166|             self.solver = "yices"
  167|             self.solver_opts = list()
  168|             self.debug_print = False
  169|             self.debug_file = None
  170|             self.dummy_file = None
  171|             self.timeinfo = os.name != "nt"
  172|             self.timeout = 0
  173|             self.unroll = False
  174|             self.noincr = False
  175|             self.info_stmts = list()
  176|             self.nocomments = False
  177| 
  178|         self.start_time = time()
  179| 
  180|         self.modinfo = dict()
  181|         self.curmod = None
  182|         self.topmod = None
  183|         self.setup_done = False
  184| 
  185|     def __del__(self):
  186|         if self.p is not None and not forced_shutdown:
  187|             os.killpg(os.getpgid(self.p.pid), signal.SIGTERM)
  188|             if running_solvers is not None:
  189|                 del running_solvers[self.p_index]
  190| 
  191|     def setup(self):
  192|         assert not self.setup_done
  193| 
  194|         if self.forall:
  195|             self.unroll = False
  196| 
  197|         if self.solver == "yices":
  198|             if self.forall:
  199|                 self.noincr = True
  200| 
  201|             if self.noincr:
  202|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yices-smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  203|             else:
  204|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yices-smt2', '--incremental'] + self.solver_opts
  205|             if self.timeout != 0:
  206|                 self.popen_vargs.append('-t')
  207|                 self.popen_vargs.append('%d' % self.timeout);
  208| 
  209|         if self.solver == "z3":
  210|             self.popen_vargs = ['z3', '-smt2', '-in'] + self.solver_opts
  211|             if self.timeout != 0:
  212|                 self.popen_vargs.append('-T:%d' % self.timeout);
  213| 
  214|         if self.solver in ["cvc4", "cvc5"]:
  215|             self.recheck = True
  216|             if self.noincr:
  217|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--lang', 'smt2.6' if self.logic_dt else 'smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  218|             else:
  219|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--incremental', '--lang', 'smt2.6' if self.logic_dt else 'smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  220|             if self.timeout != 0:
  221|                 self.popen_vargs.append('--tlimit=%d000' % self.timeout);
  222| 
  223|         if self.solver == "mathsat":
  224|             self.popen_vargs = ['mathsat'] + self.solver_opts
  225|             if self.timeout != 0:
  226|                 print('timeout option is not supported for mathsat.')
  227|                 sys.exit(1)
  228| 
  229|         if self.solver in ["boolector", "bitwuzla"]:
  230|             if self.noincr:
  231|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  232|             else:
  233|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--smt2', '-i'] + self.solver_opts
  234|             self.unroll = True
  235|             if self.timeout != 0:
  236|                 print('timeout option is not supported for %s.' % self.solver)
  237|                 sys.exit(1)
  238| 
  239|         if self.solver == "abc":
  240|             if len(self.solver_opts) > 0:
  241|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yosys-abc', '-S', '; '.join(self.solver_opts)]
  242|             else:
  243|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yosys-abc', '-S', '%blast; &sweep -C 5000; &syn4; &cec -s -m -C 2000']
  244|             self.logic_ax = False
  245|             self.unroll = True
  246|             self.noincr = True
  247|             if self.timeout != 0:
  248|                 print('timeout option is not supported for abc.')
  249|                 sys.exit(1)
  250| 
  251|         if self.solver == "dummy":
  252|             assert self.dummy_file is not None
  253|             self.dummy_fd = open(self.dummy_file, "r")
  254|         else:
  255|             if self.dummy_file is not None:
  256|                 self.dummy_fd = open(self.dummy_file, "w")
  257|             if not self.noincr:
  258|                 self.p_open()
  259| 
  260|         if self.unroll:
  261|             assert not self.forall
  262|             self.logic_uf = False
  263|             self.unroll_idcnt = 0
  264|             self.unroll_buffer = ""
  265|             self.unroll_level = 0
  266|             self.unroll_sorts = set()
  267|             self.unroll_objs = set()
  268|             self.unroll_decls = dict()
  269|             self.unroll_cache = dict()
  270|             self.unroll_stack = list()
  271| 
  272|         if self.logic is None:
  273|             self.logic = ""
  274|             if self.logic_qf: self.logic += "QF_"
  275|             if self.logic_ax: self.logic += "A"
  276|             if self.logic_uf: self.logic += "UF"
  277|             if self.logic_bv: self.logic += "BV"
  278|             if self.logic_dt: self.logic = "ALL"
  279|             if self.solver == "yices" and self.forall: self.logic = "BV"
  280| 
  281|         self.setup_done = True
  282| 
  283|         for stmt in self.info_stmts:
  284|             self.write(stmt)
  285| 
  286|         if self.produce_models:
  287|             self.write("(set-option :produce-models true)")
  288| 
  289|         #See the SMT-LIB Standard, Section 4.1.7
  290|         modestart_options = [":global-declarations", ":interactive-mode", ":produce-assertions", ":produce-assignments", ":produce-models", ":produce-proofs", ":produce-unsat-assumptions", ":produce-unsat-cores", ":random-seed"]
  291|         for key, val in self.smt2_options.items():
  292|             if key in modestart_options:
  293|                 self.write("(set-option {} {})".format(key, val))
  294| 
  295|         self.write("(set-logic %s)" % self.logic)
  296| 
  297|         if self.forall and self.solver == "yices":
  298|             self.write("(set-option :yices-ef-max-iters 1000000000)")
  299| 
  300|         for key, val in self.smt2_options.items():
  301|             if key not in modestart_options:
  302|                 self.write("(set-option {} {})".format(key, val))
  303| 
  304|     def timestamp(self):
  305|         secs = int(time() - self.start_time)
  306|         return "## %3d:%02d:%02d " % (secs // (60*60), (secs // 60) % 60, secs % 60)
  307| 
  308|     def replace_in_stmt(self, stmt, pat, repl):
  309|         if stmt == pat:
  310|             return repl
  311| 
  312|         if isinstance(stmt, list):
  313|             return [self.replace_in_stmt(s, pat, repl) for s in stmt]
  314| 
  315|         return stmt
  316| 
  317|     def unroll_stmt(self, stmt):
  318|         result = []
  319|         recursion_helper(self._unroll_stmt_into, stmt, result)
  320|         return result.pop()
  321| 
  322|     def _unroll_stmt_into(self, stmt, output, depth=128):
  323|         if not isinstance(stmt, list):
  324|             output.append(stmt)
  325|             return
  326| 
  327|         new_stmt = []
  328|         for s in stmt:
  329|             if depth:
  330|                 yield from self._unroll_stmt_into(s, new_stmt, depth - 1)
  331|             else:
  332|                 yield s, new_stmt
  333|         stmt = new_stmt
  334| 
  335|         if len(stmt) >= 2 and not isinstance(stmt[0], list) and stmt[0] in self.unroll_decls:
  336|             assert stmt[1] in self.unroll_objs
  337| 
  338|             key = tuple(stmt)
  339|             if key not in self.unroll_cache:
  340|                 decl = copy(self.unroll_decls[key[0]])
  341| 
  342|                 self.unroll_cache[key] = "|UNROLL#%d|" % self.unroll_idcnt
  343|                 decl[1] = self.unroll_cache[key]
  344|                 self.unroll_idcnt += 1
  345| 
  346|                 if decl[0] == "declare-fun":
  347|                     if isinstance(decl[3], list) or decl[3] not in self.unroll_sorts:
  348|                         self.unroll_objs.add(decl[1])
  349|                         decl[2] = list()
  350|                     else:
  351|                         self.unroll_objs.add(decl[1])
  352|                         decl = list()
  353| 
  354|                 elif decl[0] == "define-fun":
  355|                     arg_index = 1
  356|                     for arg_name, arg_sort in decl[2]:
  357|                         decl[4] = self.replace_in_stmt(decl[4], arg_name, key[arg_index])
  358|                         arg_index += 1
  359|                     decl[2] = list()
  360| 
  361|                 if len(decl) > 0:
  362|                     tmp = []
  363|                     if depth:
  364|                         yield from self._unroll_stmt_into(decl, tmp, depth - 1)
  365|                     else:
  366|                         yield decl, tmp
  367| 
  368|                     decl = tmp.pop()
  369|                     self.write(self.unparse(decl), unroll=False)
  370| 
  371|             output.append(self.unroll_cache[key])
  372|             return
  373| 
  374|         output.append(stmt)
  375| 
  376|     def p_thread_main(self):
  377|         while True:
  378|             data = self.p.stdout.readline().decode("utf-8")
  379|             if data == "": break
  380|             self.p_queue.put(data)
  381|         self.p_queue.put("")
  382|         self.p_running = False
  383| 
  384|     def p_open(self):
  385|         assert self.p is None
  386|         try:
  387|             self.p = subprocess.Popen(self.popen_vargs, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  388|         except FileNotFoundError:
  389|             print("%s SMT Solver '%s' not found in path." % (self.timestamp(), self.popen_vargs[0]), flush=True)
  390|             sys.exit(1)
  391|         running_solvers[self.p_index] = self.p
  392|         self.p_running = True
  393|         self.p_next = None
  394|         self.p_queue = Queue()
  395|         self.p_thread = Thread(target=self.p_thread_main)
  396|         self.p_thread.start()
  397| 
  398|     def p_write(self, data, flush):
  399|         assert self.p is not None
  400|         self.p.stdin.write(bytes(data, "utf-8"))
  401|         if flush: self.p.stdin.flush()
  402| 
  403|     def p_read(self):
  404|         assert self.p is not None
  405|         if self.p_next is not None:
  406|             data = self.p_next
  407|             self.p_next = None
  408|             return data
  409|         if not self.p_running:
  410|             return ""
  411|         return self.p_queue.get()
  412| 
  413|     def p_poll(self, timeout=0.1):
  414|         assert self.p is not None
  415|         assert self.p_running
  416|         if self.p_next is not None:
  417|             return False
  418|         try:
  419|             self.p_next = self.p_queue.get(True, timeout)
  420|             return False
  421|         except Empty:
  422|             return True
  423| 
  424|     def p_close(self):
  425|         assert self.p is not None
  426|         self.p.stdin.close()
  427|         self.p_thread.join()
  428|         assert not self.p_running
  429|         del running_solvers[self.p_index]
  430|         self.p = None
  431|         self.p_next = None
  432|         self.p_queue = None
  433|         self.p_thread = None
  434| 
  435|     def write(self, stmt, unroll=True):
  436|         if stmt.startswith(";"):
  437|             self.info(stmt)
  438|             if not self.setup_done:
  439|                 self.info_stmts.append(stmt)
  440|                 return
  441|         elif not self.setup_done:
  442|             self.setup()
  443| 
  444|         stmt = stmt.strip()
  445| 
  446|         if self.nocomments or self.unroll:
  447|             stmt = re.sub(r" *;.*", "", stmt)
  448|             if stmt == "": return
  449| 
  450|         recheck = None
  451| 
  452|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  453|             if self.noincr:
  454|                 # Don't close the solver yet, if we're just unrolling definitions
  455|                 # required for a (get-...) statement
  456|                 if self.p is not None and not stmt.startswith("(get-") and unroll:
  457|                     self.p_close()
  458| 
  459|         if unroll and self.unroll:
  460|             s = re.sub(r"\|[^|]*\|", "", stmt)
  461|             self.unroll_level += s.count("(") - s.count(")")
  462|             if self.unroll_level > 0:
  463|                 self.unroll_buffer += stmt
  464|                 self.unroll_buffer += " "
  465|                 return
  466|             else:
  467|                 stmt = self.unroll_buffer + stmt
  468|                 self.unroll_buffer = ""
  469| 
  470|             s = self.parse(stmt)
  471| 
  472|             if self.recheck and s and s[0].startswith("get-"):
  473|                 recheck = self.unroll_idcnt
  474| 
  475|             if self.debug_print:
  476|                 print("-> %s" % s)
  477| 
  478|             if len(s) == 3 and s[0] == "declare-sort" and s[2] == "0":
  479|                 self.unroll_sorts.add(s[1])
  480|                 return
  481| 
  482|             elif len(s) == 4 and s[0] == "declare-fun" and s[2] == [] and s[3] in self.unroll_sorts:
  483|                 self.unroll_objs.add(s[1])
  484|                 return
  485| 
  486|             elif len(s) >= 4 and s[0] == "declare-fun":
  487|                 for arg_sort in s[2]:
  488|                     if arg_sort in self.unroll_sorts:
  489|                         self.unroll_decls[s[1]] = s
  490|                         return
  491| 
  492|             elif len(s) >= 4 and s[0] == "define-fun":
  493|                 for arg_name, arg_sort in s[2]:
  494|                     if arg_sort in self.unroll_sorts:
  495|                         self.unroll_decls[s[1]] = s
  496|                         return
  497| 
  498|             stmt = self.unparse(self.unroll_stmt(s))
  499| 
  500|             if recheck is not None and recheck != self.unroll_idcnt:
  501|                 self.check_sat(["sat"])
  502| 
  503|             if stmt == "(push 1)":
  504|                 self.unroll_stack.append((
  505|                     copy(self.unroll_sorts),
  506|                     copy(self.unroll_objs),
  507|                     copy(self.unroll_decls),
  508|                     copy(self.unroll_cache),
  509|                 ))
  510| 
  511|             if stmt == "(pop 1)":
  512|                 self.unroll_sorts, self.unroll_objs, self.unroll_decls, self.unroll_cache = self.unroll_stack.pop()
  513| 
  514|         if self.debug_print:
  515|             print("> %s" % stmt)
  516| 
  517|         if self.debug_file:
  518|             print(stmt, file=self.debug_file)
  519|             self.debug_file.flush()
  520| 
  521|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  522|             if self.noincr:
  523|                 if stmt == "(push 1)":
  524|                     self.smt2cache.append(list())
  525|                 elif stmt == "(pop 1)":
  526|                     self.smt2cache.pop()
  527|                 else:
  528|                     if self.p is not None:
  529|                         self.p_write(stmt + "\n", True)
  530|                     self.smt2cache[-1].append(stmt)
  531|             else:
  532|                 self.p_write(stmt + "\n", True)
  533| 
  534|     def info(self, stmt):
  535|         if not stmt.startswith("; yosys-smt2-"):
  536|             return
  537| 
  538|         fields = stmt.split()
  539| 
  540|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-solver-option":
  541|             self.smt2_options[fields[2]] = fields[3]
  542| 
  543|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-nomem":
  544|             if self.logic is None:
  545|                 self.logic_ax = False
  546| 
  547|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-nobv":
  548|             if self.logic is None:
  549|                 self.logic_bv = False
  550| 
  551|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-stdt":
  552|             if self.logic is None:
  553|                 self.logic_dt = True
  554| 
  555|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-forall":
  556|             if self.logic is None:
  557|                 self.logic_qf = False
  558|             self.forall = True
  559| 
  560|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-module":
  561|             self.curmod = fields[2]
  562|             self.modinfo[self.curmod] = SmtModInfo()
  563| 
  564|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-cell":
  565|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].cells[fields[3]] = fields[2]
  566| 
  567|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-topmod":
  568|             self.topmod = fields[2]
  569| 
  570|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-input":
  571|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].inputs.add(fields[2])
  572|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  573| 
  574|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-output":
  575|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].outputs.add(fields[2])
  576|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  577| 
  578|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-register":
  579|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].registers.add(fields[2])
  580|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  581| 
  582|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-memory":
  583|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].memories[fields[2]] = (int(fields[3]), int(fields[4]), int(fields[5]), int(fields[6]), fields[7] == "async")
  584| 
  585|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-wire":
  586|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wires.add(fields[2])
  587|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  588| 
  589|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-clock":
  590|             for edge in fields[3:]:
  591|                 if fields[2] not in self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks:
  592|                     self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] = edge
  593|                 elif self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] != edge:
  594|                     self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] = "event"
  595| 
  596|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-assert":
  597|             if len(fields) > 4:
  598|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].asserts["%s_a %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  599|             else:
  600|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].asserts["%s_a %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  601| 
  602|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-cover":
  603|             if len(fields) > 4:
  604|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].covers["%s_c %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  605|             else:
  606|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].covers["%s_c %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  607| 
  608|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-assume":
  609|             if len(fields) > 4:
  610|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].assumes["%s_u %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  611|             else:
  612|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].assumes["%s_u %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  613| 
  614|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-maximize":
  615|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].maximize.add(fields[2])
  616| 
  617|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-minimize":
  618|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].minimize.add(fields[2])
  619| 
  620|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-anyconst":
  621|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].anyconsts[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  622|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  623| 
  624|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-anyseq":
  625|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].anyseqs[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  626|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  627| 
  628|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-allconst":
  629|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].allconsts[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  630|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  631| 
  632|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-allseq":
  633|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].allseqs[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  634|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  635| 
  636|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-witness":
  637|             data = json.loads(stmt.split(None, 2)[2])
  638|             if data.get("type") in ["cell", "mem", "posedge", "negedge", "input", "reg", "init", "seq", "blackbox"]:
  639|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].witness.append(data)
  640| 
  641|     def hiernets(self, top, regs_only=False):
  642|         def hiernets_worker(nets, mod, cursor):
  643|             for netname in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].wsize.keys()):
  644|                 if not regs_only or netname in self.modinfo[mod].registers:
  645|                     nets.append(cursor + [netname])
  646|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  647|                 hiernets_worker(nets, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  648| 
  649|         nets = list()
  650|         hiernets_worker(nets, top, [])
  651|         return nets
  652| 
  653|     def hieranyconsts(self, top):
  654|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  655|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].anyconsts.items()):
  656|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  657|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  658|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  659|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  660| 
  661|         results = list()
  662|         worker(results, top, [])
  663|         return results
  664| 
  665|     def hieranyseqs(self, top):
  666|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  667|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].anyseqs.items()):
  668|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  669|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  670|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  671|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  672| 
  673|         results = list()
  674|         worker(results, top, [])
  675|         return results
  676| 
  677|     def hierallconsts(self, top):
  678|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  679|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].allconsts.items()):
  680|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  681|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  682|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  683|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  684| 
  685|         results = list()
  686|         worker(results, top, [])
  687|         return results
  688| 
  689|     def hierallseqs(self, top):
  690|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  691|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].allseqs.items()):
  692|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  693|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  694|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  695|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  696| 
  697|         results = list()
  698|         worker(results, top, [])
  699|         return results
  700| 
  701|     def hiermems(self, top):
  702|         def hiermems_worker(mems, mod, cursor):
  703|             for memname in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].memories.keys()):
  704|                 mems.append(cursor + [memname])
  705|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  706|                 hiermems_worker(mems, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  707| 
  708|         mems = list()
  709|         hiermems_worker(mems, top, [])
  710|         return mems
  711| 
  712|     def hierwitness(self, top, allregs=False, blackbox=True):
  713|         init_witnesses = []
  714|         seq_witnesses = []
  715|         clk_witnesses = []
  716|         mem_witnesses = []
  717| 
  718|         def absolute(path, cursor, witness):
  719|             return {
  720|                 **witness,
  721|                 "path": path + tuple(witness["path"]),
  722|                 "smtpath": cursor + [witness["smtname"]],
  723|             }
  724| 
  725|         for witness in self.modinfo[top].witness:
  726|             if witness["type"] == "input":
  727|                 seq_witnesses.append(absolute((), [], witness))
  728|             if witness["type"] in ("posedge", "negedge"):
  729|                 clk_witnesses.append(absolute((), [], witness))
  730| 
  731|         init_types = ["init"]
  732|         if allregs:
  733|             init_types.append("reg")
  734| 
  735|         seq_types = ["seq"]
  736|         if blackbox:
  737|             seq_types.append("blackbox")
  738| 
  739|         def worker(mod, path, cursor):
  740|             cell_paths = {}
  741|             for witness in self.modinfo[mod].witness:
  742|                 if witness["type"] in init_types:
  743|                     init_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  744|                 if witness["type"] in seq_types:
  745|                     seq_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  746|                 if witness["type"] == "mem":
  747|                     if allregs and not witness["rom"]:
  748|                         width, size = witness["width"], witness["size"]
  749|                         witness = {**witness, "uninitialized": [{"width": width * size, "offset": 0}]}
  750|                     if not witness["uninitialized"]:
  751|                         continue
  752| 
  753|                     mem_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  754|                 if witness["type"] == "cell":
  755|                     cell_paths[witness["smtname"]] = tuple(witness["path"])
  756| 
  757|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  758|                 worker(celltype, path + cell_paths.get(cellname, ("?" + cellname,)), cursor + [cellname])
  759| 
  760|         worker(top, (), [])
  761|         return init_witnesses, seq_witnesses, clk_witnesses, mem_witnesses
  762| 
  763|     def read(self):
  764|         stmt = []
  765|         count_brackets = 0
  766| 
  767|         while True:
  768|             if self.solver == "dummy":
  769|                 line = self.dummy_fd.readline().strip()
  770|             else:
  771|                 line = self.p_read().strip()
  772|                 if self.dummy_file is not None:
  773|                     self.dummy_fd.write(line + "\n")
  774| 
  775|             count_brackets += line.count("(")
  776|             count_brackets -= line.count(")")
  777|             stmt.append(line)
  778| 
  779|             if self.debug_print:
  780|                 print("< %s" % line)
  781|             if count_brackets == 0:
  782|                 break
  783|             if self.solver != "dummy" and self.p.poll():
  784|                 print("%s Solver terminated unexpectedly: %s" % (self.timestamp(), "".join(stmt)), flush=True)
  785|                 sys.exit(1)
  786| 
  787|         stmt = "".join(stmt)
  788|         if stmt.startswith("(error"):
  789|             print("%s Solver Error: %s" % (self.timestamp(), stmt), flush=True)
  790|             if self.solver != "dummy":
  791|                 self.p_close()
  792|             sys.exit(1)
  793| 
  794|         return stmt
  795| 
  796|     def check_sat(self, expected=["sat", "unsat", "unknown", "timeout", "interrupted"]):
  797|         if self.smt2_assumptions:
  798|             assume_exprs = " ".join(self.smt2_assumptions.values())
  799|             check_stmt = f"(check-sat-assuming ({assume_exprs}))"
  800|         else:
  801|             check_stmt = "(check-sat)"
  802|         if self.debug_print:
  803|             print(f"> {check_stmt}")
  804|         if self.debug_file and not self.nocomments:
  805|             print("; running check-sat..", file=self.debug_file)
  806|             self.debug_file.flush()
  807| 
  808|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  809|             if self.noincr:
  810|                 if self.p is not None:
  811|                     self.p_close()
  812|                 self.p_open()
  813|                 for cache_ctx in self.smt2cache:
  814|                     for cache_stmt in cache_ctx:
  815|                         self.p_write(cache_stmt + "\n", False)
  816| 
  817|             self.p_write(f"{check_stmt}\n", True)
  818| 
  819|             if self.timeinfo:
  820|                 i = 0
  821|                 s = r"/-\|"
  822| 
  823|                 count = 0
  824|                 num_bs = 0
  825|                 while self.p_poll():
  826|                     count += 1
  827| 
  828|                     if count < 25:
  829|                         continue
  830| 
  831|                     if count % 10 == 0 or count == 25:
  832|                         secs = count // 10
  833| 
  834|                         if secs < 60:
  835|                             m = "(%d seconds)" % secs
  836|                         elif secs < 60*60:
  837|                             m = "(%d seconds -- %d:%02d)" % (secs, secs // 60, secs % 60)
  838|                         else:
  839|                             m = "(%d seconds -- %d:%02d:%02d)" % (secs, secs // (60*60), (secs // 60) % 60, secs % 60)
  840| 
  841|                         print("%s %s %c" % ("\b \b" * num_bs, m, s[i]), end="", file=sys.stderr)
  842|                         num_bs = len(m) + 3
  843| 
  844|                     else:
  845|                         print("\b" + s[i], end="", file=sys.stderr)
  846| 
  847|                     sys.stderr.flush()
  848|                     i = (i + 1) % len(s)
  849| 
  850|                 if num_bs != 0:
  851|                     print("\b \b" * num_bs, end="", file=sys.stderr)
  852|                     sys.stderr.flush()
  853| 
  854|             else:
  855|                 count = 0
  856|                 while self.p_poll(60):
  857|                     count += 1
  858|                     msg = None
  859| 
  860|                     if count == 1:
  861|                         msg = "1 minute"
  862| 
  863|                     elif count in [5, 10, 15, 30]:
  864|                         msg = "%d minutes" % count
  865| 
  866|                     elif count == 60:
  867|                         msg = "1 hour"
  868| 
  869|                     elif count % 60 == 0:
  870|                         msg = "%d hours" % (count // 60)
  871| 
  872|                     if msg is not None:
  873|                         print("%s waiting for solver (%s)" % (self.timestamp(), msg), flush=True)
  874| 
  875|         if self.forall:
  876|             result = self.read()
  877|             while result not in ["sat", "unsat", "unknown", "timeout", "interrupted", ""]:
  878|                 print("%s %s: %s" % (self.timestamp(), self.solver, result))
  879|                 result = self.read()
  880|         else:
  881|             result = self.read()
  882| 
  883|         if self.debug_file:
  884|             print("(set-info :status %s)" % result, file=self.debug_file)
  885|             print(check_stmt, file=self.debug_file)
  886|             self.debug_file.flush()
  887| 
  888|         if result not in expected:
  889|             if result == "":
  890|                 print("%s Unexpected EOF response from solver." % (self.timestamp()), flush=True)
  891|             else:
  892|                 print("%s Unexpected response from solver: %s" % (self.timestamp(), result), flush=True)
  893|             if self.solver != "dummy":
  894|                 self.p_close()
  895|             sys.exit(1)
  896| 
  897|         return result
  898| 
  899|     def parse(self, stmt):
  900|         def worker(stmt, cursor=0):
  901|             while stmt[cursor] in [" ", "\t", "\r", "\n"]:
  902|                 cursor += 1
  903| 
  904|             if stmt[cursor] == '(':
  905|                 expr = []
  906|                 cursor += 1
  907|                 while stmt[cursor] != ')':
  908|                     el, cursor = worker(stmt, cursor)
  909|                     expr.append(el)
  910|                 return expr, cursor+1
  911| 
  912|             if stmt[cursor] == '|':
  913|                 expr = "|"
  914|                 cursor += 1
  915|                 while stmt[cursor] != '|':
  916|                     expr += stmt[cursor]
  917|                     cursor += 1
  918|                 expr += "|"
  919|                 return expr, cursor+1
  920| 
  921|             expr = ""
  922|             while stmt[cursor] not in ["(", ")", "|", " ", "\t", "\r", "\n"]:
  923|                 expr += stmt[cursor]
  924|                 cursor += 1
  925|             return expr, cursor
  926|         return worker(stmt)[0]
  927| 
  928|     def unparse(self, stmt):
  929|         if isinstance(stmt, list):
  930|             return "(" + " ".join([self.unparse(s) for s in stmt]) + ")"
  931|         return stmt
  932| 
  933|     def bv2hex(self, v):
  934|         h = ""
  935|         v = self.bv2bin(v)
  936|         while len(v) > 0:
  937|             d = 0
  938|             if len(v) > 0 and v[-1] == "1": d += 1
  939|             if len(v) > 1 and v[-2] == "1": d += 2
  940|             if len(v) > 2 and v[-3] == "1": d += 4
  941|             if len(v) > 3 and v[-4] == "1": d += 8
  942|             h = hex(d)[2:] + h
  943|             if len(v) < 4: break
  944|             v = v[:-4]
  945|         return h
  946| 
  947|     def bv2bin(self, v):
  948|         if type(v) is list and len(v) == 3 and v[0] == "_" and v[1].startswith("bv"):
  949|             x, n = int(v[1][2:]), int(v[2])
  950|             return "".join("1" if (x & (1 << i)) else "0" for i in range(n-1, -1, -1))
  951|         if v == "true": return "1"
  952|         if v == "false": return "0"
  953|         if v.startswith("#b"):
  954|             return v[2:]
  955|         if v.startswith("#x"):
  956|             return "".join(hex_dict.get(x) for x in v[2:])
  957|         assert False
  958| 
  959|     def bv2int(self, v):
  960|         return int(self.bv2bin(v), 2)
  961| 
  962|     def get_raw_unsat_assumptions(self):
  963|         if not self.smt2_assumptions:
  964|             return []
  965|         self.write("(get-unsat-assumptions)")
  966|         exprs = set(self.unparse(part) for part in self.parse(self.read()))
  967|         unsat_assumptions = []
  968|         for key, value in self.smt2_assumptions.items():
  969|             # normalize expression
  970|             value = self.unparse(self.parse(value))
  971|             if value in exprs:
  972|                 exprs.remove(value)
  973|                 unsat_assumptions.append(key)
  974|         return unsat_assumptions
  975| 
  976|     def get_unsat_assumptions(self, minimize=False):
  977|         if not minimize:
  978|             return self.get_raw_unsat_assumptions()
  979|         orig_assumptions = self.smt2_assumptions
  980| 
  981|         self.smt2_assumptions = dict(orig_assumptions)
  982| 
  983|         required_assumptions = {}
  984| 
  985|         while True:
  986|             candidate_assumptions = {}
  987|             for key in self.get_raw_unsat_assumptions():
  988|                 if key not in required_assumptions:
  989|                     candidate_assumptions[key] = self.smt2_assumptions[key]
  990| 
  991|             while candidate_assumptions:
  992| 
  993|                 candidate_key, candidate_assume = candidate_assumptions.popitem()
  994| 
  995|                 self.smt2_assumptions = {}
  996|                 for key, assume in candidate_assumptions.items():
  997|                     self.smt2_assumptions[key] = assume
  998|                 for key, assume in required_assumptions.items():
  999|                     self.smt2_assumptions[key] = assume
 1000|                 result = self.check_sat()
 1001| 
 1002|                 if result == 'unsat':
 1003|                     candidate_assumptions = None
 1004|                 else:
 1005|                     required_assumptions[candidate_key] = candidate_assume
 1006| 
 1007|             if candidate_assumptions is not None:
 1008|                 self.smt2_assumptions = orig_assumptions
 1009|                 return list(required_assumptions)
 1010| 
 1011|     def get(self, expr):
 1012|         self.write("(get-value (%s))" % (expr))
 1013|         return self.parse(self.read())[0][1]
 1014| 
 1015|     def get_list(self, expr_list):
 1016|         if len(expr_list) == 0:
 1017|             return []
 1018|         self.write("(get-value (%s))" % " ".join(expr_list))
 1019|         return [n[1] for n in self.parse(self.read()) if n]
 1020| 
 1021|     def get_path(self, mod, path):
 1022|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1023|         path = path.replace("\\", "/").split(".")
 1024| 
 1025|         for i in range(len(path)-1):
 1026|             first = ".".join(path[0:i+1])
 1027|             second = ".".join(path[i+1:])
 1028| 
 1029|             if first in self.modinfo[mod].cells:
 1030|                 nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[first]
 1031|                 return [first] + self.get_path(nextmod, second)
 1032| 
 1033|         return [".".join(path)]
 1034| 
 1035|     def net_expr(self, mod, base, path):
 1036|         if len(path) == 0:
 1037|             return base
 1038| 
 1039|         if len(path) == 1:
 1040|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1041|             if path[0] == "":
 1042|                 return base
 1043|             if isinstance(path[0], int):
 1044|                 return "(|%s#%d| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1045|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells:
 1046|                 return "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1047|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].wsize:
 1048|                 return "(|%s_n %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1049|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].memories:
 1050|                 return "(|%s_m %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1051|             assert 0
 1052| 
 1053|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1054|         assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1055| 
 1056|         nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[path[0]]
 1057|         nextbase = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1058|         return self.net_expr(nextmod, nextbase, path[1:])
 1059| 
 1060|     def witness_net_expr(self, mod, base, witness):
 1061|         net = self.net_expr(mod, base, witness["smtpath"])
 1062|         is_bool = self.net_width(mod, witness["smtpath"]) == 1
 1063|         if is_bool:
 1064|             assert witness["width"] == 1
 1065|             assert witness["smtoffset"] == 0
 1066|             return net
 1067|         return "((_ extract %d %d) %s)" % (witness["smtoffset"] + witness["width"] - 1, witness["smtoffset"], net)
 1068| 
 1069|     def net_width(self, mod, net_path):
 1070|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1071|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1072|             assert net_path[i] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1073|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1074| 
 1075|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1076|         if isinstance(net_path[-1], int):
 1077|             return None
 1078|         assert net_path[-1] in self.modinfo[mod].wsize
 1079|         return self.modinfo[mod].wsize[net_path[-1]]
 1080| 
 1081|     def net_clock(self, mod, net_path):
 1082|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1083|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1084|             assert net_path[i] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1085|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1086| 
 1087|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1088|         if net_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].clocks:
 1089|             return None
 1090|         return self.modinfo[mod].clocks[net_path[-1]]
 1091| 
 1092|     def net_exists(self, mod, net_path):
 1093|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1094|             if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1095|             if net_path[i] not in self.modinfo[mod].cells: return False
 1096|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1097| 
 1098|         if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1099|         if net_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].wsize: return False
 1100|         return True
 1101| 
 1102|     def mem_exists(self, mod, mem_path):
 1103|         for i in range(len(mem_path)-1):
 1104|             if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1105|             if mem_path[i] not in self.modinfo[mod].cells: return False
 1106|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[mem_path[i]]
 1107| 
 1108|         if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1109|         if mem_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].memories: return False
 1110|         return True
 1111| 
 1112|     def mem_expr(self, mod, base, path, port=None, infomode=False):
 1113|         if len(path) == 1:
 1114|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1115|             assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].memories
 1116|             if infomode:
 1117|                 return self.modinfo[mod].memories[path[0]]
 1118|             return "(|%s_m%s %s| %s)" % (mod, "" if port is None else ":%s" % port, path[0], base)
 1119| 
 1120|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1121|         assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1122| 
 1123|         nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[path[0]]
 1124|         nextbase = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1125|         return self.mem_expr(nextmod, nextbase, path[1:], port=port, infomode=infomode)
 1126| 
 1127|     def mem_info(self, mod, path):
 1128|         return self.mem_expr(mod, "", path, infomode=True)
 1129| 
 1130|     def get_net(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1131|         return self.get(self.net_expr(mod_name, state_name, net_path))
 1132| 
 1133|     def get_net_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1134|         return self.get_list([self.net_expr(mod_name, state_name, n) for n in net_path_list])
 1135| 
 1136|     def get_net_hex(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1137|         return self.bv2hex(self.get_net(mod_name, net_path, state_name))
 1138| 
 1139|     def get_net_hex_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1140|         return [self.bv2hex(v) for v in self.get_net_list(mod_name, net_path_list, state_name)]
 1141| 
 1142|     def get_net_bin(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1143|         return self.bv2bin(self.get_net(mod_name, net_path, state_name))
 1144| 
 1145|     def get_net_bin_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1146|         return [self.bv2bin(v) for v in self.get_net_list(mod_name, net_path_list, state_name)]
 1147| 
 1148|     def wait(self):
 1149|         if self.p is not None:
 1150|             self.p.wait()
 1151|             self.p_close()
 1152| 
 1153| 
 1154| class SmtOpts:
 1155|     def __init__(self):
 1156|         self.shortopts = "s:S:v"
 1157|         self.longopts = ["unroll", "noincr", "noprogress", "timeout=", "dump-smt2=", "logic=", "dummy=", "info=", "nocomments", "smt2-option="]
 1158|         self.solver = "yices"
 1159|         self.solver_opts = list()
 1160|         self.debug_print = False
 1161|         self.debug_file = None
 1162|         self.dummy_file = None
 1163|         self.unroll = False
 1164|         self.noincr = False
 1165|         self.timeinfo = os.name != "nt"
 1166|         self.timeout = 0
 1167|         self.logic = None
 1168|         self.info_stmts = list()
 1169|         self.nocomments = False
 1170|         self.smt2_options = {}
 1171| 
 1172|     def handle(self, o, a):
 1173|         if o == "-s":
 1174|             self.solver = a
 1175|         elif o == "-S":
 1176|             self.solver_opts.append(a)
 1177|         elif o == "--timeout":
 1178|             self.timeout = int(a)
 1179|         elif o == "-v":
 1180|             self.debug_print = True
 1181|         elif o == "--unroll":
 1182|             self.unroll = True
 1183|         elif o == "--noincr":
 1184|             self.noincr = True
 1185|         elif o == "--noprogress":
 1186|             self.timeinfo = False
 1187|         elif o == "--dump-smt2":
 1188|             self.debug_file = open(a, "w")
 1189|         elif o == "--logic":
 1190|             self.logic = a
 1191|         elif o == "--dummy":
 1192|             self.dummy_file = a
 1193|         elif o == "--info":
 1194|             self.info_stmts.append(a)
 1195|         elif o == "--nocomments":
 1196|             self.nocomments = True
 1197|         elif o == "--smt2-option":
 1198|             args = a.split('=', 1)
 1199|             if len(args) != 2:
 1200|                 print("--smt2-option expects an <option>=<value> argument")
 1201|                 sys.exit(1)
 1202|             option, value = args
 1203|             self.smt2_options[option] = value
 1204|         else:
 1205|             return False
 1206|         return True
 1207| 
 1208|     def helpmsg(self):
 1209|         return """
 1210|     -s <solver>
 1211|         set SMT solver: z3, yices, boolector, bitwuzla, cvc4, cvc5, mathsat, dummy
 1212|         default: yices
 1213| 
 1214|     -S <opt>
 1215|         pass <opt> as command line argument to the solver
 1216| 
 1217|     --timeout <value>
 1218|         set the solver timeout to the specified value (in seconds).
 1219| 
 1220|     --logic <smt2_logic>
 1221|         use the specified SMT2 logic (e.g. QF_AUFBV)
 1222| 
 1223|     --dummy <filename>
 1224|         if solver is "dummy", read solver output from that file
 1225|         otherwise: write solver output to that file
 1226| 
 1227|     --smt2-option <option>=<value>
 1228|         enable an SMT-LIBv2 option.
 1229| 
 1230|     -v
 1231|         enable debug output
 1232| 
 1233|     --unroll
 1234|         unroll uninterpreted functions
 1235| 
 1236|     --noincr
 1237|         don't use incremental solving, instead restart solver for
 1238|         each (check-sat). This also avoids (push) and (pop).
 1239| 
 1240|     --noprogress
 1241|         disable timer display during solving
 1242|         (this option is set implicitly on Windows)
 1243| 
 1244|     --dump-smt2 <filename>
 1245|         write smt2 statements to file
 1246| 
 1247|     --info <smt2-info-stmt>
 1248|         include the specified smt2 info statement in the smt2 output
 1249| 
 1250|     --nocomments
 1251|         strip all comments from the generated smt2 code
 1252| """

 1254| 
 1255| class MkVcd:
 1256|     def __init__(self, f):
 1257|         self.f = f
 1258|         self.t = -1
 1259|         self.nets = dict()
 1260|         self.clocks = dict()
 1261| 
 1262|     def add_net(self, path, width):
 1263|         path = tuple(path)
 1264|         assert self.t == -1
 1265|         key = "n%d" % len(self.nets)
 1266|         self.nets[path] = (key, width)
 1267| 
 1268|     def add_clock(self, path, edge):
 1269|         path = tuple(path)
 1270|         assert self.t == -1
 1271|         key = "n%d" % len(self.nets)
 1272|         self.nets[path] = (key, 1)
 1273|         self.clocks[path] = (key, edge)
 1274| 
 1275|     def set_net(self, path, bits):
 1276|         path = tuple(path)
 1277|         assert self.t >= 0
 1278|         assert path in self.nets
 1279|         if path not in self.clocks:
 1280|             print("b%s %s" % (bits, self.nets[path][0]), file=self.f)
 1281| 
 1282|     def escape_name(self, name):
 1283|         name = re.sub(r"\[([0-9a-zA-Z_]*[a-zA-Z_][0-9a-zA-Z_]*)\]", r"<\1>", name)
 1284|         if re.match(r"[\[\]]", name) and name[0] != "\\":
 1285|             name = "\\" + name
 1286|         return name
 1287| 
 1288|     def set_time(self, t):
 1289|         assert t >= self.t
 1290|         if t != self.t:
 1291|             if self.t == -1:
 1292|                 print("$version Generated by Yosys-SMTBMC $end", file=self.f)
 1293|                 print("$timescale 1ns $end", file=self.f)
 1294|                 print("$var integer 32 t smt_step $end", file=self.f)
 1295|                 print("$var event 1 ! smt_clock $end", file=self.f)
 1296| 
 1297|                 def vcdescape(n):
 1298|                     if n.startswith("$") or ":" in n:
 1299|                         return "\\" + n
 1300|                     return n
 1301| 
 1302|                 scope = []
 1303|                 for path in sorted(self.nets):
 1304|                     key, width = self.nets[path]
 1305| 
 1306|                     uipath = list(path)
 1307|                     if "." in uipath[-1] and not uipath[-1].startswith("$"):
 1308|                         uipath = uipath[0:-1] + uipath[-1].split(".")
 1309|                     for i in range(len(uipath)):
 1310|                         uipath[i] = re.sub(r"\[([^\]]*)\]", r"<\1>", uipath[i])
 1311| 
 1312|                     while uipath[:len(scope)] != scope:
 1313|                         print("$upscope $end", file=self.f)
 1314|                         scope = scope[:-1]
 1315| 
 1316|                     while uipath[:-1] != scope:
 1317|                         scopename = uipath[len(scope)]
 1318|                         print("$scope module %s $end" % vcdescape(scopename), file=self.f)
 1319|                         scope.append(uipath[len(scope)])
 1320| 
 1321|                     if path in self.clocks and self.clocks[path][1] == "event":
 1322|                         print("$var event 1 %s %s $end" % (key, vcdescape(uipath[-1])), file=self.f)
 1323|                     else:
 1324|                         print("$var wire %d %s %s $end" % (width, key, vcdescape(uipath[-1])), file=self.f)
 1325| 
 1326|                 for i in range(len(scope)):
 1327|                     print("$upscope $end", file=self.f)
 1328| 
 1329|                 print("$enddefinitions $end", file=self.f)
 1330| 
 1331|             self.t = t
 1332|             assert self.t >= 0
 1333| 
 1334|             if self.t > 0:
 1335|                 print("#%d" % (10 * self.t - 5), file=self.f)
 1336|                 for path in sorted(self.clocks.keys()):
 1337|                     if self.clocks[path][1] == "posedge":
 1338|                         print("b0 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1339|                     elif self.clocks[path][1] == "negedge":
 1340|                         print("b1 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1341| 
 1342|             print("#%d" % (10 * self.t), file=self.f)
 1343|             print("1!", file=self.f)
 1344|             print("b%s t" % format(self.t, "032b"), file=self.f)
 1345| 
 1346|             for path in sorted(self.clocks.keys()):
 1347|                 if self.clocks[path][1] == "negedge":
 1348|                     print("b0 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1349|                 else:
 1350|                     print("b1 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)

   32| @cli.command(help="""
   33| Display a Yosys witness trace in a human readable format.
   34| """)
   35| @click.argument("input", type=click.File("r"))
   36| @click.option("--skip-x", help="Treat x bits as unassigned.", is_flag=True)
   37| def display(input, skip_x):
   38|     click.echo(f"Reading Yosys witness trace {input.name!r}...")
   39|     inyw = ReadWitness(input)
   40| 
   41|     if skip_x:
   42|         inyw.skip_x()
   43| 
   44|     def output():
   45| 
   46|         yield click.style("*** RTLIL bit-order below may differ from source level declarations ***", fg="red")
   47|         if inyw.clocks:
   48|             yield click.style("=== Clock Signals ===", fg="blue")
   49|             for clock in inyw.clocks:
   50|                 yield f"  {clock['edge']} {WitnessSig(clock['path'], clock['offset']).pretty()}"
   51| 
   52|         for t, values in inyw.steps():
   53|             if t:
   54|                 yield click.style(f"=== Step {t} ===", fg="blue")
   55|             else:
   56|                 yield click.style("=== Initial State ===", fg="blue")
   57| 
   58|             step_prefix = click.style(f"#{t}", fg="bright_black")
   59| 
   60|             signals, missing = values.present_signals(inyw.sigmap)
   61| 
   62|             assert not missing
   63| 
   64|             for sig in signals:
   65|                 display_bits = values[sig].replace("?", click.style("?", fg="bright_black"))
   66|                 yield f"  {step_prefix} {sig.pretty()} = {display_bits}"
   67|     click.echo_via_pager([line + "\n" for line in output()])

   70| @cli.command(help="""
   71| Display statistics of a Yosys witness trace.
   72| """)
   73| @click.argument("input", type=click.File("r"))
   74| def stats(input):
   75|     click.echo(f"Reading Yosys witness trace {input.name!r}...")
   76|     inyw = ReadWitness(input)
   77| 
   78|     total = 0
   79| 
   80|     for t, values in inyw.steps():
   81|         click.echo(f"{t:5}: {len(values.values):8} bits")
   82|         total += len(values.values)
   83| 
   84|     click.echo(f"total: {total:8} bits")

   87| @cli.command(help="""
   88| Transform a Yosys witness trace.
   89| 
   90| Currently no transformations are implemented, so it is only useful for testing.
   91| If two or more inputs are provided they will be concatenated together into the output.
   92| """)
   93| @click.argument("inputs", type=click.File("r"), nargs=-1)
   94| @click.argument("output", type=click.File("w"))
   95| @click.option("--append", "-p", type=int, multiple=True,
   96|               help="Number of steps (+ve or -ve) to append to end of input trace. "
   97|                   +"Can be defined multiple times, following the same order as input traces. ")
   98| @click.option("--skip-x", help="Leave input x bits unassigned.", is_flag=True)
   99| def yw2yw(inputs, output, append, skip_x):
  100|     if len(inputs) == 0:
  101|         raise click.ClickException(f"no inputs specified")
  102| 
  103|     outyw = WriteWitness(output, "yosys-witness yw2yw")
  104|     join_inputs = len(inputs) > 1
  105|     inyws = {}
  106| 
  107|     if not append:
  108|         # default to 0
  109|         append = [0] * len(inputs)
  110|     if len(append) != len(inputs):
  111|         print(f"Mismatch in number of --append values ({len(append)}) and input traces ({len(inputs)}).")
  112|         sys.exit(1)
  113| 
  114|     for (input, p) in zip(inputs, append):
  115|         if (join_inputs):
  116|             click.echo(f"Loading signals from yosys witness trace {input.name!r}...")
  117|         inyw = ReadWitness(input)
  118|         if p:
  119|             click.echo(f"  appending {p} steps")
  120|             if (p + len(inyw) <= 0):
  121|                 click.echo(f"  skipping {input.name!r} (only {len(inyw)} steps to skip)")
  122|                 continue
  123|             inyw.append_steps(p)
  124|         inyws[input] = inyw
  125|         for clock in inyw.clocks:
  126|             if clock not in outyw.clocks:
  127|                 outyw.add_clock(clock["path"], clock["offset"], clock["edge"])
  128| 
  129|         for sig in inyw.signals:
  130|             if sig not in outyw.signals:
  131|                 outyw.add_sig(sig.path, sig.offset, sig.width, sig.init_only)
  132| 
  133|     init_values = sum([inyw.init_step() for inyw in inyws.values()], start=WitnessValues())
  134| 
  135|     first_witness = True
  136|     for (input, inyw) in inyws.items():
  137|         click.echo(f"Copying yosys witness trace from {input.name!r} to {output.name!r}...")
  138| 
  139|         if first_witness:
  140|             outyw.step(init_values, skip_x=skip_x)
  141|         else:
  142|             outyw.step(inyw.first_step(), skip_x=skip_x)
  143| 
  144|         for t, values in inyw.steps(1):
  145|             outyw.step(values, skip_x=skip_x)
  146| 
  147|         click.echo(f"  copied {t + 1} time steps.")
  148|         first_witness = False
  149| 
  150|     outyw.end_trace()
  151| 
  152|     if join_inputs:
  153|         click.echo(f"Copied {outyw.t} total time steps.")
  154|

  156| class AigerMap:
  157|     def __init__(self, mapfile):
  158|         data = json.load(mapfile)
  159| 
  160|         version = data.get("version") if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
  161|         if version != "Yosys Witness Aiger map":
  162|             raise click.ClickException(f"{mapfile.name}: unexpected file format version {version!r}")
  163| 
  164|         self.latch_count = data["latch_count"]
  165|         self.input_count = data["input_count"]
  166| 
  167|         self.clocks = data["clocks"]
  168| 
  169|         self.sigmap = WitnessSigMap()
  170|         self.init_inputs = set(init["input"] for init in data["inits"])
  171| 
  172|         for bit in data["inputs"] + data["seqs"] + data["inits"]:
  173|             self.sigmap.add_bit((tuple(bit["path"]), bit["offset"]), bit["input"])

  177| @cli.command(help="""
  178| Convert an AIGER witness trace into a Yosys witness trace.
  179| 
  180| This requires a Yosys witness AIGER map file as generated by 'write_aiger -ywmap'.
  181| """)
  182| @click.argument("input", type=click.File("r"))
  183| @click.argument("mapfile", type=click.File("r"))
  184| @click.argument("output", type=click.File("w"))
  185| @click.option("--skip-x", help="Leave input x bits unassigned.", is_flag=True)
  186| @click.option("--present-only", help="Only include bits present in at least one time step.", is_flag=True)
  187| def aiw2yw(input, mapfile, output, skip_x, present_only):
  188|     input_name = input.name
  189|     click.echo(f"Converting AIGER witness trace {input_name!r} to Yosys witness trace {output.name!r}...")
  190|     click.echo(f"Using Yosys witness AIGER map file {mapfile.name!r}")
  191|     aiger_map = AigerMap(mapfile)
  192| 
  193|     header_lines = list(itertools.islice(input, 0, 2))
  194| 
  195|     if len(header_lines) == 2 and header_lines[1][0] in ".bcjf":
  196|         status = header_lines[0].strip()
  197|         if status == "0":
  198|             raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: file contains no trace, the AIGER status is unsat")
  199|         elif status == "2":
  200|             raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: file contains no trace, the AIGER status is sat")
  201|         elif status != "1":
  202|             raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected data in AIGER witness file")
  203|     else:
  204|         input = itertools.chain(header_lines, input)
  205| 
  206|     ffline = next(input, None)
  207|     if ffline is None:
  208|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected end of file")
  209|     ffline = ffline.strip()
  210|     if not re.match(r'[01x]*$', ffline):
  211|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected data in AIGER witness file")
  212|     if not re.match(r'[0]*$', ffline):
  213|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: non-default initial state not supported")
  214| 
  215|     if not present_only:
  216|         outyw = WriteWitness(output, "yosys-witness aiw2yw")
  217| 
  218|         for clock in aiger_map.clocks:
  219|             outyw.add_clock(clock["path"], clock["offset"], clock["edge"])
  220| 
  221|         for (path, offset), id in aiger_map.sigmap.bit_to_id.items():
  222|             outyw.add_sig(path, offset, init_only=id in aiger_map.init_inputs)
  223| 
  224|     missing = set()
  225|     seen = set()
  226| 
  227|     buffered_steps = []
  228| 
  229|     skip = "x?" if skip_x else "?"
  230| 
  231|     t = -1
  232|     while True:
  233|         inline = next(input, None)
  234|         if inline is None:
  235|             click.echo(f"Warning: {input_name}: file may be incomplete")
  236|             break
  237|         inline = inline.strip()
  238|         if inline in [".", "# DONE"]:
  239|             break
  240|         if inline.startswith("#"):
  241|             continue
  242| 
  243|         t += 1
  244| 
  245|         if not re.match(r"[01x]*$", inline):
  246|             raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected data in AIGER witness file")
  247| 
  248|         if len(inline) != aiger_map.input_count:
  249|             raise click.ClickException(
  250|                 f"{input_name}: {mapfile.name}: number of inputs does not match, "
  251|                 f"{len(inline)} in witness, {aiger_map.input_count} in map file"
  252|             )
  253| 
  254|         values = WitnessValues()
  255|         for i, v in enumerate(inline):
  256|             if v in skip or (t > 0 and i in aiger_map.init_inputs):
  257|                 continue
  258| 
  259|             try:
  260|                 bit = aiger_map.sigmap.id_to_bit[i]
  261|             except IndexError:
  262|                 bit = None
  263|             if bit is None:
  264|                 missing.add(i)
  265|             elif present_only:
  266|                 seen.add(i)
  267| 
  268|             values[bit] = v
  269| 
  270|         if present_only:
  271|             buffered_steps.append(values)
  272|         else:
  273|             outyw.step(values)
  274| 
  275|     if present_only:
  276|         outyw = WriteWitness(output, "yosys-witness aiw2yw")
  277| 
  278|         for clock in aiger_map.clocks:
  279|             outyw.add_clock(clock["path"], clock["offset"], clock["edge"])
  280| 
  281|         for (path, offset), id in aiger_map.sigmap.bit_to_id.items():
  282|             if id in seen:
  283|                 outyw.add_sig(path, offset, init_only=id in aiger_map.init_inputs)
  284| 
  285|         for values in buffered_steps:
  286|             outyw.step(values)
  287| 
  288|     outyw.end_trace()
  289| 
  290|     if missing:
  291|         click.echo("The following AIGER inputs belong to unknown signals:")
  292|         click.echo("  " + " ".join(str(id) for id in sorted(missing)))
  293| 
  294|     click.echo(f"Converted {outyw.t} time steps.")
  295| 
  296| @cli.command(help="""
  297| Convert a Yosys witness trace into an AIGER witness trace.
  298| 
  299| This requires a Yosys witness AIGER map file as generated by 'write_aiger -ywmap'.
  300| """)
  301| @click.argument("input", type=click.File("r"))
  302| @click.argument("mapfile", type=click.File("r"))
  303| @click.argument("output", type=click.File("w"))
  304| def yw2aiw(input, mapfile, output):
  305|     click.echo(f"Converting Yosys witness trace {input.name!r} to AIGER witness trace {output.name!r}...")
  306|     click.echo(f"Using Yosys witness AIGER map file {mapfile.name!r}")
  307|     aiger_map = AigerMap(mapfile)
  308|     inyw = ReadWitness(input)
  309| 
  310|     print("1", file=output)
  311|     print("b0", file=output)
  312|     # TODO the b0 status isn't really accurate, but we don't have any better info here
  313|     print("0" * aiger_map.latch_count, file=output)
  314| 
  315|     all_missing = set()
  316| 
  317|     for t, step in inyw.steps():
  318|         bits, missing = step.pack_present(aiger_map.sigmap)
  319|         bits = bits[::-1].replace('?', 'x')
  320|         all_missing.update(missing)
  321|         bits += 'x' * (aiger_map.input_count - len(bits))
  322|         print(bits, file=output)
  323| 
  324|     print(".", file=output)
  325| 
  326|     if all_missing:
  327|         click.echo("The following signals are missing in the AIGER map file and will be dropped:")
  328|         for sig in coalesce_signals(WitnessSig(*bit) for bit in all_missing):
  329|             click.echo("  " + sig.pretty())
  330| 
  331| 
  332|     click.echo(f"Converted {len(inyw)} time steps.")
  333| 
  334| class BtorMap:
  335|     def __init__(self, mapfile):
  336|         self.data = data = json.load(mapfile)
  337| 
  338|         version = data.get("version") if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
  339|         if version != "Yosys Witness BTOR map":
  340|             raise click.ClickException(f"{mapfile.name}: unexpected file format version {version!r}")
  341| 
  342|         self.sigmap = WitnessSigMap()
  343| 
  344|         for mode in ("states", "inputs"):
  345|             for btor_signal_def in data[mode]:
  346|                 if btor_signal_def is None:
  347|                     continue
  348|                 if isinstance(btor_signal_def, dict):
  349|                     btor_signal_def["path"] = tuple(btor_signal_def["path"])
  350|                 else:
  351|                     for chunk in btor_signal_def:
  352|                         chunk["path"] = tuple(chunk["path"])
  353| 
  354| 
  355| @cli.command(help="""
  356| Convert a BTOR witness trace into a Yosys witness trace.
  357| 
  358| This requires a Yosys witness BTOR map file as generated by 'write_btor -ywmap'.
  359| """)
  360| @click.argument("input", type=click.File("r"))
  361| @click.argument("mapfile", type=click.File("r"))
  362| @click.argument("output", type=click.File("w"))
  363| def wit2yw(input, mapfile, output):
  364|     input_name = input.name
  365|     click.echo(f"Converting BTOR witness trace {input_name!r} to Yosys witness trace {output.name!r}...")
  366|     click.echo(f"Using Yosys witness BTOR map file {mapfile.name!r}")
  367|     btor_map = BtorMap(mapfile)
  368| 
  369|     input = filter(None, (line.split(';', 1)[0].strip() for line in input))
  370| 
  371|     sat = next(input, None)
  372|     if sat != "sat":
  373|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: not a BTOR witness file")
  374| 
  375|     props = next(input, None)
  376| 
  377|     t = -1
  378| 
  379|     line = next(input, None)
  380| 
  381|     frames = []
  382|     bits = {}
  383| 
  384|     while line and not line.startswith('.'):
  385|         current_t = int(line[1:].strip())
  386|         if line[0] == '#':
  387|             mode = "states"
  388|         elif line[0] == '@':
  389|             mode = "inputs"
  390|         else:
  391|             raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected data in BTOR witness file")
  392| 
  393|         if current_t > t:
  394|             t = current_t
  395|             values = WitnessValues()
  396|             array_inits = set()
  397|             frames.append(values)
  398| 
  399|         line = next(input, None)
  400|         while line and line[0] not in "#@.":
  401|             if current_t > 0 and mode == "states":
  402|                 line = next(input, None)
  403|                 continue
  404|             tokens = line.split()
  405|             line = next(input, None)
  406| 
  407|             btor_sig = btor_map.data[mode][int(tokens[0])]
  408|             btor_sigs = [btor_sig]
  409| 
  410|             if btor_sig is None:
  411|                 continue
  412| 
  413|             if isinstance(btor_sig, dict):
  414|                 addr = tokens[1]
  415|                 if not addr.startswith('['):
  416|                     addr = '[*]'
  417|                     value = tokens[1]
  418|                 else:
  419|                     value = tokens[2]
  420|                 if not addr.endswith(']'):
  421|                     raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: expected address in BTOR witness file")
  422|                 path = btor_sig["path"]
  423|                 width = btor_sig["width"]
  424|                 size = btor_sig["size"]
  425|                 if addr == '[*]':
  426|                     btor_sigs = [
  427|                         [{
  428|                             "path": (*path, f"\\[{addr}]"),
  429|                             "width": width,
  430|                             "offset": 0,
  431|                         }]
  432|                         for addr in range(size)
  433|                         if (path, addr) not in array_inits
  434|                     ]
  435|                     array_inits.update((path, addr) for addr in range(size))
  436|                 else:
  437|                     addr = int(addr[1:-1], 2)
  438| 
  439|                     if addr < 0 or addr >= size:
  440|                         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: out of bounds address in BTOR witness file")
  441| 
  442|                     array_inits.add((path, addr))
  443|                     btor_sig = [{
  444|                         "path": (*path, f"\\[{addr}]"),
  445|                         "width": width,
  446|                         "offset": 0,
  447|                     }]
  448|                     btor_sigs = [btor_sig]
  449|             else:
  450|                 value = tokens[1]
  451| 
  452|             for btor_sig in btor_sigs:
  453|                 value_bits = iter(reversed(value))
  454| 
  455|                 for chunk in btor_sig:
  456|                     offset = chunk["offset"]
  457|                     path = chunk["path"]
  458|                     for i in range(offset, offset + chunk["width"]):
  459|                         key = (path, i)
  460|                         bits[key] = mode == "inputs"
  461|                         values[key] = next(value_bits)
  462| 
  463|                 if next(value_bits, None) is not None:
  464|                     raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: excess bits in BTOR witness file")
  465| 
  466| 
  467|     if line is None:
  468|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected end of BTOR witness file")
  469|     if line.strip() != '.':
  470|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected data in BTOR witness file")
  471|     if next(input, None) is not None:
  472|         raise click.ClickException(f"{input_name}: unexpected trailing data in BTOR witness file")
  473| 
  474|     outyw = WriteWitness(output, 'yosys-witness wit2yw')
  475| 
  476|     outyw.signals = coalesce_signals((), bits)
  477|     for clock in btor_map.data["clocks"]:
  478|         outyw.add_clock(clock["path"], clock["offset"], clock["edge"])
  479| 
  480|     for values in frames:
  481|         outyw.step(values)
  482| 
  483|     outyw.end_trace()
  484|     click.echo(f"Converted {outyw.t} time steps.")
  485| 
  486| if __name__ == "__main__":
  487|     cli()
