   19| import json, re
   20| 
   21| from functools import total_ordering
   22| 
   23| 
   24| class PrettyJson:
   25|     def __init__(self, f):
   26|         self.f = f
   27|         self.indent = 0
   28|         self.state = ["value"]
   29| 
   30|     def line(self):
   31|         indent = len(self.state) - bool(self.state and self.state[-1] == "value")
   32|         print("\n", end=" " * (2 * indent), file=self.f)
   33| 
   34|     def raw(self, str):
   35|         print(end=str, file=self.f)
   36| 
   37|     def begin_object(self):
   38|         self.begin_value()
   39|         self.raw("{")
   40|         self.state.append("object_first")
   41| 
   42|     def begin_array(self):
   43|         self.begin_value()
   44|         self.raw("[")
   45|         self.state.append("array_first")
   46| 
   47|     def end_object(self):
   48|         state = self.state.pop()
   49|         if state == "object":
   50|             self.line()
   51|         else:
   52|             assert state == "object_first"
   53|         self.raw("}")
   54|         self.end_value()
   55| 
   56|     def end_array(self):
   57|         state = self.state.pop()
   58|         if state == "array":
   59|             self.line()
   60|         else:
   61|             assert state == "array_first"
   62|         self.raw("]")
   63|         self.end_value()
   64| 
   65|     def name(self, name):
   66|         if self.state[-1] == "object_first":
   67|             self.state[-1] = "object"
   68|         else:
   69|             self.raw(",")
   70|         self.line()
   71|         json.dump(str(name), self.f)
   72|         self.raw(": ")
   73|         self.state.append("value")
   74| 
   75|     def begin_value(self):
   76|         if self.state[-1] == "array_first":
   77|             self.line()
   78|             self.state[-1] = "array"
   79|         elif self.state[-1] == "array":
   80|             self.raw(",")
   81|             self.line()
   82|         else:
   83|             assert self.state.pop() == "value"
   84| 
   85|     def end_value(self):
   86|         if not self.state:
   87|             print(file=self.f, flush=True)
   88| 
   89|     def value(self, value):
   90|         self.begin_value()
   91|         json.dump(value, self.f)
   92|         self.end_value()
   93| 
   94|     def entry(self, name, value):
   95|         self.name(name)
   96|         self.value(value)
   97| 
   98|     def object(self, entries=None):
   99|         if isinstance(entries, dict):
  100|             entries = dict.items()
  101|         self.begin_object()
  102|         for name, value in entries:
  103|             self.entry(name, value)
  104|         self.end_object()
  105| 
  106|     def array(self, values=None):
  107|         self.begin_array()
  108|         for value in values:
  109|             self.value(value)
  110|         self.end_array()
  111| 
  112| 
  113| addr_re = re.compile(r'\\\[[0-9]+\]$')
  114| public_name_re = re.compile(r"\\([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\[[0-9]+\])?|\[[0-9]+\])$")
  115| 
  116| def pretty_name(id):
  117|     if public_name_re.match(id):
  118|         return id.lstrip("\\")
  119|     else:
  120|         return id
  121| 
  122| def pretty_path(path):
  123|     out = ""
  124|     for name in path:
  125|         name = pretty_name(name)
  126|         if name.startswith("["):
  127|             out += name
  128|             continue
  129|         if out:
  130|             out += "."
  131|         if name.startswith("\\") or name.startswith("$"):
  132|             out += name + " "
  133|         else:
  134|             out += name
  135| 
  136|     return out
  137| 
  138| @total_ordering
  139| class WitnessSig:
  140|     def __init__(self, path, offset, width=1, init_only=False):
  141|         path = tuple(path)
  142|         self.path, self.width, self.offset, self.init_only = path, width, offset, init_only
  143| 
  144|         self.memory_path = None
  145|         self.memory_addr = None
  146| 
  147|         sort_path = path
  148|         sort_id = -1
  149|         if path and addr_re.match(path[-1]):
  150|             self.memory_path = sort_path = path[:-1]
  151|             self.memory_addr = sort_id = int(path[-1][2:-1])
  152| 
  153|         self.sort_key = (init_only, sort_path, sort_id, offset, width)
  154| 
  155|     def bits(self):
  156|         return ((self.path, i) for i in range(self.offset, self.offset + self.width))
  157| 
  158|     def rev_bits(self):
  159|         return ((self.path, i) for i in reversed(range(self.offset, self.offset + self.width)))
  160| 
  161|     def pretty(self):
  162|         if self.width > 1:
  163|             last_offset = self.offset + self.width - 1
  164|             return f"{pretty_path(self.path)}[{last_offset}:{self.offset}]"
  165|         else:
  166|             return f"{pretty_path(self.path)}[{self.offset}]"
  167| 
  168|     def __eq__(self, other):
  169|         return self.sort_key == other.sort_key
  170| 
  171|     def __hash__(self):
  172|         return hash(self.sort_key)
  173| 
  174|     def __lt__(self, other):
  175|         return self.sort_key < other.sort_key
  176| 
  177| 
  178| def coalesce_signals(signals, bits=None):
  179|     if bits is None:
  180|         bits = {}
  181|     for sig in signals:
  182|         for bit in sig.bits():
  183|             if sig.init_only:
  184|                 bits.setdefault(bit, False)
  185|             else:
  186|                 bits[bit] = True
  187| 
  188|     active = None
  189| 
  190|     out = []
  191| 
  192|     for bit, not_init in sorted(bits.items()):
  193|         if active:
  194|             if active[0] == bit[0] and active[2] == bit[1] and active[3] == not_init:
  195|                 active[2] += 1
  196|             else:
  197|                 out.append(WitnessSig(active[0], active[1], active[2] - active[1], not active[3]))
  198|                 active = None
  199| 
  200|         if active is None:
  201|             active = [bit[0], bit[1], bit[1] + 1, not_init]
  202| 
  203|     if active:
  204|         out.append(WitnessSig(active[0], active[1], active[2] - active[1], not active[3]))
  205| 
  206|     return sorted(out)
  207| 
  208| 
  209| class WitnessSigMap:
  210|     def __init__(self, signals=[]):
  211|         self.signals = []
  212| 
  213|         self.id_to_bit = []
  214|         self.bit_to_id = {}
  215|         self.bit_to_sig = {}
  216| 
  217|         for sig in signals:
  218|             self.add_signal(sig)
  219| 
  220|     def add_signal(self, sig):
  221|         self.signals.append(sig)
  222|         for bit in sig.bits():
  223|             self.add_bit(bit)
  224|             self.bit_to_sig[bit] = sig
  225| 
  226|     def add_bit(self, bit, id=None):
  227|         if id is None:
  228|             id = len(self.id_to_bit)
  229|             self.id_to_bit.append(bit)
  230|         else:
  231|             if len(self.id_to_bit) <= id:
  232|                 self.id_to_bit += [None] * (id - len(self.id_to_bit) + 1)
  233|             self.id_to_bit[id] = bit
  234|         self.bit_to_id[bit] = id
  235| 
  236| 
  237| class WitnessValues:
  238|     def __init__(self):
  239|         self.values = {}
  240| 
  241|     def __setitem__(self, key, value):
  242|         if isinstance(key, tuple) and len(key) == 2:
  243|             if value != "?":
  244|                 assert isinstance(value, str)
  245|                 assert len(value) == 1
  246|                 self.values[key] = value
  247|         else:
  248|             assert isinstance(key, WitnessSig)
  249|             assert key.width == len(value)
  250|             if isinstance(value, str):
  251|                 value = reversed(value)
  252|             for bit, bit_value in zip(key.bits(), value):
  253|                 if bit_value != "?":
  254|                     self.values[bit] = bit_value
  255| 
  256|     def __getitem__(self, key):
  257|         if isinstance(key, tuple) and len(key) == 2:
  258|             return self.values.get(key, "?")
  259|         else:
  260|             assert isinstance(key, WitnessSig)
  261|             return "".join([self.values.get(bit, "?") for bit in key.rev_bits()])
  262| 
  263|     def pack_present(self, sigmap):
  264|         missing = []
  265| 
  266|         max_id = max((sigmap.bit_to_id.get(bit, -1) for bit in self.values), default=-1)
  267| 
  268|         vector = ["?"] * (max_id + 1)
  269|         for bit, bit_value in self.values.items():
  270|             id = sigmap.bit_to_id.get(bit, - 1)
  271|             if id < 0:
  272|                 missing.append(bit)
  273|             else:
  274|                 vector[max_id - sigmap.bit_to_id[bit]] = bit_value
  275| 
  276|         return "".join(vector), missing
  277| 
  278|     def pack(self, sigmap):
  279|         packed, missing = self.pack_present(sigmap)
  280|         if missing:
  281|             raise RuntimeError(f"Cannot pack bits {missing!r}")
  282|         return packed
  283| 
  284|     def unpack(self, sigmap, bits):
  285|         for i, bit_value in enumerate(reversed(bits)):
  286|             if bit_value != "?":
  287|                 self.values[sigmap.id_to_bit[i]] = bit_value
  288| 
  289|     def present_signals(self, sigmap):
  290|         signals = set(sigmap.bit_to_sig.get(bit) for bit in self.values)
  291|         missing_signals = None in signals
  292|         if missing_signals:
  293|             signals.discard(None)
  294| 
  295|         return sorted(signals), missing_signals
  296| 
  297|     def __add__(self, other: "WitnessValues"):
  298|         new = WitnessValues()
  299|         new += self
  300|         new += other
  301|         return new
  302| 
  303|     def __iadd__(self, other: "WitnessValues"):
  304|         for key, value in other.values.items():
  305|             self.values.setdefault(key, value)
  306|         return self
  307| 
  308| class WriteWitness:
  309|     def __init__(self, f, generator):
  310|         self.out = PrettyJson(f)
  311|         self.t = 0
  312|         self.header_written = False
  313|         self.clocks = []
  314|         self.signals = []
  315| 
  316|         self.out.begin_object()
  317|         self.out.entry("format", "Yosys Witness Trace")
  318|         self.out.entry("generator", generator)
  319| 
  320|     def add_clock(self, path, offset, edge):
  321|         assert not self.header_written
  322|         self.clocks.append({
  323|             "path": path,
  324|             "edge": edge,
  325|             "offset": offset,
  326|         })
  327| 
  328|     def add_sig(self, path, offset, width=1, init_only=False):
  329|         assert not self.header_written
  330|         sig = WitnessSig(path, offset, width, init_only)
  331|         self.signals.append(sig)
  332|         return sig
  333| 
  334|     def write_header(self):
  335|         assert not self.header_written
  336|         self.header_written = True
  337|         self.out.name("clocks")
  338|         self.out.array(self.clocks)
  339| 
  340|         self.signals = coalesce_signals(self.signals)
  341|         self.sigmap = WitnessSigMap(self.signals)
  342| 
  343|         self.out.name("signals")
  344|         self.out.array({
  345|             "path": sig.path,
  346|             "width": sig.width,
  347|             "offset": sig.offset,
  348|             "init_only": sig.init_only,
  349|         } for sig in self.signals)
  350| 
  351|         self.out.name("steps")
  352|         self.out.begin_array()
  353| 
  354|     def step(self, values, skip_x=False):
  355|         if not self.header_written:
  356|             self.write_header()
  357| 
  358|         packed = values.pack(self.sigmap)
  359|         if skip_x:
  360|             packed = packed.replace('x', '?')
  361|         self.out.value({"bits": packed})
  362| 
  363|         self.t += 1
  364| 
  365|     def end_trace(self):
  366|         if not self.header_written:
  367|             self.write_header()
  368|         self.out.end_array()
  369|         self.out.end_object()
  370| 
  371| 
  372| class ReadWitness:
  373|     def __init__(self, f):
  374|         data = json.load(f)
  375|         if not isinstance(data, dict):
  376|             data = {}
  377| 
  378|         data_format = data.get("format", "Unknown Format")
  379| 
  380|         if data_format != "Yosys Witness Trace":
  381|             raise ValueError(f"unsupported format {data_format!r}")
  382| 
  383|         self.clocks = data["clocks"]
  384|         for clock in self.clocks:
  385|             clock["path"] = tuple(clock["path"])
  386| 
  387|         self.signals = [
  388|             WitnessSig(sig["path"], sig["offset"], sig["width"], sig["init_only"])
  389|             for sig in data["signals"]
  390|         ]
  391| 
  392|         self.sigmap = WitnessSigMap(self.signals)
  393| 
  394|         self.bits = [step["bits"] for step in data["steps"]]
  395| 
  396|     def skip_x(self):
  397|         self.bits = [step.replace('x', '?') for step in self.bits]
  398| 
  399|     def init_step(self):
  400|         return self.step(0)
  401|     
  402|     def non_init_bits(self):
  403|         if len(self) > 1:
  404|             return len(self.bits[1])
  405|         else:
  406|             return sum([sig.width for sig in self.signals if not sig.init_only])
  407|     
  408|     def first_step(self):
  409|         values = WitnessValues()
  410|         # may have issues when non_init_bits is 0
  411|         values.unpack(WitnessSigMap([sig for sig in self.signals if not sig.init_only]), self.bits[0][-self.non_init_bits():])
  412|         return values
  413| 
  414|     def step(self, t):
  415|         values = WitnessValues()
  416|         values.unpack(self.sigmap, self.bits[t])
  417|         return values
  418| 
  419|     def steps(self, start=0):
  420|         for i in range(start, len(self.bits)):
  421|             yield i, self.step(i)
  422| 
  423|     def append_steps(self, t):
  424|         if not t:
  425|             pass
  426|         elif t < 0:
  427|             self.bits = self.bits[:t]
  428|         else:
  429|             self.bits.extend(["0"*self.non_init_bits()]*t)
  430| 
  431|     def __len__(self):
  432|         return len(self.bits)

==> cmake/CopyData.cmake <==
    1| get_filename_component(directory "${OUTPUT}" DIRECTORY)
    2| file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${directory}")
    3| file(COPY_FILE "${INPUT}" "${OUTPUT}")
    4| file(CHMOD "${OUTPUT}" PERMISSIONS OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE GROUP_READ WORLD_READ)

==> cmake/Coverage.py <==
    1| """Collect the instrumented build's coverage report."""
    2| import os
    3| from pathlib import Path
    4| import shutil
    5| import subprocess
    6| import sys
    7| 
    8| source, build, action = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
    9| if action == 'clean':
   10|     if (source / 'coverage').exists():
   11|         shutil.rmtree(source / 'coverage')
   12|     (build / 'yosys.profdata').unlink(missing_ok=True)
   13| else:
   14|     env = dict(os.environ, LLVM_PROFILE_FILE=str(source / 'coverage/coverage_%p.profraw'))
   15|     subprocess.run([str(build / 'yosys'), '-qp', 'help; help -all'], env=env, check=True)
   16|     if (source / 'coverage_html').exists():
   17|         shutil.rmtree(source / 'coverage_html')
   18|     subprocess.run(['llvm-profdata', 'merge', '-sparse', *map(str, (source / 'coverage').glob('coverage_*.profraw')), '-o', str(build / 'yosys.profdata')], check=True)
   19|     subprocess.run(['llvm-cov', 'show', str(build / 'yosys'), '-instr-profile=' + str(build / 'yosys.profdata'), '-format=html', '-output-dir=' + str(source / 'coverage_html'), '--compilation-dir=' + str(source), '-ignore-filename-regex=(^|.*/)libs/.*|/usr/include/.*'], check=True)

==> cmake/Docs.py <==
    1| """Prepare the documentation inputs using the built programs."""
    2| import pathlib
    3| import shutil
    4| import subprocess
    5| import sys
    6| 
    7| source, build, prefix = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
    8| out = source / 'docs/source/generated'
    9| out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   10| yosys = str(build / (prefix + 'yosys'))
   11| for kind in ('cmds', 'cells'):
   12|     subprocess.run([yosys, '-p', f'help -dump-{kind}-json {out / (kind + ".json")}'], check=True)
   13| for name in ('yosys', 'yosys-smtbmc', 'yosys-witness', 'yosys-config', 'yosys-filterlib', 'yosys-abc'):
   14|     program = build / (prefix + name)
   15|     if not program.exists():
   16|         continue
   17|     stderr = name in ('yosys-filterlib', 'yosys-abc')
   18|     with (out / name).open('w') as f:
   19|         result = subprocess.run([str(program), '--help'], **({'stderr': f} if stderr else {'stdout': f}))
   20|     if result.returncode and not stderr:
   21|         raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(result.returncode, result.args)
   22| (out / 'functional').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   23| shutil.copy(source / 'backends/functional/smtlib.cc', out / 'functional/smtlib.cc')
   24| with (out / 'functional/rosette.diff').open('w') as f:
   25|     result = subprocess.run(['diff', '-U', '20', str(source / 'backends/functional/smtlib.cc'), str(source / 'backends/functional/smtlib_rosette.cc')], stdout=f)
   26|     if result.returncode not in (0, 1):
   27|         raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(result.returncode, result.args)
   28| shutil.copy(source / 'passes/cmds/chformal.cc', out / 'chformal.cc')
   29| subprocess.run([yosys, '-qQT', '-h', 'chformal', '-l', str(out / 'chformal.log')], check=True)

==> cmake/Documentation.cmake <==
    1| find_program(HARNESS_MAKE NAMES gmake make REQUIRED)
    2| add_custom_target(docs-prep
    3|   COMMAND ${Python3_EXECUTABLE} "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/Docs.py" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}" "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}" "${YOSYS_PROGRAM_PREFIX}"
    4|   COMMAND ${HARNESS_MAKE} -C "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/docs" gen
    5|   DEPENDS yosys share VERBATIM)
    6| add_custom_target(coverage COMMAND ${Python3_EXECUTABLE} "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/Coverage.py" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}" "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}" collect DEPENDS yosys VERBATIM)
    7| add_custom_target(clean-coverage COMMAND ${Python3_EXECUTABLE} "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/Coverage.py" "${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}" "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}" clean VERBATIM)

==> cmake/Generate.cmake <==
    1| execute_process(COMMAND "${PYTHON}" "${SCRIPT}" "${INPUT}" OUTPUT_FILE "${OUTPUT}" COMMAND_ERROR_IS_FATAL ANY)

==> cmake/Optional.cmake <==
    1| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_CCACHE "Use ccache" OFF)
    2| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_GCOV "Instrument for LLVM coverage" OFF)
    3| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_FUNCTIONAL_TESTS "Build functional IR tests" OFF)
    4| option(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC "Enable Verific" OFF)
    5| set(VERIFIC_DIR "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/src/verific_lib" CACHE PATH "Verific installation")
    6| if(YOSYS_ENABLE_CCACHE)
    7|   find_program(CCACHE_EXECUTABLE ccache REQUIRED)
    8|   set(CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_LAUNCHER "${CCACHE_EXECUTABLE}")
    9| endif()
   10| if(YOSYS_ENABLE_GCOV)
   11|   list(APPEND YOSYS_OPTIONS -fprofile-instr-generate -fcoverage-mapping)
   12|   add_link_options(-fprofile-instr-generate)
   13| endif()
   14| if(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC)
   15|   list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC)
   16|   list(APPEND YOSYS_SOURCES frontends/verific/verificsva.cc)
   17|   set(components database util containers)
   18|   foreach(component HIER_TREE SYSTEMVERILOG VHDL EDIF LIBERTY YOSYSHQ_EXTENSIONS)
   19|     if(component MATCHES "^(HIER_TREE|SYSTEMVERILOG|VHDL)$")
   20|       set(default ON)
   21|     else()
   22|       set(default OFF)
   23|     endif()
   24|     option(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC_${component} "Verific ${component}" ${default})
   25|   endforeach()
   26|   foreach(pair "HIER_TREE;hier_tree" "SYSTEMVERILOG;verilog" "VHDL;vhdl" "EDIF;edif" "LIBERTY;synlib" "YOSYSHQ_EXTENSIONS;extensions")
   27|     list(GET pair 0 option_name)
   28|     list(GET pair 1 directory)
   29|     if(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC_${option_name} OR ((option_name MATCHES "HIER_TREE|SYSTEMVERILOG|VHDL|YOSYSHQ_EXTENSIONS") AND IS_DIRECTORY "${VERIFIC_DIR}/${directory}"))
   30|       list(APPEND components ${directory})
   31|     endif()
   32|     if(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC_${option_name})
   33|       if(option_name STREQUAL "YOSYSHQ_EXTENSIONS")
   34|         list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS YOSYSHQ_VERIFIC_EXTENSIONS)
   35|       else()
   36|         list(APPEND YOSYS_DEFINITIONS VERIFIC_${option_name}_SUPPORT)
   37|       endif()
   38|     endif()
   39|   endforeach()
   40|   if("extensions" IN_LIST components)
   41|     list(APPEND YOSYS_SOURCES kernel/log_compat.cc)
   42|   endif()
   43|   foreach(component IN LISTS components)
   44|     list(APPEND YOSYS_INCLUDES "${VERIFIC_DIR}/${component}")
   45|     if(APPLE)
   46|       file(GLOB libraries "${VERIFIC_DIR}/${component}/*-mac.a")
   47|     else()
   48|       file(GLOB libraries "${VERIFIC_DIR}/${component}/*-linux.a")
   49|     endif()
   50|     list(APPEND YOSYS_LIBRARIES "$<LINK_LIBRARY:WHOLE_ARCHIVE,${libraries}>")
   51|   endforeach()
   52|   if(YOSYS_ENABLE_VERIFIC_VHDL)
   53|     foreach(year 1987 1993 2008 2019)
   54|       file(COPY "${VERIFIC_DIR}/vhdl_packages/vdbs_${year}/" DESTINATION "${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/share/verific/vhdl_vdbs_${year}")
   55|     endforeach()
   56|   endif()
   57| endif()

==> flake.nix <==
    1| {
    2|   description = "A nix flake for the Yosys synthesis suite";
    3| 
    4|   inputs = {
    5|     nixpkgs.url = "github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable";
    6|     flake-utils.url = "github:numtide/flake-utils";
    7|   };
    8| 
    9|   outputs = { self, nixpkgs, flake-utils }:
   10|     flake-utils.lib.eachDefaultSystem (system:
   11|       let
   12|         pkgs = import nixpkgs {
   13|           inherit system;
   14|         };
   15|         # TODO: don't override src when ./abc is empty
   16|         # which happens when the command used is `nix build` and not `nix build ?submodules=1`
   17|         abc-verifier = pkgs.abc-verifier;
   18|         yosys = pkgs.clangStdenv.mkDerivation {
   19|           name = "yosys";
   20|           src = ./. ;
   21|           buildInputs = with pkgs; [ clang bison flex libffi tcl readline python3 zlib git pkg-configUpstream llvmPackages.bintools ];
   22|           checkInputs = with pkgs; [ gtest ];
   23|           propagatedBuildInputs = [ abc-verifier ];
   24|           nativeBuildInputs = with pkgs; [ cmake ninja ];
   25|           cmakeFlags = [ "-DYOSYS_ABC_EXTERNAL=yosys-abc" ];
   26|           checkPhase = "ctest --test-dir tests/unit --output-on-failure";
   27|           installPhase = ''
   28|             cmake --install . --prefix $out
   29|             ln -s ${abc-verifier}/bin/abc $out/bin/yosys-abc
   30|           '';
   31| 					buildPhase = ''
   32|             cmake --build . -j$NIX_BUILD_CORES
   33|           '';
   34|           meta = with pkgs.lib; {
   35|             description = "Yosys Open SYnthesis Suite";
   36|             homepage = "https://yosyshq.net/yosys/";
   37|             license = licenses.isc;
   38|             maintainers = with maintainers; [ ];
   39|           };
   40|         };
   41|       in {
   42|         packages.default = yosys;
   43|         defaultPackage = yosys;
   44|         devShell = pkgs.mkShell {
   45|           buildInputs = with pkgs; [ clang llvmPackages.bintools gcc bison flex libffi tcl readline python3 zlib git gtest abc-verifier verilog ];
   46|         };
   47|       }
   48|     );
   49| }

==> passes/pmgen/pmgen.py <==
    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| 
    3| import re
    4| import sys
    5| import pprint
    6| import getopt
    7| 
    8| pp = pprint.PrettyPrinter(indent=4)
    9| 
   10| prefix = None
   11| pmgfiles = list()
   12| outfile = None
   13| debug = False
   14| genhdr = False
   15| 
   16| opts, args = getopt.getopt(sys.argv[1:], "p:o:dg")
   17| 
   18| for o, a in opts:
   19|     if o == "-p":
   20|         prefix = a
   21|     elif o == "-o":
   22|         outfile = a
   23|     elif o == "-d":
   24|         debug = True
   25|     elif o == "-g":
   26|         genhdr = True
   27| 
   28| if outfile is None:
   29|     outfile = "/dev/stdout"
   30| 
   31| for a in args:
   32|     assert a.endswith(".pmg")
   33|     if prefix is None and len(args) == 1:
   34|         prefix = a[0:-4]
   35|         prefix = prefix.split('/')[-1]
   36|     pmgfiles.append(a)
   37| 
   38| assert prefix is not None
   39| 
   40| current_pattern = None
   41| current_subpattern = None
   42| patterns = dict()
   43| subpatterns = dict()
   44| subpattern_args = dict()
   45| state_types = dict()
   46| udata_types = dict()
   47| blocks = list()
   48| ids = dict()
   49| 
   50| def rewrite_cpp(s):
   51|     t = list()
   52|     i = 0
   53|     while i < len(s):
   54|         if s[i] in ("'", '"') and i + 1 < len(s):
   55|             j = i + 1
   56|             while j + 1 < len(s) and s[j] != s[i]:
   57|                 if s[j] == '\\' and j + 1 < len(s):
   58|                     j += 1
   59|                 j += 1
   60|             t.append(s[i:j+1])
   61|             i = j + 1
   62|             continue
   63| 
   64|         if s[i] in ('$', '\\') and i + 1 < len(s):
   65|             j = i + 1
   66|             while True:
   67|                 if j == len(s):
   68|                     j -= 1
   69|                     break
   70|                 if ord('a') <= ord(s[j]) <= ord('z'):
   71|                     j += 1
   72|                     continue
   73|                 if ord('A') <= ord(s[j]) <= ord('Z'):
   74|                     j += 1
   75|                     continue
   76|                 if ord('0') <= ord(s[j]) <= ord('9'):
   77|                     j += 1
   78|                     continue
   79|                 if s[j] == '_':
   80|                     j += 1
   81|                     continue
   82|                 j -= 1
   83|                 break
   84| 
   85|             n = s[i:j+1]
   86|             i = j + 1
   87| 
   88|             if n[0] == '$':
   89|                 v = "id_d_" + n[1:]
   90|             else:
   91|                 v = "id_b_" + n[1:]
   92| 
   93|             if v not in ids:
   94|                 ids[v] = n
   95|             else:
   96|                 assert ids[v] == n
   97| 
   98|             t.append(v)
   99|             continue
  100| 
  101|         if s[i] == "\t":
  102|             t.append("  ")
  103|         else:
  104|             t.append(s[i])
  105| 
  106|         i += 1
  107| 
  108|     return "".join(t)
  109| 
  110| def process_pmgfile(f, filename):
  111|     linenr = 0
  112|     global current_pattern
  113|     global current_subpattern
  114|     while True:
  115|         linenr += 1
  116|         line = f.readline()
  117|         if line == "": break
  118|         line = line.strip()
  119| 
  120|         cmd = line.split()
  121|         if len(cmd) == 0 or cmd[0].startswith("//"): continue
  122|         cmd = cmd[0]
  123| 
  124|         if cmd == "pattern":
  125|             if current_pattern is not None:
  126|                 block = dict()
  127|                 block["type"] = "final"
  128|                 block["pattern"] = (current_pattern, current_subpattern)
  129|                 blocks.append(block)
  130|             line = line.split()
  131|             assert len(line) == 2
  132|             assert line[1] not in patterns
  133|             current_pattern = line[1]
  134|             current_subpattern = ""
  135|             patterns[current_pattern] = len(blocks)
  136|             subpatterns[(current_pattern, current_subpattern)] = len(blocks)
  137|             subpattern_args[(current_pattern, current_subpattern)] = list()
  138|             state_types[current_pattern] = dict()
  139|             udata_types[current_pattern] = dict()
  140|             continue
  141| 
  142|         assert current_pattern is not None
  143| 
  144|         if cmd == "fallthrough":
  145|             block = dict()
  146|             block["type"] = "fallthrough"
  147|             blocks.append(block)
  148|             line = line.split()
  149|             assert len(line) == 1
  150|             continue
  151| 
  152|         if cmd == "subpattern":
  153|             if len(blocks) == 0 or blocks[-1]["type"] != "fallthrough":
  154|                 block = dict()
  155|                 block["type"] = "final"
  156|                 block["pattern"] = (current_pattern, current_subpattern)
  157|                 blocks.append(block)
  158|             elif len(blocks) and blocks[-1]["type"] == "fallthrough":
  159|                 del blocks[-1]
  160|             line = line.split()
  161|             assert len(line) == 2
  162|             current_subpattern = line[1]
  163|             subpattern_args[(current_pattern, current_subpattern)] = list()
  164|             assert (current_pattern, current_subpattern) not in subpatterns
  165|             subpatterns[(current_pattern, current_subpattern)] = len(blocks)
  166|             continue
  167| 
  168|         if cmd == "arg":
  169|             line = line.split()
  170|             assert len(line) > 1
  171|             subpattern_args[(current_pattern, current_subpattern)] += line[1:]
  172|             continue
  173| 
  174|         if cmd == "state":
  175|             m = re.match(r"^state\s+<(.*?)>\s+(([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*\s+)*[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)\s*$", line)
  176|             assert m
  177|             type_str = m.group(1)
  178|             states_str = m.group(2)
  179|             for s in re.split(r"\s+", states_str):
  180|                 assert s not in state_types[current_pattern]
  181|                 state_types[current_pattern][s] = type_str
  182|             continue
  183| 
  184|         if cmd == "udata":
  185|             m = re.match(r"^udata\s+<(.*?)>\s+(([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*\s+)*[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)\s*$", line)
  186|             assert m
  187|             type_str = m.group(1)
  188|             udatas_str = m.group(2)
  189|             for s in re.split(r"\s+", udatas_str):
  190|                 assert s not in udata_types[current_pattern]
  191|                 udata_types[current_pattern][s] = type_str
  192|             continue
  193| 
  194|         if cmd == "match":
  195|             block = dict()
  196|             block["type"] = "match"
  197|             block["src"] = "%s:%d" % (filename, linenr)
  198|             block["pattern"] = (current_pattern, current_subpattern)
  199| 
  200|             block["genargs"] = None
  201|             block["gencode"] = None
  202| 
  203|             line = line.split()
  204|             assert len(line) == 2
  205|             assert (line[1] not in state_types[current_pattern]) or (state_types[current_pattern][line[1]] == "Cell*")
  206|             block["cell"] = line[1]
  207|             state_types[current_pattern][line[1]] = "Cell*";
  208| 
  209|             block["if"] = list()
  210|             block["setup"] = list()
  211|             block["index"] = list()
  212|             block["filter"] = list()
  213|             block["sets"] = list()
  214|             block["optional"] = False
  215|             block["semioptional"] = False
  216| 
  217|             while True:
  218|                 linenr += 1
  219|                 l = f.readline()
  220|                 assert l != ""
  221|                 a = l.split()
  222|                 if len(a) == 0 or a[0].startswith("//"): continue
  223|                 if a[0] == "endmatch": break
  224| 
  225|                 if a[0] == "if":
  226|                     b = l.lstrip()[2:]
  227|                     block["if"].append(rewrite_cpp(b.strip()))
  228|                     continue
  229| 
  230|                 if a[0] == "select":
  231|                     b = l.lstrip()[6:]
  232|                     block["setup"].append(("select", rewrite_cpp(b.strip())))
  233|                     continue
  234| 
  235|                 if a[0] == "slice":
  236|                     m = re.match(r"^\s*slice\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$", l)
  237|                     block["setup"].append(("slice", m.group(1), rewrite_cpp(m.group(2))))
  238|                     continue
  239| 
  240|                 if a[0] == "choice":
  241|                     m = re.match(r"^\s*choice\s+<(.*?)>\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$", l)
  242|                     block["setup"].append(("choice", m.group(1), m.group(2), rewrite_cpp(m.group(3))))
  243|                     continue
  244| 
  245|                 if a[0] == "define":
  246|                     m = re.match(r"^\s*define\s+<(.*?)>\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$", l)
  247|                     block["setup"].append(("define", m.group(1), m.group(2), rewrite_cpp(m.group(3))))
  248|                     continue
  249| 
  250|                 if a[0] == "index":
  251|                     m = re.match(r"^\s*index\s+<(.*?)>\s+(.*?)\s*===\s*(.*?)\s*$", l)
  252|                     assert m
  253|                     block["index"].append((m.group(1), rewrite_cpp(m.group(2)), rewrite_cpp(m.group(3))))
  254|                     continue
  255| 
  256|                 if a[0] == "filter":
  257|                     b = l.lstrip()[6:]
  258|                     block["filter"].append(rewrite_cpp(b.strip()))
  259|                     continue
  260| 
  261|                 if a[0] == "set":
  262|                     m = re.match(r"^\s*set\s+(\S+)\s+(.*?)\s*$", l)
  263|                     block["sets"].append((m.group(1), rewrite_cpp(m.group(2))))
  264|                     continue
  265| 
  266|                 if a[0] == "optional":
  267|                     block["optional"] = True
  268|                     continue
  269| 
  270|                 if a[0] == "semioptional":
  271|                     block["semioptional"] = True
  272|                     continue
  273| 
  274|                 if a[0] == "generate":
  275|                     block["genargs"] = list([int(s) for s in a[1:]])
  276|                     if len(block["genargs"]) == 0: block["genargs"].append(100)
  277|                     if len(block["genargs"]) == 1: block["genargs"].append(0)
  278|                     assert len(block["genargs"]) == 2
  279|                     block["gencode"] = list()
  280|                     while True:
  281|                         linenr += 1
  282|                         l = f.readline()
  283|                         assert l != ""
  284|                         a = l.split()
  285|                         if len(a) == 1 and a[0] == "endmatch": break
  286|                         block["gencode"].append(rewrite_cpp(l.rstrip()))
  287|                     break
  288| 
  289|                 raise RuntimeError("'%s' statement not recognised on line %d" % (a[0], linenr))
  290| 
  291|             if block["optional"]:
  292|                 assert not block["semioptional"]
  293| 
  294|             blocks.append(block)
  295|             continue
  296| 
  297|         if cmd == "code":
  298|             block = dict()
  299|             block["type"] = "code"
  300|             block["src"] = "%s:%d" % (filename, linenr)
  301|             block["pattern"] = (current_pattern, current_subpattern)
  302| 
  303|             block["code"] = list()
  304|             block["fcode"] = list()
  305|             block["states"] = set()
  306| 
  307|             for s in line.split()[1:]:
  308|                 if s not in state_types[current_pattern]:
  309|                     raise RuntimeError("'%s' not in state_types" % s)
  310|                 block["states"].add(s)
  311| 
  312|             codetype = "code"
  313| 
  314|             while True:
  315|                 linenr += 1
  316|                 l = f.readline()
  317|                 assert l != ""
  318|                 a = l.split()
  319|                 if len(a) == 0: continue
  320|                 if a[0] == "endcode": break
  321| 
  322|                 if a[0] == "finally":
  323|                     codetype = "fcode"
  324|                     continue
  325| 
  326|                 block[codetype].append(rewrite_cpp(l.rstrip()))
  327| 
  328|             blocks.append(block)
  329|             continue
  330| 
  331|         raise RuntimeError("'%s' command not recognised" % cmd)
  332| 
  333| for fn in pmgfiles:
  334|     with open(fn, "r") as f:
  335|         process_pmgfile(f, fn)
  336| 
  337| if current_pattern is not None:
  338|     block = dict()
  339|     block["type"] = "final"
  340|     block["pattern"] = (current_pattern, current_subpattern)
  341|     blocks.append(block)
  342| 
  343| current_pattern = None
  344| current_subpattern = None

  347|     pp.pprint(blocks)

  527|                     print("        for ({} = 0; {} < {}; {}++) {{".format(item[1], item[1], item[2], item[1]), file=f)

  735|             print("      const vector<index_{}_value_type> &cells = cells_ptr->second;".format(index), file=f)
  736|             print("      for (int _pmg_idx = 0; _pmg_idx < GetSize(cells); _pmg_idx++) {", file=f)
  737|             print("        {} = std::get<0>(cells[_pmg_idx]);".format(block["cell"]), file=f)
  738|             valueidx = 1
  739|             for item in block["setup"]:
  740|                 if item[0] == "slice":
  741|                     print("        const int &{} YS_MAYBE_UNUSED = std::get<{}>(cells[_pmg_idx]);".format(item[1], valueidx), file=f)
  742|                     valueidx += 1
  743|                 if item[0] == "choice":
  744|                     print("        const {} &{} YS_MAYBE_UNUSED = std::get<{}>(cells[_pmg_idx]);".format(item[1], item[2], valueidx), file=f)
  745|                     valueidx += 1
  746|                 if item[0] == "define":
  747|                     print("        const {} &{} YS_MAYBE_UNUSED = std::get<{}>(cells[_pmg_idx]);".format(item[1], item[2], valueidx), file=f)
  748|                     valueidx += 1
  749|             print("        if (blacklist_cells.count({})) continue;".format(block["cell"]), file=f)
  750|             for expr in block["filter"]:
  751|                 print("        if (!({})) continue;".format(expr), file=f)
  752|             if block["semioptional"] or block["genargs"] is not None:
  753|                 print("        found_any_match = true;", file=f)
  754|             for item in block["sets"]:
  755|                 print("        auto _pmg_backup_{} = {};".format(item[0], item[0]), file=f)
  756|                 print("        {} = {};".format(item[0], item[1]), file=f)
  757|             print("        auto rollback_ptr = rollback_cache.insert(make_pair(std::get<0>(cells[_pmg_idx]), recursion));", file=f)
  758|             print("        block_{}(recursion+1);".format(index+1), file=f)
  759|             for item in block["sets"]:
  760|                 print("        {} = _pmg_backup_{};".format(item[0], item[0]), file=f)
  761|             print("        if (rollback_ptr.second)", file=f)
  762|             print("          rollback_cache.erase(rollback_ptr.first);", file=f)
  763|             print("        if (rollback) {", file=f)
  764|             print("          if (rollback != recursion) {{".format(index+1), file=f)
  765|             print("            {} = _pmg_backup_{};".format(block["cell"], block["cell"]), file=f)
  766|             print("            return;", file=f)
  767|             print("          }", file=f)
  768|             print("          rollback = 0;", file=f)
  769|             print("        }", file=f)
  770|             print("      }", file=f)
  771|             print("    }", file=f)
  772| 
  773|             print("", file=f)
  774|             print("    {} = nullptr;".format(block["cell"]), file=f)
  775| 
  776|             if block["optional"]:
  777|                 print("    block_{}(recursion+1);".format(index+1), file=f)
  778| 
  779|             if block["semioptional"]:
  780|                 print("    if (!found_any_match) block_{}(recursion+1);".format(index+1), file=f)
  781| 
  782|             print("    {} = _pmg_backup_{};".format(block["cell"], block["cell"]), file=f)
  783| 
  784|             if block["genargs"] is not None:
  785|                 print("#define finish do { rollback = -1; return; } while(0)", file=f)
  786|                 print("    if (generate_mode && rng(100) < (found_any_match ? {} : {})) {{".format(block["genargs"][1], block["genargs"][0]), file=f)
  787|                 for line in block["gencode"]:
  788|                     print("      " + line, file=f)
  789|                 print("    }", file=f)
  790|                 print("#undef finish", file=f)
  791|         else:
  792|             assert False
  793| 
  794|         current_pattern = None
  795|         print("  }", file=f)
  796|         print("", file=f)
  797| 
  798|     print("};", file=f)
  799| 
  800|     if genhdr:
  801|         print("", file=f)
  802|         print("YOSYS_NAMESPACE_END", file=f)

   22| This generates:
   23| - Wrapper calls for opaque container types

   30| <= 3.12. There is so much f-string use here that the outer quote thing
   31| is a common problem. ``ruff check pyosys/generator.py`` suffices.

   66| @dataclass
   67| class PyosysClass:
   68|     """
   69|     Metadata about classes or structs intended to be wrapped using Pyosys.
   70| 
   71|     :param name: The base name of the class (without extra qualifiers)
   72|     :param ref_only: Whether this class can be copied to Python, or only
   73|         referenced.
   74|     :param string_expr:
   75|         A C++ expression that will be used for the ``__str__`` method in Python.
   76| 
   77|         The object will be available as a const reference with the identifier
   78|         `s`.
   79|     :param hash_expr:
   80|         A C++ expression that will be fed to ``run_hash`` to declare a
   81|         ``__hash__`` method for Python.
   82| 
   83|         The object will be vailable as a const reference with the identifier
   84|         `s`.
   85|     :param denylist: If specified, one or more methods can be excluded from
   86|         wrapping.
   87|     """
   88| 
   89|     name: str
   90|     ref_only: bool = False
   91| 
   92|     # in the format s.(method or property) (or just s)
   93|     string_expr: Optional[str] = None
   94|     hash_expr: Optional[str] = None
   95| 
   96|     denylist: FrozenSet[str] = frozenset({})
   97| 
   98| 
   99| @dataclass
  100| class PyosysHeader:
  101|     """
  102|     :param name: The name of the header, i.e., its relative path to the Yosys root
  103|     :param classes: A list of ``PyosysClass`` classes to be wrapped
  104|     :param enums: A list of enums to be wrapped
  105|     """
  106| 
  107|     name: str
  108|     classes: List[PyosysClass] = field(default_factory=lambda: [])
  109| 
  110|     def __post_init__(self):
  111|         self.classes_by_name = {}
  112|         if classes := self.classes:
  113|             for cls in classes:
  114|                 self.classes_by_name[cls.name] = cls

  231| 
  232| @dataclass(frozen=True)  # hashable
  233| class PyosysType:
  234|     """
  235|     Bit of a hacky object all-around: this is more or less used to encapsulate
  236|     container types so they can be later made opaque using pybind.
  237|     """
  238| 
  239|     base: str
  240|     specialization: Tuple["PyosysType", ...]
  241|     const: bool = False
  242| 
  243|     @classmethod
  244|     def from_type(Self, type_obj, drop_const=False) -> "PyosysType":
  245|         const = hasattr(type_obj, "const") and type_obj.const and not drop_const
  246|         if isinstance(type_obj, Pointer):
  247|             ptr_to = Self.from_type(type_obj.ptr_to)
  248|             return Self("ptr", (ptr_to,), const)
  249|         elif isinstance(type_obj, Reference):
  250|             ref_to = Self.from_type(type_obj.ref_to)
  251|             return Self("ref", (ref_to,), const)
  252|         elif isinstance(type_obj, FunctionType):
  253|             ret_type = Self.from_type(type_obj.return_type)
  254|             param_types = (Self.from_type(p.type) for p in type_obj.parameters)
  255|             return Self("fn", (ret_type, *param_types), False)
  256|         assert isinstance(
  257|             type_obj, Type
  258|         ), f"unexpected c++ type object of type {type(type_obj)}"
  259|         last_segment = type_obj.typename.segments[-1]
  260|         base = last_segment.name
  261|         specialization = tuple()
  262|         if (
  263|             hasattr(last_segment, "specialization")
  264|             and last_segment.specialization is not None
  265|         ):
  266|             for template_arg in last_segment.specialization.args:
  267|                 specialization = (*specialization, Self.from_type(template_arg.arg))
  268|         return Self(base, specialization, const)
  269| 
  270|     def generate_identifier(self):
  271|         title = self.base.title()
  272|         if len(self.specialization) == 0:
  273|             return title
  274| 
  275|         if title == "Dict":
  276|             key, value = self.specialization
  277|             return f"{key.generate_identifier()}To{value.generate_identifier()}{title}"
  278|         elif title == "Fn":
  279|             identifier = self.specialization[0].generate_identifier()
  280|             if identifier == "Void":
  281|                 identifier = ""
  282|             else:
  283|                 identifier += "From"
  284|             identifier += "And".join(
  285|                 p.generate_identifier() for p in self.specialization[1:]
  286|             )
  287|             return identifier
  288| 
  289|         return (
  290|             "".join(spec.generate_identifier() for spec in self.specialization) + title
  291|         )
  292| 
  293|     def generate_cpp_name(self):
  294|         const_prefix = "const " * self.const
  295|         if len(self.specialization) == 0:
  296|             return const_prefix + self.base
  297|         elif self.base == "ptr":
  298|             return const_prefix + f"{self.specialization[0].generate_cpp_name()} *"
  299|         elif self.base == "ref":
  300|             return const_prefix + f"{self.specialization[0].generate_cpp_name()} &"
  301|         elif self.base == "fn":
  302|             param_cpp_names = (s.generate_cpp_name() for s in self.specialization[1:])
  303|             return f"{self.specialization[0].generate_cpp_name()}({','.join(param_cpp_names)})"
  304|         else:
  305|             return (
  306|                 const_prefix
  307|                 + f"{self.base}<{', '.join(spec.generate_cpp_name() for spec in self.specialization)}>"
  308|             )
  309| 
  310| 
  311| class PyosysWrapperGenerator(object):
  312|     def __init__(
  313|         self,
  314|         from_headers: Iterable[PyosysHeader],
  315|         wrapper_stream: io.TextIOWrapper,
  316|         header_stream: io.TextIOWrapper,
  317|     ):
  318|         self.headers = from_headers
  319|         self.f = wrapper_stream
  320|         self.f_inc = header_stream
  321|         self.found_containers: Dict[PyosysType, Any] = {}
  322|         self.class_registry: Dict[str, Tuple[ClassScope, PyosysClass]] = {}
  323| 
  324|     # entry point
  325|     def generate(self):
  326|         tpl = __file_dir__ / "wrappers_tpl.cc"
  327|         preprocessor_opts = self.make_preprocessor_options()
  328|         with open(tpl, encoding="utf8") as f:
  329|             do_line_directive = True
  330|             for i, line in enumerate(f):
  331|                 if do_line_directive:
  332|                     self.f.write(f'#line {i + 1} "{tpl}"\n')
  333|                     do_line_directive = False
  334|                 if "<!-- generated includes -->" in line:
  335|                     for header in self.headers:
  336|                         self.f.write(f'#include "{header.name}"\n')
  337|                     do_line_directive = True
  338|                 elif "<!-- generated pymod-level code -->" in line:
  339|                     for header in self.headers:
  340|                         header_path = __yosys_root__ / header.name
  341|                         parsed = parse_file(header_path, options=preprocessor_opts)
  342|                         global_namespace = parsed.namespace
  343|                         self.process_namespace(header, global_namespace)
  344|                 else:
  345|                     self.f.write(line)
  346| 
  347|         for container, _ in self.found_containers.items():
  348|             identifier = container.generate_identifier()
  349|             print(
  350|                 f"using {identifier} = {container.generate_cpp_name()};",
  351|                 file=self.f_inc,
  352|             )
  353|             print(f"PYBIND11_MAKE_OPAQUE({identifier})", file=self.f_inc)
  354| 
  355|         print(
  356|             f"static void bind_autogenerated_opaque_containers(py::module &m) {{",
  357|             file=self.f_inc,
  358|         )
  359|         for container, _ in self.found_containers.items():
  360|             identifier = container.generate_identifier()
  361|             cxx = container.generate_cpp_name()
  362|             tpl_args = [cxx]
  363|             for spec in container.specialization:
  364|                 tpl_args.append(spec.generate_cpp_name())
  365|             print(
  366|                 f'\tpy::hashlib::bind_{container.base}<{", ".join(tpl_args)}>(m, "{container.generate_identifier()}");',
  367|                 file=self.f_inc,
  368|             )
  369|             print(
  370|                 f"\tpy::implicitly_convertible<py::iterable, {identifier}>();",
  371|                 file=self.f_inc,
  372|             )
  373|         print(f"}}", file=self.f_inc)
  374| 
  375|     # helpers
  376|     def make_preprocessor_options(self):
  377|         py_include = get_paths()["include"]
  378|         preprocessor_bin = shutil.which("clang++") or "g++"
  379|         cxx_std = os.getenv("CXX_STD", "c++20")
  380|         return ParserOptions(
  381|             preprocessor=make_gcc_preprocessor(
  382|                 defines=["_YOSYS_", "YOSYS_ENABLE_PYTHON"],
  383|                 gcc_args=[preprocessor_bin, "-fsyntax-only", f"-std={cxx_std}"],
  384|                 include_paths=[str(__yosys_root__), py_include, pybind11.get_include()],
  385|             ),
  386|         )
  387| 
  388|     @staticmethod
  389|     def find_containers(
  390|         containers: Iterable[str], type_info: Any
  391|     ) -> Dict[PyosysType, Any]:
  392|         if isinstance(type_info, Pointer):
  393|             return PyosysWrapperGenerator.find_containers(containers, type_info.ptr_to)
  394|         if isinstance(type_info, MoveReference):
  395|             return PyosysWrapperGenerator.find_containers(
  396|                 containers, type_info.moveref_to
  397|             )
  398|         if isinstance(type_info, Reference):
  399|             return PyosysWrapperGenerator.find_containers(containers, type_info.ref_to)
  400|         if not isinstance(type_info, Type):
  401|             return {}
  402|         segments = type_info.typename.segments
  403|         containers_found = {}
  404|         for segment in segments:
  405|             if isinstance(segment, FundamentalSpecifier):
  406|                 continue
  407|             if segment.name in containers:
  408|                 containers_found.update(
  409|                     {PyosysType.from_type(type_info, drop_const=True): type_info}
  410|                 )
  411|             if segment.specialization is not None:
  412|                 for arg in segment.specialization.args:
  413|                     sub_containers = PyosysWrapperGenerator.find_containers(
  414|                         containers, arg.arg
  415|                     )
  416|                     containers_found.update(sub_containers)
  417|         return containers_found
  418| 
  419|     @staticmethod
  420|     def find_anonymous_union(cls: ClassScope):
  421|         if cls.class_decl.typename.classkey != "union":
  422|             return None
  423|         for s in cls.class_decl.typename.segments:
  424|             if isinstance(s, AnonymousName):
  425|                 return s
  426|         return None  # named union
  427| 
  428|     @staticmethod
  429|     def get_parameter_types(function: Function) -> str:
  430|         return ", ".join(p.type.format() for p in function.parameters)
  431| 
  432|     def register_containers(self, target: Union[Function, Field, Variable]) -> bool:
  433|         supported = ("dict", "idict", "pool", "set", "vector")
  434|         found = False
  435|         if isinstance(target, Function):
  436|             return_type_containers = self.find_containers(supported, target.return_type)
  437|             found = found or len(return_type_containers)
  438|             self.found_containers.update(return_type_containers)
  439| 
  440|             for parameter in target.parameters:
  441|                 parameter_containers = self.find_containers(supported, parameter.type)
  442|                 found = found or len(parameter_containers)
  443|                 self.found_containers.update(parameter_containers)
  444|         else:
  445|             variable_containers = self.find_containers(supported, target.type)
  446|             found = found or len(variable_containers)
  447|             self.found_containers.update(variable_containers)
  448|         return found
  449| 
  450|     # processors
  451|     def get_overload_cast(
  452|         self, function: Function, class_basename: Optional[str]
  453|     ) -> str:
  454|         is_method = isinstance(function, Method)
  455|         function_return_type = function.return_type.format()
  456|         if class_basename in {"Const","IdString"} and function_return_type in {
  457|             "iterator",
  458|             "const_iterator",
  459|         }:
  460|             # HACK: qualify Const's iterators
  461|             function_return_type = f"{class_basename}::{function_return_type}"
  462| 
  463|         pointer_kind = (
  464|             f"{class_basename}::*" if (is_method and not function.static) else "*"
  465|         )
  466| 
  467|         retval = f"static_cast <"
  468|         retval += function_return_type
  469|         retval += f"({pointer_kind})"
  470|         retval += f"({self.get_parameter_types(function)})"
  471|         if is_method and function.const:
  472|             retval += " const"
  473|         retval += ">"
  474|         retval += "(&"
  475|         if is_method:
  476|             retval += f"{class_basename}::"
  477|         retval += function.name.segments[-1].format()
  478|         retval += ")"
  479|         return retval
  480| 
  481|     def get_definition_args(
  482|         self,
  483|         function: Function,
  484|         class_basename: Optional[str],
  485|         python_name_override: Optional[str] = None,
  486|     ) -> List[str]:
  487|         function_basename = function.name.segments[-1].format()
  488| 
  489|         python_function_basename = python_name_override or keyword_aliases.get(
  490|             function_basename, function_basename
  491|         )
  492| 
  493|         def_args = [f'"{python_function_basename}"']
  494|         def_args.append(self.get_overload_cast(function, class_basename))
  495|         for i, parameter in enumerate(function.parameters):
  496|             name = parameter.name or f"arg{i}"
  497|             parameter_arg = f'py::arg("{name}")'
  498|             if parameter.default is not None:
  499|                 parameter_arg += f" = {parameter.default.format()}"
  500|             def_args.append(parameter_arg)
  501| 
  502|         return def_args
  503| 
  504|     def process_method(self, metadata: PyosysClass, function: Method):
  505|         if (
  506|             function.deleted
  507|             or function.template
  508|             or function.vararg
  509|             or function.access != "public"
  510|             or function.pure_virtual
  511|             or function.destructor
  512|         ):
  513|             return
  514| 
  515|         if any(isinstance(p.type, MoveReference) for p in function.parameters):
  516|             # skip move constructors
  517|             return
  518| 
  519|         if len(function.name.segments) > 1:
  520|             # can't handle, skip
  521|             return
  522| 
  523|         if function.constructor:
  524|             if (
  525|                 not metadata.ref_only
  526|             ):  # ref-only classes should not be constructed from python
  527|                 print(
  528|                     f"\t\t\t.def(py::init<{self.get_parameter_types(function)}>())",
  529|                     file=self.f,
  530|                 )
  531|             return
  532| 
  533|         python_name_override = None
  534|         if function.operator is not None:
  535|             if function.operator == "==":
  536|                 python_name_override = "__eq__"
  537|             elif function.operator == "!=":
  538|                 python_name_override = "__ne__"
  539|             elif function.operator == "<":
  540|                 python_name_override = "__lt__"
  541|             elif function.operator == "[]" and function.const:
  542|                 python_name_override = "__getitem__"
  543|             else:
  544|                 return
  545| 
  546|         self.register_containers(function)
  547| 
  548|         definition_fn = "def"
  549|         if function.static:
  550|             definition_fn = "def_static"
  551| 
  552|         definition_args = self.get_definition_args(
  553|             function, metadata.name, python_name_override
  554|         )
  555| 
  556|         print(
  557|             f"\t\t\t.{definition_fn}({', '.join(definition_args)})",
  558|             file=self.f,
  559|         )
  560| 
  561|     def process_function(self, function: Function):
  562|         if function.deleted or function.template or function.vararg or function.static:
  563|             return
  564| 
  565|         if function.operator is not None:
  566|             # Python doesn't do global operators
  567|             return
  568| 
  569|         if function.name.segments[-1].format() in global_denylist:
  570|             return
  571| 
  572|         self.register_containers(function)
  573| 
  574|         print(
  575|             f"\t\t\tm.def({', '.join(self.get_definition_args(function, None))});",
  576|             file=self.f,
  577|         )
  578| 
  579|     def process_field(self, metadata: PyosysClass, field: Field):
  580|         if field.access != "public":
  581|             return
  582| 
  583|         if field.name is None:
  584|             # anonymous structs and unions
  585|             # unions handled in calling function
  586|             # ASSUMPTION: No anonymous structs in the yosys codebase
  587|             # 			  (they're not in the C++ standard anyway)
  588|             return
  589| 
  590|         unique_ptrs = self.find_containers(("unique_ptr",), field.type)
  591|         if len(unique_ptrs):
  592|             # TODO: figure out how to bridge unique pointers maybe does anyone
  593|             # care
  594|             return
  595| 
  596|         self.register_containers(field)
  597|         rvp = "py::return_value_policy::copy"
  598|         if isinstance(field.type, Pointer):
  599|             rvp = "py::return_value_policy::reference_internal"
  600| 
  601|         definition_fn = f"def_{'readonly' if field.type.const else 'readwrite'}"
  602|         if field.static:
  603|             definition_fn += "_static"
  604| 
  605|         field_python_basename = keyword_aliases.get(field.name, field.name)
  606| 
  607|         def_args = [
  608|             f'"{field_python_basename}"',
  609|             f"&{metadata.name}::{field.name}",
  610|         ]
  611|         def_args.append(rvp)
  612|         print(
  613|             f"\t\t\t.{definition_fn}({', '.join(def_args)})",
  614|             file=self.f,
  615|         )
  616| 
  617|     def process_variable(self, variable: Variable):
  618|         if isinstance(variable.type, Array):
  619|             return
  620| 
  621|         variable_basename = variable.name.segments[-1].format()
  622|         if variable_basename in global_denylist:
  623|             return
  624| 
  625|         self.register_containers(variable)
  626| 
  627|         definition_fn = (
  628|             f"def_{'readonly' if variable.type.const else 'readwrite'}_static"
  629|         )
  630| 
  631|         variable_python_basename = keyword_aliases.get(
  632|             variable_basename, variable_basename
  633|         )
  634|         variable_name = variable.name.format()
  635| 
  636|         print(
  637|             f'\t\t\tglobal_variables.{definition_fn}("{variable_python_basename}", &{variable_name});',
  638|             file=self.f,
  639|         )
  640| 
  641|     def process_class_members(
  642|         self,
  643|         metadata: PyosysClass,
  644|         base_metadata: PyosysClass,
  645|         cls: ClassScope,
  646|         basename: str,
  647|     ):
  648|         for method in cls.methods:
  649|             if method.name.segments[-1].name in base_metadata.denylist:
  650|                 continue
  651|             self.process_method(metadata, method)
  652| 
  653|         visited_anonymous_unions = set()
  654|         for field_ in cls.fields:
  655|             if field_.name in base_metadata.denylist:
  656|                 continue
  657|             self.process_field(metadata, field_)
  658| 
  659|             # Handle anonymous unions
  660|             for subclass in cls.classes:
  661|                 if subclass.class_decl.access != "public":
  662|                     continue
  663|                 if au := self.find_anonymous_union(subclass):
  664|                     if au.id in visited_anonymous_unions:
  665|                         continue
  666|                     visited_anonymous_unions.add(au.id)
  667|                     for subfield in subclass.fields:
  668|                         self.process_field(metadata, subfield)
  669| 
  670|         for base in cls.class_decl.bases:
  671|             if base.access != "public":
  672|                 continue
  673|             name = base.typename.segments[-1].format()
  674|             if processed := self.class_registry.get(name):
  675|                 base_scope, base_metadata = processed
  676|                 self.process_class_members(
  677|                     metadata, base_metadata, base_scope, basename
  678|                 )
  679| 
  680|     def process_class(
  681|         self,
  682|         metadata: PyosysClass,
  683|         cls: ClassScope,
  684|         namespace_components: Tuple[str, ...],
  685|     ):
  686|         pqname: PQName = cls.class_decl.typename
  687|         full_path = list(namespace_components) + [
  688|             segment.format() for segment in pqname.segments
  689|         ]
  690|         basename = full_path.pop()
  691|         self.class_registry[basename] = (cls, metadata)
  692| 
  693|         declaration_namespace = "::".join(full_path)
  694|         tpl_args = [basename]
  695|         if metadata.ref_only:
  696|             tpl_args.append(f"std::unique_ptr<{basename}, py::nodelete>")
  697|         print(
  698|             f'\t\t{{using namespace {declaration_namespace}; py::class_<{", ".join(tpl_args)}>(m, "{basename}")',
  699|             file=self.f,
  700|         )
  701| 
  702|         self.process_class_members(metadata, metadata, cls, basename)
  703| 
  704|         if basename == "Design":
  705|             print(
  706|                 '\t\t\t.def("run_pass", [](Design &s, std::vector<std::string> cmd) { Pass::call(&s, cmd); })',
  707|                 file=self.f,
  708|             )
  709|             print(
  710|                 '\t\t\t.def("run_pass", [](Design &s, std::string cmd) { Pass::call(&s, cmd); })',
  711|                 file=self.f,
  712|             )
  713| 
  714|         if expr := metadata.string_expr:
  715|             print(
  716|                 f'\t\t.def("__str__", [](const {basename} &s) {{ return {expr}; }})',
  717|                 file=self.f,
  718|             )
  719|             print(
  720|                 f'\t\t.def("__repr__", [](const {basename} &s) {{ std::stringstream ss; ss << "<{basename} " << {expr} << ">"; return ss.str(); }})',
  721|                 file=self.f,
  722|             )
  723| 
  724|         if expr := metadata.hash_expr:
  725|             print(
  726|                 f'\t\t.def("__hash__", [](const {basename} &s) {{ return run_hash({expr}); }})',
  727|                 file=self.f,
  728|             )
  729| 
  730|         print(f"\t\t;}}", file=self.f)
  731| 
  732|     def process_enum(
  733|         self,
  734|         enum: EnumDecl,
  735|         namespace_components: Tuple[str, ...],
  736|     ):
  737|         pqname: PQName = enum.typename
  738|         full_path = list(namespace_components) + [
  739|             segment.format() for segment in pqname.segments
  740|         ]
  741|         basename = full_path.pop()
  742| 
  743|         declaration_namespace = "::".join(full_path)
  744|         print(
  745|             f'\t\t{{using namespace {declaration_namespace}; py::native_enum<{basename}>(m, "{basename}", "enum.Enum")',
  746|             file=self.f,
  747|         )
  748|         enum_class = enum.typename.classkey == "enum class"
  749|         for value in enum.values:
  750|             enum_class_qualifier = f"{basename}::" * enum_class
  751|             print(
  752|                 f'\t\t\t.value("{value.name}", {enum_class_qualifier}{value.name})',
  753|                 file=self.f,
  754|             )
  755|         print(f"\t\t\t.finalize();}}", file=self.f)
  756| 
  757|     def process_namespace(
  758|         self,
  759|         header: PyosysHeader,
  760|         ns: NamespaceScope,
  761|         namespace_components: Tuple[str, ...] = (),
  762|     ):
  763|         for name, subns in ns.namespaces.items():
  764|             self.process_namespace(header, subns, (*namespace_components, name))
  765|         if len(namespace_components) and (len(ns.functions) + len(ns.variables)):
  766|             # TODO: Not essential but maybe move namespace usage into
  767|             # process_function for consistency?
  768|             print(
  769|                 f"\t\t{{ using namespace {'::'.join(namespace_components)};",
  770|                 file=self.f,
  771|             )
  772|             for function in ns.functions:
  773|                 self.process_function(function)
  774|             for variable in ns.variables:
  775|                 self.process_variable(variable)
  776|             print(f"\t\t}}", file=self.f)
  777|         for enum in ns.enums:
  778|             self.process_enum(enum, namespace_components)
  779|         for cls in ns.classes:
  780|             pqname = cls.class_decl.typename
  781|             declared_name_str = pqname.segments[-1].format()
  782|             if cls_metadata := header.classes_by_name.get(declared_name_str):
  783|                 self.process_class(cls_metadata, cls, namespace_components)
  784| 
  785| 
  786| keyword_aliases = {
  787|     "False": "False_",
  788|     "None": "None_",
  789|     "True": "True_",
  790|     "and": "and_",
  791|     "as": "as_",
  792|     "assert": "assert_",
  793|     "break": "break_",
  794|     "class": "class_",
  795|     "continue": "continue_",
  796|     "def": "def_",
  797|     "del": "del_",
  798|     "elif": "elif_",
  799|     "else": "else_",
  800|     "except": "except_",
  801|     "for": "for_",
  802|     "from": "from_",
  803|     "global": "global_",
  804|     "if": "if_",
  805|     "import": "import_",
  806|     "in": "in_",
  807|     "is": "is_",
  808|     "lambda": "lambda_",
  809|     "nonlocal": "nonlocal_",
  810|     "not": "not_",
  811|     "or": "or_",
  812|     "pass": "pass_",
  813|     "raise": "raise_",
  814|     "return": "return_",
  815|     "try": "try_",
  816|     "while": "while_",
  817|     "with": "with_",
  818|     "yield": "yield_",
  819| }
  820| 
  821| 
  822| def print_includes():
  823|     for header in pyosys_headers:
  824|         print(header.name)
  825| 
  826| 
  827| if __name__ == "__main__":
  828|     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
  829|     ap.add_argument("--debug", default=0, type=int)
  830|     group = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
  831|     group.add_argument("--print-includes", action="store_true")
  832|     group.add_argument("output", nargs="?")
  833|     ns = ap.parse_args()
  834|     if ns.print_includes:
  835|         print_includes()
  836|         exit(0)
  837| 
  838|     out_path = Path(ns.output)
  839|     out_inc = out_path.parent / (out_path.stem + ".inc.cc")
  840|     with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf8") as f, open(
  841|         out_inc, "w", encoding="utf8"
  842|     ) as inc_f:
  843|         generator = PyosysWrapperGenerator(
  844|             from_headers=pyosys_headers, wrapper_stream=f, header_stream=inc_f
  845|         )
  846|         generator.generate()
