   11| # ANY SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
   12| # WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN
   13| # ACTION OF CONTRACT, NEGLIGENCE OR OTHER TORTIOUS ACTION, ARISING OUT OF
   14| # OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE USE OR PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.
   15| import os
   16| import re
   17| import shlex
   18| import shutil
   19| from pathlib import Path
   20| from setuptools import setup, Extension
   21| 
   22| import pybind11
   23| from pybind11.setup_helpers import build_ext
   24| 
   25| __yosys_root__ = Path(__file__).parent
   26| 
   27| yosys_version_rx = re.compile(r'set\(YOSYS_VERSION "([\w\-\+\.]+)"\)')
   28| 
   29| with open(__yosys_root__ / "CMakeLists.txt", encoding="utf8") as f:
   30|     # Extract and convert + to patch version
   31|     version = yosys_version_rx.search(f.read())[1].replace("+", ".")
   32| 
   33| 
   34| class libyosys_so_ext(Extension):
   35|     def __init__(
   36|         self,
   37|     ) -> None:
   38|         super().__init__(
   39|             "libyosys.so",
   40|             [],
   41|         )
   42| 
   43|         # when iterating locally, you probably want to set this variable
   44|         # to avoid mass rebuilds bec of pybind11's include path changing
   45|         pybind_include = os.getenv("_FORCE_PYBIND_INCLUDE", pybind11.get_include())
   46| 
   47|         self.args = [
   48|             "-DYOSYS_ENABLE_PYOSYS=ON",
   49|             "-DYOSYS_WITHOUT_TCL=ON",
   50|             "-DYOSYS_WITHOUT_READLINE=ON",
   51|             "-DBUILD_TESTING=OFF",
   52|         ]
   53| 
   54|     def custom_build(self, bext: build_ext):
   55|         build_dir = Path(bext.build_temp).resolve() / "yosys"
   56|         cpus = len(os.sched_getaffinity(0)) if hasattr(os, "sched_getaffinity") else (os.cpu_count() or 1)
   57|         quota = Path("/sys/fs/cgroup/cpu.max")
   58|         if quota.exists():
   59|             limit, period = quota.read_text().split()
   60|             if limit != "max":
   61|                 cpus = min(cpus, max(1, int(limit) // int(period)))
   62|         bext.spawn(["cmake", "-S", str(__yosys_root__.resolve()), "-B", str(build_dir),
   63|                     "-G", "Ninja", f"-DPython3_EXECUTABLE={os.sys.executable}",
   64|                     *self.args, *shlex.split(os.getenv("CMAKE_ARGS", ""))])
   65|         bext.spawn(["cmake", "--build", str(build_dir), "--target", "yosys-abc", "-j" + str(cpus)])
   66|         bext.spawn(["cmake", "--build", str(build_dir), "--target", "libyosys", "share", "-j" + str(cpus)])
   67|         ext_fullpath = Path(bext.get_ext_fullpath(self.name))
   68|         build_path = ext_fullpath.parents[1]
   69|         pyosys_path = build_path / "pyosys"
   70|         os.makedirs(pyosys_path, exist_ok=True)
   71| 
   72|         # libyosys.so
   73|         target = pyosys_path / self.name
   74|         shutil.copy(build_dir / self.name, target)
   75|         bext.spawn(["strip", "-S", str(target)])
   76| 
   77|         # yosys-abc
   78|         yosys_abc_target = pyosys_path / "yosys-abc"
   79|         shutil.copy(build_dir / "yosys-abc", yosys_abc_target)
   80|         bext.spawn(["strip", "-S", str(yosys_abc_target)])
   81| 
   82|         # share directory
   83|         share_target = pyosys_path / "share"
   84|         try:
   85|             shutil.rmtree(share_target)
   86|         except FileNotFoundError:
   87|             pass
   88| 
   89|         shutil.copytree(build_dir / "share", share_target)
   90| 
   91| 
   92| class custom_build_ext(build_ext):
   93|     def build_extension(self, ext) -> None:
   94|         if not hasattr(ext, "custom_build"):
   95|             return super().build_extension(ext)
   96|         return ext.custom_build(self)
   97| 
   98| 
   99| with open(__yosys_root__ / "README.md", encoding="utf8") as f:
  100|     long_description = f.read()
  101| 
  102| setup(
  103|     name="pyosys",
  104|     packages=["pyosys"],
  105|     version=version,
  106|     description="Python access to libyosys",
  107|     long_description=long_description,
  108|     long_description_content_type="text/markdown",
  109|     license="MIT",
  110|     classifiers=[
  111|         "Programming Language :: Python :: 3",
  112|         "Intended Audience :: Developers",
  113|         "Operating System :: POSIX :: Linux",
  114|         "Operating System :: MacOS :: MacOS X",
  115|     ],
  116|     python_requires=">=3.8",
  117|     ext_modules=[libyosys_so_ext()],
  118|     cmdclass={
  119|         "build_ext": custom_build_ext,
  120|     },
  121| )

==> techlibs/common/cellhelp.py <==
    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| 
    3| from __future__ import annotations
    4| import fileinput
    5| import json
    6| from pathlib import Path
    7| 
    8| class SimHelper:
    9|     name: str = ""
   10|     title: str = ""
   11|     ports: str = ""
   12|     source: str = ""
   13|     desc: list[str]
   14|     code: list[str]
   15|     group: str = ""
   16|     ver: str = "1"
   17|     tags: list[str]
   18| 
   19|     def __init__(self) -> None:
   20|         self.desc = []
   21|         self.tags = []
   22|     
   23|     def __str__(self) -> str:
   24|         printed_fields = [
   25|             "name", "title", "ports", "source", "desc", "code", "group", "ver",
   26|             "tags",
   27|         ]
   28|         # generate C++ struct
   29|         val = f"cell_help[{json.dumps(self.name)}] = "
   30|         val += "{\n"
   31|         for field in printed_fields:
   32|             field_val = getattr(self, field)
   33|             if isinstance(field_val, list):
   34|                 field_val = "\n".join(field_val)
   35|             field_val = field_val.strip()
   36|             val += f'  {json.dumps(field_val)},\n'
   37|         val += "};\n"
   38|         return val
   39| 
   40| def simcells_reparse(cell: SimHelper):
   41|     # cut manual signature
   42|     cell.desc = cell.desc[3:]
   43| 
   44|     # code-block truth table
   45|     new_desc = []
   46|     indent = ""
   47|     for line in cell.desc:
   48|         if line.startswith("Truth table:"):
   49|             indent = "   "
   50|             new_desc.pop()
   51|             new_desc.extend(["::", ""])
   52|         new_desc.append(indent + line)
   53|     cell.desc = new_desc
   54| 
   55|     # set version
   56|     cell.ver = "2a"
   57| 
   58| simHelper = SimHelper()
   59| 
   60| for line in fileinput.input():
   61|     line = line.rstrip()
   62|     # special comments
   63|     if line.startswith("//-"):
   64|         simHelper.desc.append(line[4:] if len(line) > 4 else "")
   65|     elif line.startswith("//* "):
   66|         _, key, val = line.split(maxsplit=2)
   67|         setattr(simHelper, key, val)
   68|     
   69|     # code parsing
   70|     if line.startswith("module "):
   71|         clean_line = line[7:].replace("\\", "").replace(";", "")
   72|         simHelper.name, simHelper.ports = clean_line.split(maxsplit=1)
   73|         simHelper.code = []
   74|         short_filename = Path(fileinput.filename()).name
   75|         simHelper.source = f'{short_filename}:{fileinput.filelineno()}'
   76|     elif not line.startswith("endmodule"):
   77|         line = "    " + line
   78|     try:
   79|         simHelper.code.append(line.replace("\t", "    "))
   80|     except AttributeError:
   81|         # no module definition, ignore line
   82|         pass
   83|     if line.startswith("endmodule"):
   84|         short_filename = Path(fileinput.filename()).name
   85|         if simHelper.ver == "1" and short_filename == "simcells.v":
   86|             # default simcells parsing
   87|             simcells_reparse(simHelper)
   88| 
   89|         # check help
   90|         if simHelper.desc and simHelper.ver == "1" and short_filename == "simlib.v" and simHelper.desc[1].startswith('    '):
   91|             simHelper.desc.pop(1)
   92| 
   93|         # check group
   94|         assert simHelper.group, f"techlibs/common/{simHelper.source}: {simHelper.name} cell missing group"
   95| 
   96|         # dump
   97|         print(simHelper)
   98|         # new
   99|         simHelper = SimHelper()
  100| 

==> techlibs/gatemate/make_lut_tree_lib.py <==
    1| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    2| 
    3| class FNode:
    4|     def __init__(self, fun, *args):
    5|         self.fun = fun
    6|         self.args = args
    7| 
    8|         if len(self.args) == 0:
    9|             assert fun not in ("BUF", "NOT", "AND", "OR", "XOR", "MUX")
   10| 
   11|         if len(self.args) == 1:
   12|             assert fun in ("BUF", "NOT")
   13| 
   14|         if len(self.args) == 2:
   15|             assert fun in ("AND", "OR", "XOR")
   16| 
   17|         if len(self.args) == 3:
   18|             assert fun in ("MUX")
   19| 
   20|     def __str__(self):
   21|         if len(self.args) == 0:
   22|             return self.fun
   23|         if self.fun == "NOT" and len(self.args[0].args) == 0:
   24|             return "!" + self.args[0].fun
   25|         return self.fun + "(" + ",".join([str(a) for a in self.args]) + ")"
   26| 
   27|     def as_genlib_term(self):
   28|         if len(self.args) == 0:
   29|             return self.fun
   30|         if self.fun == "NOT":
   31|             assert len(self.args[0].args) == 0
   32|             return "!" + self.args[0].fun
   33|         if self.fun == "AND":
   34|             return "(" + self.args[0].as_genlib_term() + "*" + self.args[1].as_genlib_term() + ")"
   35|         if self.fun == "OR":
   36|             return "(" + self.args[0].as_genlib_term() + "+" + self.args[1].as_genlib_term() + ")"
   37|         assert False
   38| 
   39|     def mapMux(self):
   40|         if self.fun == "MUX":
   41|             A, B, C = self.args
   42|             return OR(AND(A, NOT(C)), AND(B, C)).mapMux()
   43|         return FNode(self.fun, *[a.mapMux() for a in self.args])
   44| 
   45|     def mapXor(self):
   46|         if self.fun == "XOR":
   47|             A, B = self.args
   48|             return OR(AND(A, NOT(B)), AND(NOT(A), B)).mapXor()
   49|         return FNode(self.fun, *[a.mapXor() for a in self.args])
   50| 
   51|     def mapNot(self):
   52|         if self.fun == "BUF":
   53|             return self.arg1.mapNot()
   54|         if self.fun == "NOT":
   55|             if self.args[0].fun == "AND":
   56|                 return OR(NOT(self.args[0].args[0]),NOT(self.args[0].args[1])).mapNot()
   57|             if self.args[0].fun == "OR":
   58|                 return AND(NOT(self.args[0].args[0]),NOT(self.args[0].args[1])).mapNot()
   59|             if self.args[0].fun == "NOT":
   60|                 return self.args[0].args[0].mapNot()
   61|         return FNode(self.fun, *[a.mapNot() for a in self.args])
   62| 
   63|     def map(self):
   64|         n = self
   65|         n = n.mapMux()
   66|         n = n.mapXor()
   67|         n = n.mapNot()
   68|         return n
   69| 
   70|     def isInv(self):
   71|         if len(self.args) == 0:
   72|             return False
   73|         if self.fun == "XOR":
   74|             return False
   75|         if self.fun == "NOT":
   76|             return self.args[0].isNonInv()
   77|         for a in self.args:
   78|             if not a.isInv():
   79|                 return False
   80|         return True
   81| 
   82|     def isNonInv(self):
   83|         if len(self.args) == 0:
   84|             return True
   85|         if self.fun == "XOR":
   86|             return False
   87|         if self.fun == "NOT":
   88|             return self.args[0].isInv()
   89|         for a in self.args:
   90|             if not a.isNonInv():
   91|                 return False
   92|         return True
   93| 
   94| A = FNode("A")
   95| B = FNode("B")
   96| C = FNode("C")
   97| D = FNode("D")
   98| E = FNode("E")
   99| 
  100| def BUF(arg): return FNode("BUF", arg)
  101| def NOT(arg): return FNode("NOT", arg)
  102| def AND(arg1, arg2): return FNode("AND", arg1, arg2)
  103| def  OR(arg1, arg2): return FNode( "OR", arg1, arg2)
  104| def XOR(arg1, arg2): return FNode("XOR", arg1, arg2)
  105| def MUX(arg1, arg2, arg3): return FNode("MUX", arg1, arg2, arg3)
  106| 
  107| # Genlib Format:
  108| #
  109| # GATE <cell-name> <cell-area> <cell-logic-function>
  110| #
  111| # PIN <pin-name> <phase> <input-load> <max-load>
  112| #     <rise-block-delay> <rise-fanout-delay>
  113| #     <fall-block-delay> <fall-fanout-delay>
  114| #
  115| # phase:
  116| #     INV, NONINV, or UNKNOWN
  117| #
  118| # cell-logic-function:
  119| #     <output> = <term with *(AND), +(OR), !(NOT)>
  120|

  126| ]

  183|     if name not in ("$__CC4_OO", "$__CC3_O"):

  185|     if name not in ("$__CC4_XX", "$__CC3_X"):

  188| with open("techlibs/gatemate/lut_tree_cells.genlib", "w") as glf:
  189|     def mkGate(name, cost, expr, max_load=9999, block_delay = 10, fanout_delay = 5):
  190|         name = name.replace(" ", "")
  191|         expr = expr.map()
  192| 
  193|         phase = "UNKNOWN"
  194|         if expr.isInv(): phase = "INV"
  195|         if expr.isNonInv(): phase = "NONINV"
  196| 
  197|         print("", file=glf)
  198|         print("GATE %s %d Y=%s;" % (name, cost, expr.as_genlib_term()), file=glf)
  199|         print("PIN * %s 1 %d %d %d %d %d" % (phase, max_load, block_delay, fanout_delay, block_delay, fanout_delay), file=glf)
  200|     print("GATE $__ZERO 0 Y=CONST0;", file=glf)
  201|     print("GATE $__ONE 0 Y=CONST1;", file=glf)
  202|     for name, cost, expr in cells:
  203|         mkGate(name, cost, expr)
  204| 
  205| class LUTTreeNode:
  206|     def __init__(self, name, width, inputs=None):
  207|         self.name = name
  208|         self.width = width
  209|         self.inputs = inputs
  210|     def is_input(self):
  211|         return self.width == 0
  212|     def map(self, expr, params, ports):
  213|         if self.is_input():
  214|             # Input to LUT tree
  215|             if expr is None:
  216|                 ports[self.name] = "" # disconnected input
  217|             else:
  218|                 assert(len(expr.args) == 0)
  219|                 ports[self.name] = expr.fun
  220|             return
  221|         if expr is None:
  222|             # Unused part of tree
  223|             params[self.name] = "4'b0000"
  224|             for i in self.inputs:
  225|                 i.map(None, params, ports)
  226|             return
  227|         elif len(expr.args) == 0:
  228|             # Input to the expression; but not LUT tree
  229|             # Insert a route through
  230|             params[self.name] = "4'b1010"
  231|             self.inputs[0].map(expr, params, ports)
  232|             for i in self.inputs[1:]:
  233|                 i.map(None, params, ports)
  234|             return
  235|         # Map uphill LUTs; uninverting arguments and keeping track of that if needed
  236|         arg_inv = []
  237|         for (i, arg) in zip(self.inputs, expr.args):
  238|             if arg.fun == "NOT":
  239|                 i.map(arg.args[0], params, ports)
  240|                 arg_inv.append(True)
  241|             else:
  242|                 i.map(arg, params, ports)
  243|                 arg_inv.append(False)
  244|         # Determine base init value
  245|         assert self.width == 2
  246|         if expr.fun == "AND":
  247|             init = 0b1000
  248|         elif expr.fun == "OR":
  249|             init = 0b1110
  250|         elif expr.fun == "XOR":
  251|             init = 0b0110
  252|         else:
  253|             assert False, expr.fun
  254|         # Swap bits if init inverted
  255|         swapped_init = 0b0000
  256|         for b in range(4):
  257|             if ((init >> b) & 0x1) == 0: continue
  258|             for i in range(2):
  259|                 if arg_inv[i]:
  260|                     b ^= (1 << i)
  261|             swapped_init |= (1 << b)
  262|         # Set init param
  263|         params[self.name] = "4'b{:04b}".format(swapped_init)
  264| 
  265| def LUT2(name, i0, i1): return LUTTreeNode(name, 2, [i0, i1])
  266| def I(name): return LUTTreeNode(name, 0)
  267| 
  268| lut_prims = {
  269|     "CC_LUT2": LUT2("INIT", I("I0"), I("I1")),
  270|     "CC_L2T4": LUT2(
  271|         "INIT_L10",
  272|         LUT2("INIT_L00", I("I0"), I("I1")),
  273|         LUT2("INIT_L01", I("I2"), I("I3")),
  274|     ),
  275|     "CC_L2T5": LUT2(
  276|         "INIT_L20", I("I4"), LUT2("INIT_L11",
  277|             LUT2("INIT_L02", I("I0"), I("I1")),
  278|             LUT2("INIT_L03", I("I2"), I("I3")),
  279|         )
  280|     )
  281| }
  282| 
  283| with open("techlibs/gatemate/lut_tree_map.v", "w") as vf:
  284|     # Non-automatic rules
  285|     print("""
  286| module \\$__ZERO (output Y); assign Y = 1'b0; endmodule
  287| module \\$__ONE (output Y); assign Y = 1'b1; endmodule
  288| 
  289| module \\$__CC_BUF (input A, output Y); assign Y = A; endmodule
  290| 
  291| module \\$__CC_MUX (input A, B, C, output Y);
  292|     CC_MX2 _TECHMAP_REPLACE_ (
  293|         .D0(A), .D1(B), .S0(C),
  294|         .Y(Y)
  295|     );
  296| endmodule
  297| """, file=vf)
  298|     for name, cost, expr in cells:
  299|         expr = expr.mapMux().mapNot() # Don't map XOR
  300|         if name in ("$__CC_BUF", "$__CC_NOT", "$__CC_MUX"):
  301|             # Special cases
  302|             continue
  303|         if name.startswith("$__CC2_"):
  304|             prim = "CC_LUT2"
  305|         elif name.startswith("$__CC5_") or (name.startswith("$__CC4_") and cost == 12):
  306|             prim = "CC_L2T5"
  307|         else:
  308|             prim = "CC_L2T4"
  309|         ports = {}
  310|         params = {}
  311|         lut_prims[prim].map(expr, params, ports)
  312|         print("", file=vf)
  313|         print("module \\%s (input %s, output Y);" % (name,
  314|             ", ".join(sorted(set(x for x in ports.values() if x)))), file=vf)
  315|         print("    %s #(" % prim, file=vf)
  316|         for k, v in sorted(params.items(), key=lambda x: x[0]):
  317|             print("        .%s(%s)," % (k, v), file=vf)
  318|         print("    ) _TECHMAP_REPLACE_ (", file=vf)
  319|         print("         %s," % ", ".join(".%s(%s)" % (k, v) for k, v in sorted(ports.items(), key=lambda x:x[0])),
  320|             file=vf)
  321|         print("         .O(Y)", file=vf)
  322|         print("    );", file=vf)
  323|         print("endmodule", file=vf)

   19| import sys, re, os, signal, json
   20| import subprocess
   21| if os.name == "posix":
   22|     import resource
   23| from copy import copy
   24| from select import select

   29| 
   30| # This is needed so that the recursive SMT2 S-expression parser
   31| # does not run out of stack frames when parsing large expressions
   32| if os.name == "posix":
   33|     smtio_reclimit = 64 * 1024
   34|     if sys.getrecursionlimit() < smtio_reclimit:
   35|         sys.setrecursionlimit(smtio_reclimit)
   36| 
   37|     current_rlimit_stack = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_STACK)
   38|     if current_rlimit_stack[0] != resource.RLIM_INFINITY:
   39|         smtio_stacksize = 128 * 1024 * 1024
   40|         if os.uname().sysname == "Darwin":
   41|             # MacOS has rather conservative stack limits
   42|             smtio_stacksize = 8 * 1024 * 1024
   43|         if current_rlimit_stack[1] != resource.RLIM_INFINITY:
   44|             smtio_stacksize = min(smtio_stacksize, current_rlimit_stack[1])
   45|         if current_rlimit_stack[0] < smtio_stacksize:
   46|             try:
   47|                 resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_STACK, (smtio_stacksize, current_rlimit_stack[1]))
   48|             except ValueError:
   49|                 # couldn't get more stack, just run with what we have
   50|                 pass
   51| 
   52| 
   53| # currently running solvers (so we can kill them)
   54| running_solvers = dict()
   55| forced_shutdown = False
   56| solvers_index = 0
   57| 
   58| def force_shutdown(signum, frame):
   59|     global forced_shutdown
   60|     if not forced_shutdown:
   61|         forced_shutdown = True
   62|         if signum is not None:
   63|             print("<%s>" % signal.Signals(signum).name)
   64|         for p in running_solvers.values():
   65|             # os.killpg(os.getpgid(p.pid), signal.SIGTERM)
   66|             os.kill(p.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
   67|     sys.exit(1)
   68| 
   69| if os.name == "posix":
   70|     signal.signal(signal.SIGHUP, force_shutdown)
   71| signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, force_shutdown)
   72| signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, force_shutdown)
   73| 
   74| def except_hook(exctype, value, traceback):
   75|     if not forced_shutdown:
   76|         sys.__excepthook__(exctype, value, traceback)
   77|     force_shutdown(None, None)
   78| 
   79| sys.excepthook = except_hook
   80| 
   81| 
   82| def recursion_helper(iteration, *request):
   83|     stack = [iteration(*request)]
   84| 
   85|     while stack:
   86|         top = stack.pop()
   87|         try:
   88|             request = next(top)
   89|         except StopIteration:
   90|             continue
   91| 
   92|         stack.append(top)
   93|         stack.append(iteration(*request))
   94| 
   95| 
   96| hex_dict = {
   97|     "0": "0000", "1": "0001", "2": "0010", "3": "0011",
   98|     "4": "0100", "5": "0101", "6": "0110", "7": "0111",
   99|     "8": "1000", "9": "1001", "A": "1010", "B": "1011",
  100|     "C": "1100", "D": "1101", "E": "1110", "F": "1111",
  101|     "a": "1010", "b": "1011", "c": "1100", "d": "1101",
  102|     "e": "1110", "f": "1111"
  103| }

  125|         self.asize = dict()

  129| class SmtIo:
  130|     def __init__(self, opts=None):
  131|         global solvers_index
  132| 
  133|         self.logic = None
  134|         self.logic_qf = True
  135|         self.logic_ax = True
  136|         self.logic_uf = True
  137|         self.logic_bv = True
  138|         self.logic_dt = False
  139|         self.forall = False
  140|         self.timeout = 0
  141|         self.produce_models = True
  142|         self.recheck = False
  143|         self.smt2cache = [list()]
  144|         self.smt2_options = dict()
  145|         self.smt2_assumptions = dict()
  146|         self.p = None
  147|         self.p_index = solvers_index
  148|         solvers_index += 1
  149| 
  150|         if opts is not None:
  151|             self.logic = opts.logic
  152|             self.solver = opts.solver
  153|             self.solver_opts = opts.solver_opts
  154|             self.debug_print = opts.debug_print
  155|             self.debug_file = opts.debug_file
  156|             self.dummy_file = opts.dummy_file
  157|             self.timeinfo = opts.timeinfo
  158|             self.timeout = opts.timeout
  159|             self.unroll = opts.unroll
  160|             self.noincr = opts.noincr
  161|             self.info_stmts = opts.info_stmts
  162|             self.nocomments = opts.nocomments
  163| 
  164|         else:
  165|             self.solver = "yices"
  166|             self.solver_opts = list()
  167|             self.debug_print = False
  168|             self.debug_file = None
  169|             self.dummy_file = None
  170|             self.timeinfo = os.name != "nt"
  171|             self.timeout = 0
  172|             self.unroll = False
  173|             self.noincr = False
  174|             self.info_stmts = list()
  175|             self.nocomments = False
  176| 
  177|         self.start_time = time()
  178| 
  179|         self.modinfo = dict()
  180|         self.curmod = None
  181|         self.topmod = None
  182|         self.setup_done = False
  183| 
  184|     def __del__(self):
  185|         if self.p is not None and not forced_shutdown:
  186|             os.killpg(os.getpgid(self.p.pid), signal.SIGTERM)
  187|             if running_solvers is not None:
  188|                 del running_solvers[self.p_index]
  189| 
  190|     def setup(self):
  191|         assert not self.setup_done
  192| 
  193|         if self.forall:
  194|             self.unroll = False
  195| 
  196|         if self.solver == "yices":
  197|             if self.forall:
  198|                 self.noincr = True
  199| 
  200|             if self.noincr:
  201|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yices-smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  202|             else:
  203|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yices-smt2', '--incremental'] + self.solver_opts
  204|             if self.timeout != 0:
  205|                 self.popen_vargs.append('-t')
  206|                 self.popen_vargs.append('%d' % self.timeout);
  207| 
  208|         if self.solver == "z3":
  209|             self.popen_vargs = ['z3', '-smt2', '-in'] + self.solver_opts
  210|             if self.timeout != 0:
  211|                 self.popen_vargs.append('-T:%d' % self.timeout);
  212| 
  213|         if self.solver in ["cvc4", "cvc5"]:
  214|             self.recheck = True
  215|             if self.noincr:
  216|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--lang', 'smt2.6' if self.logic_dt else 'smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  217|             else:
  218|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--incremental', '--lang', 'smt2.6' if self.logic_dt else 'smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  219|             if self.timeout != 0:
  220|                 self.popen_vargs.append('--tlimit=%d000' % self.timeout);
  221| 
  222|         if self.solver == "mathsat":
  223|             self.popen_vargs = ['mathsat'] + self.solver_opts
  224|             if self.timeout != 0:
  225|                 print('timeout option is not supported for mathsat.')
  226|                 sys.exit(1)
  227| 
  228|         if self.solver in ["boolector", "bitwuzla"]:
  229|             if self.noincr:
  230|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--smt2'] + self.solver_opts
  231|             else:
  232|                 self.popen_vargs = [self.solver, '--smt2', '-i'] + self.solver_opts
  233|             self.unroll = True
  234|             if self.timeout != 0:
  235|                 print('timeout option is not supported for %s.' % self.solver)
  236|                 sys.exit(1)
  237| 
  238|         if self.solver == "abc":
  239|             if len(self.solver_opts) > 0:
  240|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yosys-abc', '-S', '; '.join(self.solver_opts)]
  241|             else:
  242|                 self.popen_vargs = ['yosys-abc', '-S', '%blast; &sweep -C 5000; &syn4; &cec -s -m -C 2000']
  243|             self.logic_ax = False
  244|             self.unroll = True
  245|             self.noincr = True
  246|             if self.timeout != 0:
  247|                 print('timeout option is not supported for abc.')
  248|                 sys.exit(1)
  249| 
  250|         if self.solver == "dummy":
  251|             assert self.dummy_file is not None
  252|             self.dummy_fd = open(self.dummy_file, "r")
  253|         else:
  254|             if self.dummy_file is not None:
  255|                 self.dummy_fd = open(self.dummy_file, "w")
  256|             if not self.noincr:
  257|                 self.p_open()
  258| 
  259|         if self.unroll:
  260|             assert not self.forall
  261|             self.logic_uf = False
  262|             self.unroll_idcnt = 0
  263|             self.unroll_buffer = ""
  264|             self.unroll_level = 0
  265|             self.unroll_sorts = set()
  266|             self.unroll_objs = set()
  267|             self.unroll_decls = dict()
  268|             self.unroll_cache = dict()
  269|             self.unroll_stack = list()
  270| 
  271|         if self.logic is None:
  272|             self.logic = ""
  273|             if self.logic_qf: self.logic += "QF_"
  274|             if self.logic_ax: self.logic += "A"
  275|             if self.logic_uf: self.logic += "UF"
  276|             if self.logic_bv: self.logic += "BV"
  277|             if self.logic_dt: self.logic = "ALL"
  278|             if self.solver == "yices" and self.forall: self.logic = "BV"
  279| 
  280|         self.setup_done = True
  281| 
  282|         for stmt in self.info_stmts:
  283|             self.write(stmt)
  284| 
  285|         if self.produce_models:
  286|             self.write("(set-option :produce-models true)")
  287| 
  288|         #See the SMT-LIB Standard, Section 4.1.7
  289|         modestart_options = [":global-declarations", ":interactive-mode", ":produce-assertions", ":produce-assignments", ":produce-models", ":produce-proofs", ":produce-unsat-assumptions", ":produce-unsat-cores", ":random-seed"]
  290|         for key, val in self.smt2_options.items():
  291|             if key in modestart_options:
  292|                 self.write("(set-option {} {})".format(key, val))
  293| 
  294|         self.write("(set-logic %s)" % self.logic)
  295| 
  296|         if self.forall and self.solver == "yices":
  297|             self.write("(set-option :yices-ef-max-iters 1000000000)")
  298| 
  299|         for key, val in self.smt2_options.items():
  300|             if key not in modestart_options:
  301|                 self.write("(set-option {} {})".format(key, val))
  302| 
  303|     def timestamp(self):
  304|         secs = int(time() - self.start_time)
  305|         return "## %3d:%02d:%02d " % (secs // (60*60), (secs // 60) % 60, secs % 60)
  306| 
  307|     def replace_in_stmt(self, stmt, pat, repl):
  308|         if stmt == pat:
  309|             return repl
  310| 
  311|         if isinstance(stmt, list):
  312|             return [self.replace_in_stmt(s, pat, repl) for s in stmt]
  313| 
  314|         return stmt
  315| 
  316|     def unroll_stmt(self, stmt):
  317|         result = []
  318|         recursion_helper(self._unroll_stmt_into, stmt, result)
  319|         return result.pop()
  320| 
  321|     def _unroll_stmt_into(self, stmt, output, depth=128):
  322|         if not isinstance(stmt, list):
  323|             output.append(stmt)
  324|             return
  325| 
  326|         new_stmt = []
  327|         for s in stmt:
  328|             if depth:
  329|                 yield from self._unroll_stmt_into(s, new_stmt, depth - 1)
  330|             else:
  331|                 yield s, new_stmt
  332|         stmt = new_stmt
  333| 
  334|         if len(stmt) >= 2 and not isinstance(stmt[0], list) and stmt[0] in self.unroll_decls:
  335|             assert stmt[1] in self.unroll_objs
  336| 
  337|             key = tuple(stmt)
  338|             if key not in self.unroll_cache:
  339|                 decl = copy(self.unroll_decls[key[0]])
  340| 
  341|                 self.unroll_cache[key] = "|UNROLL#%d|" % self.unroll_idcnt
  342|                 decl[1] = self.unroll_cache[key]
  343|                 self.unroll_idcnt += 1
  344| 
  345|                 if decl[0] == "declare-fun":
  346|                     if isinstance(decl[3], list) or decl[3] not in self.unroll_sorts:
  347|                         self.unroll_objs.add(decl[1])
  348|                         decl[2] = list()
  349|                     else:
  350|                         self.unroll_objs.add(decl[1])
  351|                         decl = list()
  352| 
  353|                 elif decl[0] == "define-fun":
  354|                     arg_index = 1
  355|                     for arg_name, arg_sort in decl[2]:
  356|                         decl[4] = self.replace_in_stmt(decl[4], arg_name, key[arg_index])
  357|                         arg_index += 1
  358|                     decl[2] = list()
  359| 
  360|                 if len(decl) > 0:
  361|                     tmp = []
  362|                     if depth:
  363|                         yield from self._unroll_stmt_into(decl, tmp, depth - 1)
  364|                     else:
  365|                         yield decl, tmp
  366| 
  367|                     decl = tmp.pop()
  368|                     self.write(self.unparse(decl), unroll=False)
  369| 
  370|             output.append(self.unroll_cache[key])
  371|             return
  372| 
  373|         output.append(stmt)
  374| 
  375|     def p_thread_main(self):
  376|         while True:
  377|             data = self.p.stdout.readline().decode("utf-8")
  378|             if data == "": break
  379|             self.p_queue.put(data)
  380|         self.p_queue.put("")
  381|         self.p_running = False
  382| 
  383|     def p_open(self):
  384|         assert self.p is None
  385|         try:
  386|             self.p = subprocess.Popen(self.popen_vargs, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  387|         except FileNotFoundError:
  388|             print("%s SMT Solver '%s' not found in path." % (self.timestamp(), self.popen_vargs[0]), flush=True)
  389|             sys.exit(1)
  390|         running_solvers[self.p_index] = self.p
  391|         self.p_running = True
  392|         self.p_next = None
  393|         self.p_queue = Queue()
  394|         self.p_thread = Thread(target=self.p_thread_main)
  395|         self.p_thread.start()
  396| 
  397|     def p_write(self, data, flush):
  398|         assert self.p is not None
  399|         self.p.stdin.write(bytes(data, "utf-8"))
  400|         if flush: self.p.stdin.flush()
  401| 
  402|     def p_read(self):
  403|         assert self.p is not None
  404|         if self.p_next is not None:
  405|             data = self.p_next
  406|             self.p_next = None
  407|             return data
  408|         if not self.p_running:
  409|             return ""
  410|         return self.p_queue.get()
  411| 
  412|     def p_poll(self, timeout=0.1):
  413|         assert self.p is not None
  414|         assert self.p_running
  415|         if self.p_next is not None:
  416|             return False
  417|         try:
  418|             self.p_next = self.p_queue.get(True, timeout)
  419|             return False
  420|         except Empty:
  421|             return True
  422| 
  423|     def p_close(self):
  424|         assert self.p is not None
  425|         self.p.stdin.close()
  426|         self.p_thread.join()
  427|         assert not self.p_running
  428|         del running_solvers[self.p_index]
  429|         self.p = None
  430|         self.p_next = None
  431|         self.p_queue = None
  432|         self.p_thread = None
  433| 
  434|     def write(self, stmt, unroll=True):
  435|         if stmt.startswith(";"):
  436|             self.info(stmt)
  437|             if not self.setup_done:
  438|                 self.info_stmts.append(stmt)
  439|                 return
  440|         elif not self.setup_done:
  441|             self.setup()
  442| 
  443|         stmt = stmt.strip()
  444| 
  445|         if self.nocomments or self.unroll:
  446|             stmt = re.sub(r" *;.*", "", stmt)
  447|             if stmt == "": return
  448| 
  449|         recheck = None
  450| 
  451|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  452|             if self.noincr:
  453|                 # Don't close the solver yet, if we're just unrolling definitions
  454|                 # required for a (get-...) statement
  455|                 if self.p is not None and not stmt.startswith("(get-") and unroll:
  456|                     self.p_close()
  457| 
  458|         if unroll and self.unroll:
  459|             s = re.sub(r"\|[^|]*\|", "", stmt)
  460|             self.unroll_level += s.count("(") - s.count(")")
  461|             if self.unroll_level > 0:
  462|                 self.unroll_buffer += stmt
  463|                 self.unroll_buffer += " "
  464|                 return
  465|             else:
  466|                 stmt = self.unroll_buffer + stmt
  467|                 self.unroll_buffer = ""
  468| 
  469|             s = self.parse(stmt)
  470| 
  471|             if self.recheck and s and s[0].startswith("get-"):
  472|                 recheck = self.unroll_idcnt
  473| 
  474|             if self.debug_print:
  475|                 print("-> %s" % s)
  476| 
  477|             if len(s) == 3 and s[0] == "declare-sort" and s[2] == "0":
  478|                 self.unroll_sorts.add(s[1])
  479|                 return
  480| 
  481|             elif len(s) == 4 and s[0] == "declare-fun" and s[2] == [] and s[3] in self.unroll_sorts:
  482|                 self.unroll_objs.add(s[1])
  483|                 return
  484| 
  485|             elif len(s) >= 4 and s[0] == "declare-fun":
  486|                 for arg_sort in s[2]:
  487|                     if arg_sort in self.unroll_sorts:
  488|                         self.unroll_decls[s[1]] = s
  489|                         return
  490| 
  491|             elif len(s) >= 4 and s[0] == "define-fun":
  492|                 for arg_name, arg_sort in s[2]:
  493|                     if arg_sort in self.unroll_sorts:
  494|                         self.unroll_decls[s[1]] = s
  495|                         return
  496| 
  497|             stmt = self.unparse(self.unroll_stmt(s))
  498| 
  499|             if recheck is not None and recheck != self.unroll_idcnt:
  500|                 self.check_sat(["sat"])
  501| 
  502|             if stmt == "(push 1)":
  503|                 self.unroll_stack.append((
  504|                     copy(self.unroll_sorts),
  505|                     copy(self.unroll_objs),
  506|                     copy(self.unroll_decls),
  507|                     copy(self.unroll_cache),
  508|                 ))
  509| 
  510|             if stmt == "(pop 1)":
  511|                 self.unroll_sorts, self.unroll_objs, self.unroll_decls, self.unroll_cache = self.unroll_stack.pop()
  512| 
  513|         if self.debug_print:
  514|             print("> %s" % stmt)
  515| 
  516|         if self.debug_file:
  517|             print(stmt, file=self.debug_file)
  518|             self.debug_file.flush()
  519| 
  520|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  521|             if self.noincr:
  522|                 if stmt == "(push 1)":
  523|                     self.smt2cache.append(list())
  524|                 elif stmt == "(pop 1)":
  525|                     self.smt2cache.pop()
  526|                 else:
  527|                     if self.p is not None:
  528|                         self.p_write(stmt + "\n", True)
  529|                     self.smt2cache[-1].append(stmt)
  530|             else:
  531|                 self.p_write(stmt + "\n", True)
  532| 
  533|     def info(self, stmt):
  534|         if not stmt.startswith("; yosys-smt2-"):
  535|             return
  536| 
  537|         fields = stmt.split()
  538| 
  539|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-solver-option":
  540|             self.smt2_options[fields[2]] = fields[3]
  541| 
  542|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-nomem":
  543|             if self.logic is None:
  544|                 self.logic_ax = False
  545| 
  546|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-nobv":
  547|             if self.logic is None:
  548|                 self.logic_bv = False
  549| 
  550|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-stdt":
  551|             if self.logic is None:
  552|                 self.logic_dt = True
  553| 
  554|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-forall":
  555|             if self.logic is None:
  556|                 self.logic_qf = False
  557|             self.forall = True
  558| 
  559|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-module":
  560|             self.curmod = fields[2]
  561|             self.modinfo[self.curmod] = SmtModInfo()
  562| 
  563|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-cell":
  564|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].cells[fields[3]] = fields[2]
  565| 
  566|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-topmod":
  567|             self.topmod = fields[2]
  568| 
  569|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-input":
  570|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].inputs.add(fields[2])
  571|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  572| 
  573|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-output":
  574|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].outputs.add(fields[2])
  575|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  576| 
  577|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-register":
  578|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].registers.add(fields[2])
  579|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  580| 
  581|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-memory":
  582|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].memories[fields[2]] = (int(fields[3]), int(fields[4]), int(fields[5]), int(fields[6]), fields[7] == "async")
  583| 
  584|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-wire":
  585|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wires.add(fields[2])
  586|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].wsize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  587| 
  588|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-clock":
  589|             for edge in fields[3:]:
  590|                 if fields[2] not in self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks:
  591|                     self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] = edge
  592|                 elif self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] != edge:
  593|                     self.modinfo[self.curmod].clocks[fields[2]] = "event"
  594| 
  595|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-assert":
  596|             if len(fields) > 4:
  597|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].asserts["%s_a %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  598|             else:
  599|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].asserts["%s_a %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  600| 
  601|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-cover":
  602|             if len(fields) > 4:
  603|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].covers["%s_c %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  604|             else:
  605|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].covers["%s_c %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  606| 
  607|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-assume":
  608|             if len(fields) > 4:
  609|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].assumes["%s_u %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = f'{fields[4]} ({fields[3]})'
  610|             else:
  611|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].assumes["%s_u %s" % (self.curmod, fields[2])] = fields[3]
  612| 
  613|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-maximize":
  614|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].maximize.add(fields[2])
  615| 
  616|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-minimize":
  617|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].minimize.add(fields[2])
  618| 
  619|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-anyconst":
  620|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].anyconsts[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  621|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  622| 
  623|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-anyseq":
  624|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].anyseqs[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  625|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  626| 
  627|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-allconst":
  628|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].allconsts[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  629|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  630| 
  631|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-allseq":
  632|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].allseqs[fields[2]] = (fields[4], None if len(fields) <= 5 else fields[5])
  633|             self.modinfo[self.curmod].asize[fields[2]] = int(fields[3])
  634| 
  635|         if fields[1] == "yosys-smt2-witness":
  636|             data = json.loads(stmt.split(None, 2)[2])
  637|             if data.get("type") in ["cell", "mem", "posedge", "negedge", "input", "reg", "init", "seq", "blackbox"]:
  638|                 self.modinfo[self.curmod].witness.append(data)
  639| 
  640|     def hiernets(self, top, regs_only=False):
  641|         def hiernets_worker(nets, mod, cursor):
  642|             for netname in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].wsize.keys()):
  643|                 if not regs_only or netname in self.modinfo[mod].registers:
  644|                     nets.append(cursor + [netname])
  645|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  646|                 hiernets_worker(nets, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  647| 
  648|         nets = list()
  649|         hiernets_worker(nets, top, [])
  650|         return nets
  651| 
  652|     def hieranyconsts(self, top):
  653|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  654|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].anyconsts.items()):
  655|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  656|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  657|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  658|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  659| 
  660|         results = list()
  661|         worker(results, top, [])
  662|         return results
  663| 
  664|     def hieranyseqs(self, top):
  665|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  666|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].anyseqs.items()):
  667|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  668|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  669|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  670|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  671| 
  672|         results = list()
  673|         worker(results, top, [])
  674|         return results
  675| 
  676|     def hierallconsts(self, top):
  677|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  678|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].allconsts.items()):
  679|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  680|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  681|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  682|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  683| 
  684|         results = list()
  685|         worker(results, top, [])
  686|         return results
  687| 
  688|     def hierallseqs(self, top):
  689|         def worker(results, mod, cursor):
  690|             for name, value in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].allseqs.items()):
  691|                 width = self.modinfo[mod].asize[name]
  692|                 results.append((cursor, name, value[0], value[1], width))
  693|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  694|                 worker(results, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  695| 
  696|         results = list()
  697|         worker(results, top, [])
  698|         return results
  699| 
  700|     def hiermems(self, top):
  701|         def hiermems_worker(mems, mod, cursor):
  702|             for memname in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].memories.keys()):
  703|                 mems.append(cursor + [memname])
  704|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  705|                 hiermems_worker(mems, celltype, cursor + [cellname])
  706| 
  707|         mems = list()
  708|         hiermems_worker(mems, top, [])
  709|         return mems
  710| 
  711|     def hierwitness(self, top, allregs=False, blackbox=True):
  712|         init_witnesses = []
  713|         seq_witnesses = []
  714|         clk_witnesses = []
  715|         mem_witnesses = []
  716| 
  717|         def absolute(path, cursor, witness):
  718|             return {
  719|                 **witness,
  720|                 "path": path + tuple(witness["path"]),
  721|                 "smtpath": cursor + [witness["smtname"]],
  722|             }
  723| 
  724|         for witness in self.modinfo[top].witness:
  725|             if witness["type"] == "input":
  726|                 seq_witnesses.append(absolute((), [], witness))
  727|             if witness["type"] in ("posedge", "negedge"):
  728|                 clk_witnesses.append(absolute((), [], witness))
  729| 
  730|         init_types = ["init"]
  731|         if allregs:
  732|             init_types.append("reg")
  733| 
  734|         seq_types = ["seq"]
  735|         if blackbox:
  736|             seq_types.append("blackbox")
  737| 
  738|         def worker(mod, path, cursor):
  739|             cell_paths = {}
  740|             for witness in self.modinfo[mod].witness:
  741|                 if witness["type"] in init_types:
  742|                     init_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  743|                 if witness["type"] in seq_types:
  744|                     seq_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  745|                 if witness["type"] == "mem":
  746|                     if allregs and not witness["rom"]:
  747|                         width, size = witness["width"], witness["size"]
  748|                         witness = {**witness, "uninitialized": [{"width": width * size, "offset": 0}]}
  749|                     if not witness["uninitialized"]:
  750|                         continue
  751| 
  752|                     mem_witnesses.append(absolute(path, cursor, witness))
  753|                 if witness["type"] == "cell":
  754|                     cell_paths[witness["smtname"]] = tuple(witness["path"])
  755| 
  756|             for cellname, celltype in sorted(self.modinfo[mod].cells.items()):
  757|                 worker(celltype, path + cell_paths.get(cellname, ("?" + cellname,)), cursor + [cellname])
  758| 
  759|         worker(top, (), [])
  760|         return init_witnesses, seq_witnesses, clk_witnesses, mem_witnesses
  761| 
  762|     def read(self):
  763|         stmt = []
  764|         count_brackets = 0
  765| 
  766|         while True:
  767|             if self.solver == "dummy":
  768|                 line = self.dummy_fd.readline().strip()
  769|             else:
  770|                 line = self.p_read().strip()
  771|                 if self.dummy_file is not None:
  772|                     self.dummy_fd.write(line + "\n")
  773| 
  774|             count_brackets += line.count("(")
  775|             count_brackets -= line.count(")")
  776|             stmt.append(line)
  777| 
  778|             if self.debug_print:
  779|                 print("< %s" % line)
  780|             if count_brackets == 0:
  781|                 break
  782|             if self.solver != "dummy" and self.p.poll():
  783|                 print("%s Solver terminated unexpectedly: %s" % (self.timestamp(), "".join(stmt)), flush=True)
  784|                 sys.exit(1)
  785| 
  786|         stmt = "".join(stmt)
  787|         if stmt.startswith("(error"):
  788|             print("%s Solver Error: %s" % (self.timestamp(), stmt), flush=True)
  789|             if self.solver != "dummy":
  790|                 self.p_close()
  791|             sys.exit(1)
  792| 
  793|         return stmt
  794| 
  795|     def check_sat(self, expected=["sat", "unsat", "unknown", "timeout", "interrupted"]):
  796|         if self.smt2_assumptions:
  797|             assume_exprs = " ".join(self.smt2_assumptions.values())
  798|             check_stmt = f"(check-sat-assuming ({assume_exprs}))"
  799|         else:
  800|             check_stmt = "(check-sat)"
  801|         if self.debug_print:
  802|             print(f"> {check_stmt}")
  803|         if self.debug_file and not self.nocomments:
  804|             print("; running check-sat..", file=self.debug_file)
  805|             self.debug_file.flush()
  806| 
  807|         if self.solver != "dummy":
  808|             if self.noincr:
  809|                 if self.p is not None:
  810|                     self.p_close()
  811|                 self.p_open()
  812|                 for cache_ctx in self.smt2cache:
  813|                     for cache_stmt in cache_ctx:
  814|                         self.p_write(cache_stmt + "\n", False)
  815| 
  816|             self.p_write(f"{check_stmt}\n", True)
  817| 
  818|             if self.timeinfo:
  819|                 i = 0
  820|                 s = r"/-\|"
  821| 
  822|                 count = 0
  823|                 num_bs = 0
  824|                 while self.p_poll():
  825|                     count += 1
  826| 
  827|                     if count < 25:
  828|                         continue
  829| 
  830|                     if count % 10 == 0 or count == 25:
  831|                         secs = count // 10
  832| 
  833|                         if secs < 60:
  834|                             m = "(%d seconds)" % secs
  835|                         elif secs < 60*60:
  836|                             m = "(%d seconds -- %d:%02d)" % (secs, secs // 60, secs % 60)
  837|                         else:
  838|                             m = "(%d seconds -- %d:%02d:%02d)" % (secs, secs // (60*60), (secs // 60) % 60, secs % 60)
  839| 
  840|                         print("%s %s %c" % ("\b \b" * num_bs, m, s[i]), end="", file=sys.stderr)
  841|                         num_bs = len(m) + 3
  842| 
  843|                     else:
  844|                         print("\b" + s[i], end="", file=sys.stderr)
  845| 
  846|                     sys.stderr.flush()
  847|                     i = (i + 1) % len(s)
  848| 
  849|                 if num_bs != 0:
  850|                     print("\b \b" * num_bs, end="", file=sys.stderr)
  851|                     sys.stderr.flush()
  852| 
  853|             else:
  854|                 count = 0
  855|                 while self.p_poll(60):
  856|                     count += 1
  857|                     msg = None
  858| 
  859|                     if count == 1:
  860|                         msg = "1 minute"
  861| 
  862|                     elif count in [5, 10, 15, 30]:
  863|                         msg = "%d minutes" % count
  864| 
  865|                     elif count == 60:
  866|                         msg = "1 hour"
  867| 
  868|                     elif count % 60 == 0:
  869|                         msg = "%d hours" % (count // 60)
  870| 
  871|                     if msg is not None:
  872|                         print("%s waiting for solver (%s)" % (self.timestamp(), msg), flush=True)
  873| 
  874|         if self.forall:
  875|             result = self.read()
  876|             while result not in ["sat", "unsat", "unknown", "timeout", "interrupted", ""]:
  877|                 print("%s %s: %s" % (self.timestamp(), self.solver, result))
  878|                 result = self.read()
  879|         else:
  880|             result = self.read()
  881| 
  882|         if self.debug_file:
  883|             print("(set-info :status %s)" % result, file=self.debug_file)
  884|             print(check_stmt, file=self.debug_file)
  885|             self.debug_file.flush()
  886| 
  887|         if result not in expected:
  888|             if result == "":
  889|                 print("%s Unexpected EOF response from solver." % (self.timestamp()), flush=True)
  890|             else:
  891|                 print("%s Unexpected response from solver: %s" % (self.timestamp(), result), flush=True)
  892|             if self.solver != "dummy":
  893|                 self.p_close()
  894|             sys.exit(1)
  895| 
  896|         return result
  897| 
  898|     def parse(self, stmt):
  899|         def worker(stmt, cursor=0):
  900|             while stmt[cursor] in [" ", "\t", "\r", "\n"]:
  901|                 cursor += 1
  902| 
  903|             if stmt[cursor] == '(':
  904|                 expr = []
  905|                 cursor += 1
  906|                 while stmt[cursor] != ')':
  907|                     el, cursor = worker(stmt, cursor)
  908|                     expr.append(el)
  909|                 return expr, cursor+1
  910| 
  911|             if stmt[cursor] == '|':
  912|                 expr = "|"
  913|                 cursor += 1
  914|                 while stmt[cursor] != '|':
  915|                     expr += stmt[cursor]
  916|                     cursor += 1
  917|                 expr += "|"
  918|                 return expr, cursor+1
  919| 
  920|             expr = ""
  921|             while stmt[cursor] not in ["(", ")", "|", " ", "\t", "\r", "\n"]:
  922|                 expr += stmt[cursor]
  923|                 cursor += 1
  924|             return expr, cursor
  925|         return worker(stmt)[0]
  926| 
  927|     def unparse(self, stmt):
  928|         if isinstance(stmt, list):
  929|             return "(" + " ".join([self.unparse(s) for s in stmt]) + ")"
  930|         return stmt
  931| 
  932|     def bv2hex(self, v):
  933|         h = ""
  934|         v = self.bv2bin(v)
  935|         while len(v) > 0:
  936|             d = 0
  937|             if len(v) > 0 and v[-1] == "1": d += 1
  938|             if len(v) > 1 and v[-2] == "1": d += 2
  939|             if len(v) > 2 and v[-3] == "1": d += 4
  940|             if len(v) > 3 and v[-4] == "1": d += 8
  941|             h = hex(d)[2:] + h
  942|             if len(v) < 4: break
  943|             v = v[:-4]
  944|         return h
  945| 
  946|     def bv2bin(self, v):
  947|         if type(v) is list and len(v) == 3 and v[0] == "_" and v[1].startswith("bv"):
  948|             x, n = int(v[1][2:]), int(v[2])
  949|             return "".join("1" if (x & (1 << i)) else "0" for i in range(n-1, -1, -1))
  950|         if v == "true": return "1"
  951|         if v == "false": return "0"
  952|         if v.startswith("#b"):
  953|             return v[2:]
  954|         if v.startswith("#x"):
  955|             return "".join(hex_dict.get(x) for x in v[2:])
  956|         assert False
  957| 
  958|     def bv2int(self, v):
  959|         return int(self.bv2bin(v), 2)
  960| 
  961|     def get_raw_unsat_assumptions(self):
  962|         self.write("(get-unsat-assumptions)")
  963|         exprs = set(self.unparse(part) for part in self.parse(self.read()))
  964|         unsat_assumptions = []
  965|         for key, value in self.smt2_assumptions.items():
  966|             # normalize expression
  967|             value = self.unparse(self.parse(value))
  968|             if value in exprs:
  969|                 exprs.remove(value)
  970|                 unsat_assumptions.append(key)
  971|         return unsat_assumptions
  972| 
  973|     def get_unsat_assumptions(self, minimize=False):
  974|         if not minimize:
  975|             return self.get_raw_unsat_assumptions()
  976|         required_assumptions = {}
  977| 
  978|         while True:
  979|             candidate_assumptions = {}
  980|             for key in self.get_raw_unsat_assumptions():
  981|                 if key not in required_assumptions:
  982|                     candidate_assumptions[key] = self.smt2_assumptions[key]
  983| 
  984|             while candidate_assumptions:
  985| 
  986|                 candidate_key, candidate_assume = candidate_assumptions.popitem()
  987| 
  988|                 self.smt2_assumptions = {}
  989|                 for key, assume in candidate_assumptions.items():
  990|                     self.smt2_assumptions[key] = assume
  991|                 for key, assume in required_assumptions.items():
  992|                     self.smt2_assumptions[key] = assume
  993|                 result = self.check_sat()
  994| 
  995|                 if result == 'unsat':
  996|                     candidate_assumptions = None
  997|                 else:
  998|                     required_assumptions[candidate_key] = candidate_assume
  999| 
 1000|             if candidate_assumptions is not None:
 1001|                 return list(required_assumptions)
 1002| 
 1003|     def get(self, expr):
 1004|         self.write("(get-value (%s))" % (expr))
 1005|         return self.parse(self.read())[0][1]
 1006| 
 1007|     def get_list(self, expr_list):
 1008|         if len(expr_list) == 0:
 1009|             return []
 1010|         self.write("(get-value (%s))" % " ".join(expr_list))
 1011|         return [n[1] for n in self.parse(self.read()) if n]
 1012| 
 1013|     def get_path(self, mod, path):
 1014|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1015|         path = path.replace("\\", "/").split(".")
 1016| 
 1017|         for i in range(len(path)-1):
 1018|             first = ".".join(path[0:i+1])
 1019|             second = ".".join(path[i+1:])
 1020| 
 1021|             if first in self.modinfo[mod].cells:
 1022|                 nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[first]
 1023|                 return [first] + self.get_path(nextmod, second)
 1024| 
 1025|         return [".".join(path)]
 1026| 
 1027|     def net_expr(self, mod, base, path):
 1028|         if len(path) == 0:
 1029|             return base
 1030| 
 1031|         if len(path) == 1:
 1032|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1033|             if path[0] == "":
 1034|                 return base
 1035|             if isinstance(path[0], int):
 1036|                 return "(|%s#%d| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1037|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells:
 1038|                 return "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1039|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].wsize:
 1040|                 return "(|%s_n %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1041|             if path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].memories:
 1042|                 return "(|%s_m %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1043|             assert 0
 1044| 
 1045|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1046|         assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1047| 
 1048|         nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[path[0]]
 1049|         nextbase = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1050|         return self.net_expr(nextmod, nextbase, path[1:])
 1051| 
 1052|     def witness_net_expr(self, mod, base, witness):
 1053|         net = self.net_expr(mod, base, witness["smtpath"])
 1054|         is_bool = self.net_width(mod, witness["smtpath"]) == 1
 1055|         if is_bool:
 1056|             assert witness["width"] == 1
 1057|             assert witness["smtoffset"] == 0
 1058|             return net
 1059|         return "((_ extract %d %d) %s)" % (witness["smtoffset"] + witness["width"] - 1, witness["smtoffset"], net)
 1060| 
 1061|     def net_width(self, mod, net_path):
 1062|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1063|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1064|             assert net_path[i] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1065|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1066| 
 1067|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1068|         if isinstance(net_path[-1], int):
 1069|             return None
 1070|         assert net_path[-1] in self.modinfo[mod].wsize
 1071|         return self.modinfo[mod].wsize[net_path[-1]]
 1072| 
 1073|     def net_clock(self, mod, net_path):
 1074|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1075|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1076|             assert net_path[i] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1077|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1078| 
 1079|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1080|         if net_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].clocks:
 1081|             return None
 1082|         return self.modinfo[mod].clocks[net_path[-1]]
 1083| 
 1084|     def net_exists(self, mod, net_path):
 1085|         for i in range(len(net_path)-1):
 1086|             if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1087|             if net_path[i] not in self.modinfo[mod].cells: return False
 1088|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[net_path[i]]
 1089| 
 1090|         if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1091|         if net_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].wsize: return False
 1092|         return True
 1093| 
 1094|     def mem_exists(self, mod, mem_path):
 1095|         for i in range(len(mem_path)-1):
 1096|             if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1097|             if mem_path[i] not in self.modinfo[mod].cells: return False
 1098|             mod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[mem_path[i]]
 1099| 
 1100|         if mod not in self.modinfo: return False
 1101|         if mem_path[-1] not in self.modinfo[mod].memories: return False
 1102|         return True
 1103| 
 1104|     def mem_expr(self, mod, base, path, port=None, infomode=False):
 1105|         if len(path) == 1:
 1106|             assert mod in self.modinfo
 1107|             assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].memories
 1108|             if infomode:
 1109|                 return self.modinfo[mod].memories[path[0]]
 1110|             return "(|%s_m%s %s| %s)" % (mod, "" if port is None else ":%s" % port, path[0], base)
 1111| 
 1112|         assert mod in self.modinfo
 1113|         assert path[0] in self.modinfo[mod].cells
 1114| 
 1115|         nextmod = self.modinfo[mod].cells[path[0]]
 1116|         nextbase = "(|%s_h %s| %s)" % (mod, path[0], base)
 1117|         return self.mem_expr(nextmod, nextbase, path[1:], port=port, infomode=infomode)
 1118| 
 1119|     def mem_info(self, mod, path):
 1120|         return self.mem_expr(mod, "", path, infomode=True)
 1121| 
 1122|     def get_net(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1123|         return self.get(self.net_expr(mod_name, state_name, net_path))
 1124| 
 1125|     def get_net_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1126|         return self.get_list([self.net_expr(mod_name, state_name, n) for n in net_path_list])
 1127| 
 1128|     def get_net_hex(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1129|         return self.bv2hex(self.get_net(mod_name, net_path, state_name))
 1130| 
 1131|     def get_net_hex_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1132|         return [self.bv2hex(v) for v in self.get_net_list(mod_name, net_path_list, state_name)]
 1133| 
 1134|     def get_net_bin(self, mod_name, net_path, state_name):
 1135|         return self.bv2bin(self.get_net(mod_name, net_path, state_name))
 1136| 
 1137|     def get_net_bin_list(self, mod_name, net_path_list, state_name):
 1138|         return [self.bv2bin(v) for v in self.get_net_list(mod_name, net_path_list, state_name)]
 1139| 
 1140|     def wait(self):
 1141|         if self.p is not None:
 1142|             self.p.wait()
 1143|             self.p_close()
 1144| 
 1145| 
 1146| class SmtOpts:
 1147|     def __init__(self):
 1148|         self.shortopts = "s:S:v"
 1149|         self.longopts = ["unroll", "noincr", "noprogress", "timeout=", "dump-smt2=", "logic=", "dummy=", "info=", "nocomments"]
 1150|         self.solver = "yices"
 1151|         self.solver_opts = list()
 1152|         self.debug_print = False
 1153|         self.debug_file = None
 1154|         self.dummy_file = None
 1155|         self.unroll = False
 1156|         self.noincr = False
 1157|         self.timeinfo = os.name != "nt"
 1158|         self.timeout = 0
 1159|         self.logic = None
 1160|         self.info_stmts = list()
 1161|         self.nocomments = False
 1162| 
 1163|     def handle(self, o, a):
 1164|         if o == "-s":
 1165|             self.solver = a
 1166|         elif o == "-S":
 1167|             self.solver_opts.append(a)
 1168|         elif o == "--timeout":
 1169|             self.timeout = int(a)
 1170|         elif o == "-v":
 1171|             self.debug_print = True
 1172|         elif o == "--unroll":
 1173|             self.unroll = True
 1174|         elif o == "--noincr":
 1175|             self.noincr = True
 1176|         elif o == "--noprogress":
 1177|             self.timeinfo = False
 1178|         elif o == "--dump-smt2":
 1179|             self.debug_file = open(a, "w")
 1180|         elif o == "--logic":
 1181|             self.logic = a
 1182|         elif o == "--dummy":
 1183|             self.dummy_file = a
 1184|         elif o == "--info":
 1185|             self.info_stmts.append(a)
 1186|         elif o == "--nocomments":
 1187|             self.nocomments = True
 1188|         else:
 1189|             return False
 1190|         return True
 1191| 
 1192|     def helpmsg(self):
 1193|         return """
 1194|     -s <solver>
 1195|         set SMT solver: z3, yices, boolector, bitwuzla, cvc4, mathsat, dummy
 1196|         default: yices
 1197| 
 1198|     -S <opt>
 1199|         pass <opt> as command line argument to the solver
 1200| 
 1201|     --timeout <value>
 1202|         set the solver timeout to the specified value (in seconds).
 1203| 
 1204|     --logic <smt2_logic>
 1205|         use the specified SMT2 logic (e.g. QF_AUFBV)
 1206| 
 1207|     --dummy <filename>
 1208|         if solver is "dummy", read solver output from that file
 1209|         otherwise: write solver output to that file
 1210| 
 1211|     -v
 1212|         enable debug output
 1213| 
 1214|     --unroll
 1215|         unroll uninterpreted functions
 1216| 
 1217|     --noincr
 1218|         don't use incremental solving, instead restart solver for
 1219|         each (check-sat). This also avoids (push) and (pop).
 1220| 
 1221|     --noprogress
 1222|         disable timer display during solving
 1223|         (this option is set implicitly on Windows)
 1224| 
 1225|     --dump-smt2 <filename>
 1226|         write smt2 statements to file
 1227| 
 1228|     --info <smt2-info-stmt>
 1229|         include the specified smt2 info statement in the smt2 output
 1230| 
 1231|     --nocomments
 1232|         strip all comments from the generated smt2 code
 1233| """

 1235| 
 1236| class MkVcd:
 1237|     def __init__(self, f):
 1238|         self.f = f
 1239|         self.t = -1
 1240|         self.nets = dict()
 1241|         self.clocks = dict()
 1242| 
 1243|     def add_net(self, path, width):
 1244|         path = tuple(path)
 1245|         assert self.t == -1
 1246|         key = "n%d" % len(self.nets)
 1247|         self.nets[path] = (key, width)
 1248| 
 1249|     def add_clock(self, path, edge):
 1250|         path = tuple(path)
 1251|         assert self.t == -1
 1252|         key = "n%d" % len(self.nets)
 1253|         self.nets[path] = (key, 1)
 1254|         self.clocks[path] = (key, edge)
 1255| 
 1256|     def set_net(self, path, bits):
 1257|         path = tuple(path)
 1258|         assert self.t >= 0
 1259|         assert path in self.nets
 1260|         if path not in self.clocks:
 1261|             print("b%s %s" % (bits, self.nets[path][0]), file=self.f)
 1262| 
 1263|     def escape_name(self, name):
 1264|         name = re.sub(r"\[([0-9a-zA-Z_]*[a-zA-Z_][0-9a-zA-Z_]*)\]", r"<\1>", name)
 1265|         if re.match(r"[\[\]]", name) and name[0] != "\\":
 1266|             name = "\\" + name
 1267|         return name
 1268| 
 1269|     def set_time(self, t):
 1270|         assert t >= self.t
 1271|         if t != self.t:
 1272|             if self.t == -1:
 1273|                 print("$version Generated by Yosys-SMTBMC $end", file=self.f)
 1274|                 print("$timescale 1ns $end", file=self.f)
 1275|                 print("$var integer 32 t smt_step $end", file=self.f)
 1276|                 print("$var event 1 ! smt_clock $end", file=self.f)
 1277| 
 1278|                 def vcdescape(n):
 1279|                     if n.startswith("$") or ":" in n:
 1280|                         return "\\" + n
 1281|                     return n
 1282| 
 1283|                 scope = []
 1284|                 for path in sorted(self.nets):
 1285|                     key, width = self.nets[path]
 1286| 
 1287|                     uipath = list(path)
 1288|                     if "." in uipath[-1] and not uipath[-1].startswith("$"):
 1289|                         uipath = uipath[0:-1] + uipath[-1].split(".")
 1290|                     for i in range(len(uipath)):
 1291|                         uipath[i] = re.sub(r"\[([^\]]*)\]", r"<\1>", uipath[i])
 1292| 
 1293|                     while uipath[:len(scope)] != scope:
 1294|                         print("$upscope $end", file=self.f)
 1295|                         scope = scope[:-1]
 1296| 
 1297|                     while uipath[:-1] != scope:
 1298|                         scopename = uipath[len(scope)]
 1299|                         print("$scope module %s $end" % vcdescape(scopename), file=self.f)
 1300|                         scope.append(uipath[len(scope)])
 1301| 
 1302|                     if path in self.clocks and self.clocks[path][1] == "event":
 1303|                         print("$var event 1 %s %s $end" % (key, vcdescape(uipath[-1])), file=self.f)
 1304|                     else:
 1305|                         print("$var wire %d %s %s $end" % (width, key, vcdescape(uipath[-1])), file=self.f)
 1306| 
 1307|                 for i in range(len(scope)):
 1308|                     print("$upscope $end", file=self.f)
 1309| 
 1310|                 print("$enddefinitions $end", file=self.f)
 1311| 
 1312|             self.t = t
 1313|             assert self.t >= 0
 1314| 
 1315|             if self.t > 0:
 1316|                 print("#%d" % (10 * self.t - 5), file=self.f)
 1317|                 for path in sorted(self.clocks.keys()):
 1318|                     if self.clocks[path][1] == "posedge":
 1319|                         print("b0 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1320|                     elif self.clocks[path][1] == "negedge":
 1321|                         print("b1 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1322| 
 1323|             print("#%d" % (10 * self.t), file=self.f)
 1324|             print("1!", file=self.f)
 1325|             print("b%s t" % format(self.t, "032b"), file=self.f)
 1326| 
 1327|             for path in sorted(self.clocks.keys()):
 1328|                 if self.clocks[path][1] == "negedge":
 1329|                     print("b0 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)
 1330|                 else:
 1331|                     print("b1 %s" % self.nets[path][0], file=self.f)

==> tests/unit/CMakeLists.txt <==
    1| find_package(GTest REQUIRED)
    2| file(GLOB_RECURSE unit_sources CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "*Test.cc")
    3| foreach(source IN LISTS unit_sources)
    4|   get_filename_component(name "${source}" NAME_WE)
    5|   add_executable(${name} "${source}")
    6|   target_compile_definitions(${name} PRIVATE ${YOSYS_DEFINITIONS})
    7|   target_compile_options(${name} PRIVATE ${YOSYS_OPTIONS})
    8|   target_include_directories(${name} PRIVATE ${YOSYS_INCLUDES})
    9|   target_link_options(${name} PRIVATE ${YOSYS_LINK_OPTIONS})
   10|   target_link_libraries(${name} PRIVATE libyosys GTest::gtest GTest::gmock GTest::gtest_main ${YOSYS_LIBRARIES})
   11|   add_test(NAME ${name} COMMAND ${name})
   12| endforeach()
